-- ============================================================
--  VETERANO TEAM - TELA DE LOGIN / KEY SYSTEM
--  Valida a key na planilha (via Google Apps Script) antes de
--  liberar o hub. Se a key for invalida, o script nao continua.
-- ============================================================
if not (function()
    local CFG = {
        -- URL do Web App do Apps Script (termina em /exec)
        API_URL  = "https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbzqOAHVc8kuJRKlr6w5bp6-mknBzJ5heZlJAmKapLDVw9SwXXh0xxCrPh8DKERdDvWnhQ/exec",
        DISCORD  = "https://discord.com/invite/vNKTAtTwFu",
        KEY_FILE = "VeteranoTeam/key.txt",
        TITLE    = "VETERANO TEAM",
        MAX_TRIES = 5,     -- tentativas erradas antes do bloqueio
        LOCK_SECS = 15,    -- segundos de bloqueio
    }

    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local cr = cloneref or function(x) return x end
    local Players          = cr(game:GetService("Players"))
    local TweenService     = cr(game:GetService("TweenService"))
    local UserInputService = cr(game:GetService("UserInputService"))
    local HttpService      = cr(game:GetService("HttpService"))
    local CoreGui          = cr(game:GetService("CoreGui"))

    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    while not lp do
        task.wait()
        lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    end

    -- evita abrir duas telas ao executar duas vezes
    if env.__VT_LOGIN_GUI then
        pcall(function() env.__VT_LOGIN_GUI:Destroy() end)
        env.__VT_LOGIN_GUI = nil
    end

    -- ---------- utilitarios ----------
    local function getRequest()
        return (syn and syn.request) or request or http_request
            or (http and http.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    end

    local function httpGet(url)
        local req = getRequest()
        if req then
            local ok, res = pcall(req, { Url = url, Method = "GET" })
            if ok and type(res) == "table" then
                return res.Body or res.body
            end
        end
        local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url, true) end)
        if ok then return body end
        return nil
    end

    local function copyText(t)
        local f = setclipboard or toclipboard or (Clipboard and Clipboard.set)
        if f then
            local ok = pcall(f, t)
            return ok
        end
        return false
    end

    local function readSaved()
        if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
        local ok, v = pcall(function()
            if isfile(CFG.KEY_FILE) then return readfile(CFG.KEY_FILE) end
        end)
        if ok and type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then return v end
        return nil
    end

    local function saveKey(k)
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
        pcall(function()
            if type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" and not isfolder("VeteranoTeam") then
                makefolder("VeteranoTeam")
            end
            writefile(CFG.KEY_FILE, k)
        end)
    end

    local function clearSaved()
        if type(delfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then return end
        pcall(function()
            if isfile(CFG.KEY_FILE) then delfile(CFG.KEY_FILE) end
        end)
    end

    local function cleanKey(s)
        s = tostring(s or ""):gsub("%s+", ""):upper()
        return s
    end

    local function keyLooksValid(k)
        return k:match("^VTRN%-[A-Z0-9]+%-[A-Z0-9]+$") ~= nil
    end

    -- consulta a planilha (Apps Script). retorna ok:boolean, mensagem:string
    local function validateRemote(key)
        if CFG.API_URL:find("COLE_AQUI", 1, true) then
            return false, "Login nao configurado (API_URL)."
        end
        local url = CFG.API_URL
            .. "?action=validate"
            .. "&key=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(key)
            .. "&userid=" .. tostring(lp.UserId)
        local body = httpGet(url)
        if not body then
            return false, "Sem conexao com o servidor de keys."
        end
        local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
        if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then
            return false, "Resposta invalida do servidor."
        end
        return data.valid == true, tostring(data.message or (data.valid and "Key valida." or "Key invalida."))
    end

    -- ---------- paleta (mesmo tema roxo do hub) ----------
    local C = {
        bg      = Color3.fromRGB(16, 9, 28),
        panel   = Color3.fromRGB(26, 15, 44),
        input   = Color3.fromRGB(34, 20, 58),
        purple  = Color3.fromRGB(140, 40, 255),
        purple2 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 100, 255),
        text    = Color3.fromRGB(240, 232, 255),
        muted   = Color3.fromRGB(160, 140, 190),
        ok      = Color3.fromRGB(110, 255, 160),
        err     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 125),
        warn    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 100),
    }

    local function corner(o, r)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)
        c.Parent = o
        return c
    end
    local function stroke(o, col, th, tr)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s.Color = col
        s.Thickness = th or 1
        s.Transparency = tr or 0
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        s.Parent = o
        return s
    end
    local function label(parent, text, size, font, col, pos, sz)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = text
        l.TextSize = size
        l.Font = font
        l.TextColor3 = col
        l.Position = pos
        l.Size = sz
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        l.Parent = parent
        return l
    end
    local function tween(o, t, props)
        local tw = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
        tw:Play()
        return tw
    end

    -- ---------- interface ----------
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "VT_Login_" .. tostring(math.random(1000, 9999))
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 999
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    do
        local parented = false
        if gethui then
            parented = pcall(function() gui.Parent = gethui() end)
        end
        if not parented or not gui.Parent then
            parented = pcall(function() gui.Parent = CoreGui end)
        end
        if not parented or not gui.Parent then
            gui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        end
    end
    env.__VT_LOGIN_GUI = gui

    local main = Instance.new("Frame")
    main.Name = "Main"
    main.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 330)
    main.BackgroundColor3 = C.bg
    main.BorderSizePixel = 0
    main.Parent = gui
    corner(main, 16)
    local mainStroke = stroke(main, C.purple, 1.5, 0.2)
    local sg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    sg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.purple2),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, C.purple),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.purple2),
    })
    sg.Rotation = 45
    sg.Parent = mainStroke

    local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    scale.Scale = 0.9
    scale.Parent = main

    -- barra superior (arrastavel)
    local top = Instance.new("Frame")
    top.Name = "Top"
    top.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    top.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70)
    top.Parent = main

    label(top, CFG.TITLE, 22, Enum.Font.GothamBlack, C.purple2,
        UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26))
    label(top, "Key System  -  Acesso gratuito", 12, Enum.Font.Gotham, C.muted,
        UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 44), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16))

    local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeBtn.Text = "X"
    closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    closeBtn.TextSize = 14
    closeBtn.TextColor3 = C.muted
    closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    closeBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    closeBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    closeBtn.Parent = top

    -- passos
    local steps = Instance.new("Frame")
    steps.BackgroundColor3 = C.panel
    steps.BorderSizePixel = 0
    steps.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 76)
    steps.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 64)
    steps.Parent = main
    corner(steps, 10)
    local stepsTxt = label(steps,
        "1. Entre no nosso Discord\n2. Use o comando /gerar-key\n3. Cole a key abaixo e clique em Entrar",
        12, Enum.Font.Gotham, C.text,
        UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0))
    stepsTxt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    stepsTxt.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

    -- campo da key
    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Name = "KeyBox"
    box.BackgroundColor3 = C.input
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 152)
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 42)
    box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    box.TextSize = 14
    box.TextColor3 = C.text
    box.PlaceholderText = "VTRN-XXXXXXXX-XXXXXXXX"
    box.PlaceholderColor3 = C.muted
    box.Text = ""
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    box.Parent = main
    corner(box, 10)
    local boxStroke = stroke(box, C.purple, 1, 0.6)
    box.Focused:Connect(function() tween(boxStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0 }) end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function() tween(boxStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0.6 }) end)

    -- status
    local status = label(main, "", 12, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, C.muted,
        UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 200), UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 18))

    -- botao entrar
    local enter = Instance.new("TextButton")
    enter.Name = "Enter"
    enter.AutoButtonColor = false
    enter.BackgroundColor3 = C.purple
    enter.BorderSizePixel = 0
    enter.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 226)
    enter.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 42)
    enter.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    enter.TextSize = 15
    enter.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    enter.Text = "Entrar"
    enter.Parent = main
    corner(enter, 10)
    local eg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    eg.Color = ColorSequence.new(C.purple2, C.purple)
    eg.Rotation = 90
    eg.Parent = enter

    -- botao discord
    local disc = Instance.new("TextButton")
    disc.Name = "Discord"
    disc.AutoButtonColor = false
    disc.BackgroundColor3 = C.panel
    disc.BorderSizePixel = 0
    disc.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 278)
    disc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 38)
    disc.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    disc.TextSize = 13
    disc.TextColor3 = C.purple2
    disc.Text = "Pegar Key no Discord"
    disc.Parent = main
    corner(disc, 10)
    stroke(disc, C.purple, 1, 0.4)

    for _, b in ipairs({ enter, disc }) do
        local base = b.BackgroundColor3
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            tween(b, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = base:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.12) })
        end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            tween(b, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = base })
        end)
    end

    -- arrastar pela barra superior
    do
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos
        top.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = i.Position
                startPos = main.Position
                i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local d = i.Position - dragStart
                main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
    end

    tween(scale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 })

    -- ---------- logica ----------
    local result = nil          -- nil = aguardando | true = liberado | false = cancelado
    local busy = false
    local fails = 0
    local lockedUntil = 0

    local function setStatus(text, kind)
        status.Text = text
        status.TextColor3 = (kind == "ok" and C.ok) or (kind == "err" and C.err)
            or (kind == "warn" and C.warn) or C.muted
    end

    local function finish(ok)
        if result ~= nil then return end
        tween(scale, 0.25, { Scale = 0 })
        task.wait(0.28)
        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
        env.__VT_LOGIN_GUI = nil
        result = ok
    end

    -- limpa e forca maiusculas enquanto digita
    local fixing = false
    box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if fixing then return end
        local fixed = cleanKey(box.Text)
        if fixed ~= box.Text then
            fixing = true
            box.Text = fixed
            fixing = false
        end
    end)

    local function attempt(key, silent)
        if busy then return end
        if os.clock() < lockedUntil then
            setStatus("Muitas tentativas. Aguarde " .. math.ceil(lockedUntil - os.clock()) .. "s.", "warn")
            return
        end
        key = cleanKey(key)
        if key == "" then
            setStatus("Cole sua key primeiro.", "warn")
            return
        end
        if not keyLooksValid(key) then
            setStatus("Formato invalido. Ex: VTRN-XXXXXXXX-XXXXXXXX", "err")
            return
        end

        busy = true
        enter.Text = "Verificando..."
        setStatus(silent and "Verificando key salva..." or "Consultando planilha de keys...", "info")

        local ok, msg = validateRemote(key)

        if ok then
            saveKey(key)
            setStatus("Acesso liberado! Carregando o hub...", "ok")
            enter.Text = "Liberado"
            task.wait(0.7)
            busy = false
            finish(true)
            return
        end

        busy = false
        enter.Text = "Entrar"
        if silent then
            clearSaved()
            setStatus("Sua key salva expirou. Gere outra no Discord.", "warn")
            return
        end
        fails = fails + 1
        setStatus(msg, "err")
        -- tremidinha na janela para indicar erro
        task.spawn(function()
            local p0 = main.Position
            for _, dx in ipairs({ -8, 8, -5, 5, 0 }) do
                if not main.Parent then return end
                main.Position = UDim2.new(p0.X.Scale, p0.X.Offset + dx, p0.Y.Scale, p0.Y.Offset)
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)
        if fails >= CFG.MAX_TRIES then
            fails = 0
            lockedUntil = os.clock() + CFG.LOCK_SECS
            setStatus("Muitas tentativas. Aguarde " .. CFG.LOCK_SECS .. "s.", "warn")
        end
    end

    enter.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() attempt(box.Text, false) end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        if enterPressed then attempt(box.Text, false) end
    end)

    disc.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local copied = copyText(CFG.DISCORD)
        -- tenta abrir o convite direto no app do Discord (RPC local)
        local req = getRequest()
        local code = CFG.DISCORD:match("invite/([%w%-]+)") or CFG.DISCORD:match("gg/([%w%-]+)")
        if req and code then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(req, {
                    Url = "http://127.0.0.1:6463/rpc?v=1",
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = {
                        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                        ["Origin"] = "https://discord.com",
                    },
                    Body = HttpService:JSONEncode({
                        cmd = "INVITE_BROWSER",
                        args = { code = code },
                        nonce = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false),
                    }),
                })
            end)
        end
        if copied then
            setStatus("Link copiado! Cole no navegador e use /gerar-key", "ok")
        else
            setStatus("Entre em: " .. CFG.DISCORD, "info")
        end
    end)

    closeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(closeBtn, 0.12, { TextColor3 = C.err }) end)
    closeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(closeBtn, 0.12, { TextColor3 = C.muted }) end)
    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() finish(false) end)

    setStatus("Cole sua key para continuar.", "info")

    -- login automatico com a key salva
    local saved = readSaved()
    if saved then
        saved = cleanKey(saved)
        box.Text = saved
        task.spawn(function() attempt(saved, true) end)
    end

    while result == nil do
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return result
end)() then
    return
end

oihubEnv.__VETERANO_LICENSE = {
    user = "1503165597750530078",
    marker = "8f2f6a948d75eb4e3d148131",
    build = "6284ecc5d1105c10",
    expires = 1787538445,
}
(((getgenv and getgenv()) or _G)).__VETERANO_INTERNAL_TRACE =
{"t1_8f2f6a948d75eb4e3d148131", "t2_v2_2549b62660f417374e51", "t3_df350cc63a2ef0737fdd"}
do
    local C = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local Y = C.__VETERANO_UI_V2_LIBRARY
    if Y and type(Y.Unload) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            Y:Unload()
        end)
    end
    do
        local C = "LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE"
        local Y = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
        Y[C] = Y[C] or function(C)
            return C
        end
    end
    cloneref = cloneref or function(C)
        return C
    end
    gethui = gethui or get_hidden_gui
    getcustomasset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
    getgenv = getgenv or getfenv
    local g = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if(game:GetService("RunService")):IsStudio()then
            local C = (game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")):FindFirstChild("PRI_WORKSPACE")
            or Instance.new("Folder", game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"))
            C.Name = "PRI\000."
            .. (
            tostring(string.char(math.random(50, 120)))
            .. (
            tostring(string.char(math.random(50, 120)))
            .. (
            tostring(string.char(math.random(50, 120)))
            .. (
            tostring(string.char(math.random(50, 120)))
            .. (
            tostring(string.char(math.random(50, 120)))
            .. tostring(string.char(math.random(50, 120)))
        )
    )
)
)
)
local isYeValid = function(C)
    return(string.find(C, "/", 1, true) and string.split(C, "/"))
    or (string.find(C, "\\", 1, true) and string.split(C, "\\"))
    or {C}
end
local handleY = function(g)
    local m = isYeValid(g)
    local E = C
    for C, isYeValid in next, m do
        E = E[isYeValid]
    end
    return E
end
(getgenv()).readfile = function(C)
    local isYeValid = handleY(C)
    return isYeValid.Value
end
(getgenv()).isfile = function(C)
    local isYeValid, m = pcall(function()
        return handleY(C)
    end)
    if isYeValid and not m:IsA("Folder")then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
(getgenv()).isfolder = function(C)
    local isYeValid, m = pcall(function()
        return handleY(C)
    end)
    if isYeValid and m:IsA("Folder")then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
(getgenv()).writefile = function(handleY, m)
    local E = isYeValid(handleY)
    local M = C
    for C, isYeValid in next, E do
        local handleY = M:FindFirstChild(isYeValid)
        if not handleY then
            local C = Instance.new("StringValue", M)
            C.Name = tostring(isYeValid)
            C.Value = m
        else
            if handleY:IsA("StringValue") and tostring(handleY) == isYeValid then
                handleY.Name = tostring(isYeValid)
                handleY.Value = m
            end
            M = handleY
        end
    end
end
(getgenv()).listfiles = function(C)
    local isYeValid = handleY(C)
    local m = {}
    for isYeValid, handleY in next, isYeValid:GetChildren()do
        if handleY:IsA("StringValue")then
            table.insert(m, C .. ("/" .. tostring(handleY)))
        end
    end
    return m
end
(getgenv()).makefolder = function(handleY)
    local m = isYeValid(handleY)
    local E = C
    for C, isYeValid in next, m do
        local handleY = E:FindFirstChild(isYeValid)
        if not handleY then
            local C = Instance.new("Folder", E)
            C.Name = tostring(isYeValid)
        else
            E = handleY
        end
    end
end
(getgenv()).delfile = function(handleY)
    local m = isYeValid(handleY)
    local E = C
    for C, isYeValid in next, m do
        local handleY = E:FindFirstChild(isYeValid)
        if handleY and handleY:IsA("StringValue")then
            handleY:Destroy()
        else
            E = handleY
        end
    end
end
end
end)
g()
writefile = writefile or (getgenv()).writefile
makefolder = makefolder or (getgenv()).makefolder
readfile = readfile or (getgenv()).readfile
delfolder = delfolder or (getgenv()).delfolder
delfile = delfile or (getgenv()).delfile
listfiles = listfiles or (getgenv()).listfiles
isfolder = isfolder or (getgenv()).isfolder
isfile = isfile or (getgenv()).isfile
local m = {}
local function new(C)
    local Y, g = pcall(
    Font.new,
    "rbxasset://LuaPackages/Packages/_Index/BuilderIcons/BuilderIcons/BuilderIcons.json",
    C,
    Enum.FontStyle.Normal
)
if Y and g then
    return g
end
return Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", C, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
end
m.Name = "VeteranoTeamUIV2"
m.Version = "0.1.0-transition"
m.BuiltInRegular = new(Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
m.BuiltInBold = new(Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
m.GlobalSignals = {}
m.UnloadEnabled = true
m.Windows = {}
m.ConfigNamespace = "VeteranoTeam/configs/v2"
m.__Unloaded = false
m.ConfigSupported = type(writefile) == "function"
and (
type(readfile) == "function"
and (
type(listfiles) == "function"
and (type(makefolder) == "function" and (type(isfolder) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function"))
)
)
function m.EnsureFolder(Y, C)
    if not m.ConfigSupported then
        return false
    end
    local g = ""
    for C, Y in ipairs(string.split(tostring(C), "/"))do
        if Y ~= "" then
            g = g == "" and Y or (g .. ("/" .. Y))
            if not isfolder(g)then
                makefolder(g)
            end
        end
    end
    return true
end
local M = cloneref or function(C)
    return C
end
local H = M(game:GetService("TweenService"))
local A = M(game:GetService("UserInputService"))
local F = M(game:GetService("TextService"))
local u = M(game:GetService("RunService"))
local q = M(game:GetService("Players"))
local P = M(game:GetService("HttpService"))

local function isTValid()
    for C, Y in next, {gethui, get_hidden_gui} do
        if type(Y) == "function" then
            local C, g = pcall(Y)
            if C and g then
                return g
            end
        end
    end
    local C = game:FindFirstChild("CoreGui")
    return(C and M(C)) or z:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

function m.SetMemberStatusLabel(g, C, Y)
    local new = tostring(Y or "Never")
    C.RichText = false
    C.Text = new
    local M = C:FindFirstChild("VeteranoMemberNeon")
    local A = C:FindFirstChild("VeteranoMemberNeonGlow")
    if new ~= "VT MEMBRO" then
        if M then
            M:Destroy()
        end
        if A then
            A:Destroy()
        end
        return
    end
    if M and A then
        A.Text = new
        return
    end
    M = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    M.Name = "VeteranoMemberNeon"
    M.Color = ColorSequence.new( {
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(200, 100, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, Color3.fromRGB(140, 40, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.68, Color3.fromRGB(220, 130, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(200, 100, 255)),
    } )
    M.Offset = Vector2.new( - 1, 0)
    M.Parent = C
    A = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    A.Name = "VeteranoMemberNeonGlow"
    A.Parent = C
    A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    A.BorderSizePixel = 0
    A.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    A.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    A.ZIndex = math.max(1, C.ZIndex - 1)
    A.FontFace = C.FontFace
    A.Text = new
    A.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    A.TextSize = C.TextSize
    A.TextTransparency = 0.72
    A.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    A.TextXAlignment = C.TextXAlignment
    A.TextYAlignment = C.TextYAlignment
    A.TextTruncate = C.TextTruncate
    A.TextWrapped = C.TextWrapped
    local F = M:Clone()
    F.Name = "GlowGradient"
    F.Parent = A
    local u = TweenInfo.new(1.75, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, - 1, true)
    local q = H:Create(M, u, {Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0)} )
    local P = H:Create(F, u, {Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0)} )
    local z = H:Create(
    A,
    TweenInfo.new(1.05, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, - 1, true),
    {TextTransparency = 0.48}
)
q:Play()
P:Play()
z:Play()
m:AddSignal(C.Destroying:Connect(function()
    pcall(function()
        q:Cancel()
    end)
    pcall(function()
        P:Cancel()
    end)
    pcall(function()
        z:Cancel()
    end)
end))
end
function m.ApplyVersionNeon(Y, C)
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Name = "OiVersionNeon"
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new( {
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(120, 30, 200)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.24, Color3.fromRGB(180, 80, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, Color3.fromRGB(230, 170, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.78, Color3.fromRGB(160, 60, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(120, 30, 200)),
    } )
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new( {
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.22),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.62, 0.1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.22),
    } )
    g.Rotation = - 180
    g.Parent = C
    local new = H:Create(
    g,
    TweenInfo.new(6.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, - 1, false),
    {Rotation = 180}
)
new:Play()
m:AddSignal(C.Destroying:Connect(function()
    pcall(function()
        new:Cancel()
    end)
end))
end

if getcustomasset and (writefile and (makefolder and (isfolder and isfile)))then
    local C =
    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/4lpaca-pin/NeverLose/2308c101331855d7ca09fecc1a1db814d489aaf4/assets/%s"
    local Y = "VeteranoTeamAssets"
    if not isfolder(Y)then
        makefolder(Y)
    end
    pcall(function()
        if not isfile(Y .. "/logo.png")then
            local g = game:HttpGet(string.format(C, "logo.png"))
            writefile(Y .. "/logo.png", g)
            task.wait()
        end
        if isfile(Y .. "/logo.png")then
            m.GlobalLogo = getcustomasset(Y .. "/logo.png")
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not isfile(Y .. "/saturation_value_gradient.png")then
            local g = game:HttpGet(string.format(C, "saturation_value_gradient.png"))
            writefile(Y .. "/saturation_value_gradient.png", g)
            task.wait()
        end
        if isfile(Y .. "/saturation_value_gradient.png")then
            m.ImageColorMapping = getcustomasset(Y .. "/saturation_value_gradient.png")
        end
    end)
end
local function handleY(Y)
    local g = (syn and syn.request)
    or (http and http.request)
    or C.http_request
    or C.request
    or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    if type(g) == "function" then
        local C, m = pcall(g, {Url = Y, Method = "GET", Headers = {["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0"} } )
        if C and type(m) == "table" then
            local C = m.StatusCode or m.status_code or 200
            local Y = m.Body or m.body
            if type(Y) == "string" and (Y ~= "" and (C >= 200 and C < 400))then
                return Y
            end
        end
    end
    local m, new = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(Y)
    end)
    if m and (type(new) == "string" and new ~= "")then
        return new
    end
    return nil
end
function m.ResolveImage(m, C, Y, g)
    if type(C) ~= "string" or C == "" then
        return g or ""
    end
    if not string.match(C, "^https?://")then
        return C
    end
    if
    type(getcustomasset) ~= "function"
    or type(writefile) ~= "function"
    or type(isfile) ~= "function"
    or type(makefolder) ~= "function"
    or type(isfolder) ~= "function"
    then
        return g or ""
    end
    local new = "VeteranoTeamAssets"
    local M = new .. ("/" .. tostring(Y or "image.png"))
    local H, A = pcall(function()
        if not isfolder(new)then
            makefolder(new)
        end
        local Y = false
        if isfile(M) and type(readfile) == "function" then
            local C, g = pcall(readfile, M)
            Y = C and (type(g) == "string" and #g > 0)
        end
        if not Y then
            local Y = handleY(C)
            if not Y then
                return g or ""
            end
            writefile(M, Y)
        end
        return getcustomasset(M)
    end)
    return(H and A) or g or ""
end

    if m.UnloadEnabled then
        table.insert(m.GlobalSignals, C)
    end
    return C
end
function m.AddQuery(g, C, Y)
    table.insert(m.NameRegisitry, {Root = C, Idx = Y} )
end
function B.new(C)
    local Y = {}
    local g = ((#C + 3782) % 111) + 1
    string.gsub(
    C,
    ".",
    LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        table.insert(Y, tostring(C:byte() + g))
    end)
)
local m = table.concat(Y, "?")
table.clear(Y)
return "{" .. (tostring(g + 72667) .. ("}?" .. m))
end
function B.reverse(C)
    local Y = string.split(C, "?")
    local g = (Y[1]:gsub("{", "")):gsub("}", "")
    local m = tonumber(g)
    local new = {}
    local M = m - 72667
    for C, Y in next, Y do
        if C > 1 then
            local C = tonumber(Y)
            table.insert(new, string.char(C - M))
        end
    end
    local H = table.concat(new)
    table.clear(new)
    return H
end
do
    local C = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
    m.Base64Encode = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Y)
        return(
        ((Y:gsub(".", function(C)
            local Y, g = "", C:byte()
            for C = 8, 1, - 1 do
                Y = Y .. (g % 2 ^ C - g % 2 ^ (C - 1) > 0 and "1" or "0")
            end
            return Y
        end) .. "0000")):gsub("%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?", function(Y)
            if#Y < 6 then
                return ""
            end
            local g = 0
            for C = 1, 6, 1 do
                g = g + (Y:sub(C, C) == "1" and 2 ^ (6 - C) or 0)
            end
            return C:sub(g + 1, g + 1)
        end) .. ( {"", "==", "="} )[#Y % 3 + 1]
    )
end)
m.Base64Decode = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Y)
    Y = string.gsub(Y, "[^" .. (C .. "=]"), "")
    return(
    (Y:gsub(".", function(Y)
        if Y == "=" then
            return ""
        end
        local g, m = "", (C:find(Y) - 1)
        for C = 6, 1, - 1 do
            g = g .. (m % 2 ^ C - m % 2 ^ (C - 1) > 0 and "1" or "0")
        end
        return g
    end)):gsub("%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?", function(C)
        if#C ~= 8 then
            return ""
        end
        local Y = 0
        for g = 1, 8, 1 do
            Y = Y + (C:sub(g, g) == "1" and 2 ^ (8 - g) or 0)
        end
        return string.char(Y)
    end)
)
end)
end

m.IsMouseOverFrame = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    if not Y then
        return
    end
    if m.Global3DRenderMode then
        if Y.GuiState == Enum.GuiState.Hover or Y.GuiState == Enum.GuiState.Press then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    local g, new = Y.AbsolutePosition, Y.AbsoluteSize
    if f.X >= g.X and (f.X <= g.X + new.X and (f.Y >= g.Y and f.Y <= g.Y + new.Y))then
        return true
    end
end)
m.CreateSignal = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    local g = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
    local new = {Value = Y, __event = g}
    function new.GetValue(C)
        return new.Value
    end
    function new.SetValue(Y, C)
        new.Value = C
        return g:Fire(C)
    end
    function new.Connect(Y, C)
        local new = g.Event:Connect(C)
        m:AddSignal(new)
        return new
    end
    return new
end)
m.SetIconMode = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    local new = string.lower(string.sub(g, - 5)) == "-bold"
    if new then
        Y.Text = g:sub(1, - 6)
        Y.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
    else
        Y.Text = g
        Y.FontFace = m.BuiltInRegular
    end
end)
function m.GetIconFont(Y, C)
    local g = string.lower(string.sub(C, - 5)) == "-bold"
    if g then
        return m.BuiltInBold
    end
    return m.BuiltInRegular
end
function m.MoreThanHalfY(Y, C)
    return(m.ScreenGui.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2) < C
end
m.IsStudio = u:IsStudio()
m.IsMobile = A.TouchEnabled
m.CreateInput = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    local m = Instance.new("ImageButton", Y)
    m.ZIndex = Y.ZIndex + 10
    m.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    m.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    m.ImageTransparency = 1
    m.Image = ""
    m.AutoButtonColor = false
    m.Active = true
    m.Selectable = false
    if g then
        local C = m.MouseButton1Click:Connect(g)
        return m, C
    end
    return m
end)
m.PlayAnimate = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    local m = H:Create(C, Y or TweenInfo.new(0.25), g)
    m:Play()
    return m
end)
m.Drag = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    local new = false
    local M = nil
    local H = nil
    local F = TweenInfo.new(g)
    local callback = function(C)
        local g = C.Position - M
        local new = UDim2.new(H.X.Scale, H.X.Offset + g.X, H.Y.Scale, H.Y.Offset + g.Y)
        if m.Global3DRenderMode then
            m.PlayAnimate(Y, F, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(Y, F, {Position = new} )
        end
    end
    m:AddSignal(C.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
        if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            new = true
            M = C.Position
            H = Y.Position
            local g
            g = C.Changed:Connect(function()
                if C.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    new = false
                    g:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end))
    m:AddSignal(A.InputChanged:Connect(function(C)
        if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if new then
                callback(C)
            end
        end
    end))
end)
m.Rounding = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    local g = 10 ^ (Y or 0)
    return math.floor(C * g + 0.5) / g
end)
m.ProcessParams = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    Y = Y or {}
    local m = Y or {}
    for C, g in next, g do
        m[C] = Y[C] or g
    end
    table.clear(g)
    return m
end)
m.EnabledBlur = true
m.BlurModuleParent = workspace.CurrentCamera
m.GetCalculatePosition = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g, m)
    local new = Y
    local M = m
    local H = g - C
    local A = ((new.x * H.x) + (new.y * H.y)) + (new.z * H.z)
    local F = ((new.x * M.x) + (new.y * M.y)) + (new.z * M.z)
    local u = - A / F
    return g + (u * m)
end)
m.CreateBlurModule = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    if not m.EnabledBlur then
        return m:AddSignal((Instance.new("BindableEvent")).Event:Connect(function()
            return "nl"
        end))
    end
    local new = Instance.new("Part", m.BlurModuleParent)
    local H = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect", M(game:GetService("Lighting")))
    local F = Instance.new("BlockMesh")
    F.Parent = new
    new.Material = Enum.Material.Glass
    new.Transparency = 1
    new.Reflectance = 1
    new.CastShadow = false
    new.Anchored = true
    new.CanCollide = false
    new.CanQuery = false
    new.CollisionGroup = m.RandomString()
    new.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 0.01
    new.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    H.Enabled = true
    H.FarIntensity = 0
    H.FocusDistance = 0
    H.InFocusRadius = 1000
    H.NearIntensity = 1
    H.Name = m.RandomString()
    new.Name = m.RandomString()
    local u
    local onGetPropertyChangedSignal = function()
        local C = g:GetValue()
        if C and not m.Global3DRenderMode then
            m.PlayAnimate(H, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {NearIntensity = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(new, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 0.97, Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 0.01} )
            new.Parent = m.BlurModuleParent
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(H, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {NearIntensity = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(new, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Size = Vector3.zero, Transparency = 1.5} )
            new.Parent = nil
            return false
        end
        if C then
            local C = Y.AbsolutePosition
            local g = C + Y.AbsoluteSize
            local M = R.ScreenPointToRay(R, C.X, C.Y, 1)
            local H = R.ScreenPointToRay(R, g.X, g.Y, 1)
            local A = R.CFrame.Position + R.CFrame.LookVector * (0.05 - R.NearPlaneZ)
            local u = R.CFrame.LookVector
            local q = m.GetCalculatePosition(A, u, M.Origin, M.Direction)
            local P = m.GetCalculatePosition(A, u, H.Origin, H.Direction)
            q = R.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(q)
            P = R.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(P)
            local z = P - q
            local isTValid = (q + P) / 2
            F.Offset = isTValid
            F.Scale = z / 0.0101
            new.CFrame = R.CFrame
        end
    end
    local P = m:AddSignal((R:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame")):Connect(onGetPropertyChangedSignal))
    local z = m:AddSignal(A.InputChanged:Connect(function(C)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            pcall(onGetPropertyChangedSignal)
        end
    end))
    local isTValid = task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.1)
            pcall(onGetPropertyChangedSignal)
        end
    end)
    u = function()
        P:Disconnect()
        z:Disconnect()
        task.cancel(isTValid)
        new:Destroy()
        H:Destroy()
    end
    Y.Destroying:Connect(u)
    return P
end)
local handleG = function()end
function m.RollingEffect(Y, C)
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new( {NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.4), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)} )
    g.Parent = C
    return g
end
function m.CreateShadow(g, C, Y)
    local new = {}
    local M = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local H = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local A = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local F = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    M.Thickness = 6
    M.Transparency = 1
    M.Parent = C
    H.Thickness = 5
    H.Transparency = 1
    H.Parent = C
    A.Thickness = 4
    A.Transparency = 1
    A.Parent = C
    F.Thickness = 3
    F.Transparency = 1
    F.Parent = C
    local u
    local q, P, z, isTValid
    if Y then
        q = m:RollingEffect(M)
        P = m:RollingEffect(H)
        z = m:RollingEffect(A)
        isTValid = m:RollingEffect(F)
    end
    new.Render = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, g)
        if u then
            task.cancel(u)
            u = nil
        end
        if g then
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {Transparency = 0.9} )
            m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {Transparency = 0.9} )
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 0.9} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {Transparency = 0.9} )
            if Y then
                u = task.spawn(function()
                    local C = 20
                    while true do
                        task.wait(0.025)
                        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {Rotation = q.Rotation + C} )
                        m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {Rotation = P.Rotation + C} )
                        m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {Rotation = z.Rotation + C} )
                        m.PlayAnimate(isTValid, I, {Rotation = isTValid.Rotation + C} )
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    return new
end
function m.CreateOptionWindow(g, C, Y)
    Y = Y or 9
    local new = {Signal = m:CreateSignal(false)}
    local M = Instance.new("Frame")
    local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local A = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local F = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local u = m:CreateShadow(M)
    M.Name = m.RandomString()
    M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 14, 32)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.035
    M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.ClipsDescendants = true
    M.Position = UDim2.new(255, 255, 255, 255)
    M.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 75)
    M.ZIndex = Y + 9
    H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    H.Parent = M
    A.Parent = M
    A.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    A.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    F.Transparency = 0.65
    F.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    F.Parent = M
    m:AddSignal((A:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, A.AbsoluteContentSize.Y - 1)} )
    end)))
    m:AddSignal((M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if M.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            M.Visible = false
            M.Active = false
            A.Parent = nil
            M.Parent = nil
        else
            M.Visible = true
            M.Active = true
            A.Parent = M
            if m.Global3DRenderMode then
                M.Parent = m.GlobalSurfaceGui
            else
                M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
            end
        end
    end)))
    local q
    local P = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if m:MoreThanHalfY(C.AbsolutePosition.Y + 65)then
            M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        else
            M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        end
        M.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(C.AbsolutePosition.X + 18, C.AbsolutePosition.Y + 65)
    end)
    new.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if q then
            task.cancel(q)
            q = nil
        end
        if C then
            P()
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.035} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
            u:Render(true)
            if m.Global3DRenderMode then
                M.Parent = m.GlobalSurfaceGui
            else
                M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
            end
            q = task.spawn(function()
                while true do
                    task.wait()
                    P()
                end
            end)
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            u:Render(false)
        end
    end)
    new.SetRender(false)
    new.Signal:Connect(new.SetRender)
    local z = m:RegisiterItem(M, new.Signal)
    z.Winbdow = new
    z.Root = M
    z.Signal = new.Signal
    return z
end
function m.CreateColorPicker(Y, C)
    local g = C.ZIndex
    local new = {}
    local M = Instance.new("Frame")
    local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local F = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local u = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    local q = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local P = Instance.new("Frame")
    local z = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local isTValid = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local T = Instance.new("Frame")
    local R = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    local Q = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local V = Instance.new("Frame")
    local O = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local X = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local B = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local s = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local handleY = m:CreateShadow(M)
    M.Name = m.RandomString()
    M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 14, 32)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.035
    M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.ClipsDescendants = true
    M.Position = UDim2.new(255, 0, 255, 20)
    M.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 240)
    M.ZIndex = g + 125
    m:AddSignal((M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if M.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            M.Visible = false
            M.Active = false
            M.Parent = nil
        else
            M.Visible = true
            M.Active = true
            if m.Global3DRenderMode then
                M.Parent = m.GlobalSurfaceGui
            else
                M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
            end
        end
    end)))
    H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    H.Parent = M
    F.Transparency = 0.65
    F.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    F.Parent = M
    u.Name = m.RandomString()
    u.Parent = M
    u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 4)
    u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    u.BorderSizePixel = 0
    u.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 5)
    u.Size = UDim2.new(0, 185, 0, 185)
    u.ZIndex = g + 126
    u.Image = m.ImageColorMapping
    q.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    q.Parent = u
    P.Name = m.RandomString()
    P.Parent = u
    P.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    P.BorderSizePixel = 0
    P.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    P.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
    P.ZIndex = g + 127
    z.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    z.Parent = P
    isTValid.Color = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    isTValid.Parent = P
    T.Name = m.RandomString()
    T.Parent = M
    T.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    T.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    T.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    T.BorderSizePixel = 0
    T.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 200)
    T.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 15, 0, 10)
    T.ZIndex = g + 126
    R.Color = ColorSequence.new( {
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 153, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, Color3.fromRGB(203, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 102)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 101, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, Color3.fromRGB(50, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, Color3.fromRGB(204, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.9, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 153)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    } )
    R.Parent = T
    Q.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
    Q.Parent = T
    V.Name = m.RandomString()
    V.Parent = T
    V.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    V.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    V.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    V.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    V.BorderSizePixel = 0
    V.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
    V.Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, 0)
    V.ZIndex = g + 126
    O.Thickness = 2
    O.Color = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    O.Parent = V
    X.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
    X.Parent = V
    B.Name = m.RandomString()
    B.Parent = M
    B.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 18, 40)
    B.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75
    B.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    B.BorderSizePixel = 0
    B.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 217)
    B.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 15)
    B.ZIndex = g + 127
    B.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    B.Text = "#FFFFFF"
    B.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    B.TextSize = 12
    B.TextTransparency = 0.4
    B.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    s.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    s.Parent = B
    new.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Y)
        if Y then
            M.Position = UDim2.new(0, C.AbsolutePosition.X + 20, 0, C.AbsolutePosition.Y + 75)
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.035} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, ImageTransparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(isTValid, I, {Transparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(T, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(O, I, {Transparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(B, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.75, TextTransparency = 0.4} )
            handleY:Render(true)
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, ImageTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(isTValid, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(T, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(O, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(B, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextTransparency = 1} )
            handleY:Render(false)
        end
    end)
    new.SetRender(false)
    new.Root = M
    new.H = 1
    new.S = 1
    new.V = 1
    new.Callback = handleG
    function new.Update(C)
        local Y = Color3.fromHSV(new.H, new.S, new.V)
        m.PlayAnimate(P, p, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(new.S, 1 - new.V)} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, p, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(new.H, 1, 1)} )
        m.PlayAnimate(V, p, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(new.H, 0.5)} )
        B.Text = "#" .. Y:ToHex()
        new.Callback(Y)
    end
    function new.SetValue(Y, C)
        if typeof(C) == "string" then
            C = Color3.fromHex(C)
        end
        local g, m, M = C:ToHSV()
        new.H = g
        new.S = m
        new.V = M
        new:Update()
    end
    new.IsHold = false
    m:AddSignal(M.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
        if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            new.IsHold = true
        end
    end))
    m:AddSignal(M.InputEnded:Connect(function(C)
        if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            new.IsHold = false
        end
    end))
    m:AddSignal(T.InputBegan:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            new.IsHold = true
            while A:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) or new.IsHold do
                task.wait()
                local C = T.AbsolutePosition.X
                local Y = C + T.AbsoluteSize.X
                local g = math.clamp(f.X, C, Y)
                local m = ((g - C) / (Y - C))
                new.H = m
                new:Update()
            end
        end
    end)))
    m:AddSignal(u.InputBegan:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            new.IsHold = true
            while A:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) or new.IsHold do
                task.wait()
                local C = u.AbsolutePosition.X
                local Y = C + u.AbsoluteSize.X
                local g, m = math.clamp(f.X, C, Y), u.AbsolutePosition.Y
                local M = m + u.AbsoluteSize.Y
                local H = math.clamp(f.Y, m, M)
                new.S = (g - C) / (Y - C)
                new.V = (1 - ((H - m) / (M - m)))
                new:Update()
            end
        end
    end)))
    return new
end
m.KeyEnum = {
    One = "1",
    Two = "2",
    Three = "3",
    Four = "4",
    Five = "5",
    Six = "6",
    Seven = "7",
    Eight = "8",
    Nine = "9",
    Zero = "0",
    Minus = "-",
    Plus = "+",
    BackSlash = "\\",
    Slash = "/",
    Period = ".",
    Semicolon = ";",
    Colon = ":",
    LeftControl = "LCtrl",
    RightControl = "RCtrl",
    LeftShift = "LShift",
    RightShift = "RShift",
    Return = "Enter",
    LeftBracket = "[",
    RightBracket = "]",
    Quote = "'",
    Comma = ",",
    Equals = "=",
    LeftSuper = "Super",
    RightSuper = "Super",
    LeftAlt = "LAlt",
    RightAlt = "RAlt",
    Escape = "Esc",
}
m.EnumReverse = {}
for C, Y in next, m.KeyEnum do
    m.EnumReverse[Y] = C
end
function m.KeyCodeToStr(Y, C)
    if typeof(C) == "string" then
        if m.KeyEnum[C]then
            return m.KeyEnum[C]
        end
        return C
    end
    return(m.KeyEnum[C.Name] or C.Name)
end
function m.StrToKeyCode(Y, C)
    if m.EnumReverse[C]then
        return Enum.KeyCode[m.EnumReverse[C]]
    end
    return Enum.KeyCode[C]
end
function m.RegisiterHandler(g, C, Y)
    local new = {}
    local M = C.ZIndex
    function new.AddToggle(new, g)
        g = m:ProcessParams(g, {Default = false, Flag = nil, Callback = handleG} )
        local H = Instance.new("Frame")
        local A = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local F = Instance.new("Frame")
        local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
        H.Name = m.RandomString()
        H.Parent = C
        H.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 24)
        H.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        H.BorderSizePixel = 0
        H.ClipsDescendants = true
        H.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 18)
        H.ZIndex = M + 13
        H.LayoutOrder = - (#C:GetChildren() + 5)
        A.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        A.Parent = H
        F.Name = m.RandomString()
        F.Parent = H
        F.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        F.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        F.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
        F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        F.BorderSizePixel = 0
        F.Position = UDim2.new(0.300000012, 0, 0.5, 0)
        F.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        F.ZIndex = M + 14
        u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        u.Parent = F
        local q = {Root = H}
        q.SetUI = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
            if C then
                m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = m.AccentColor} )
                m.PlayAnimate(
                F,
                I,
                {
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0,
                    Position = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0.5, 0),
                }
            )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 24)} )
            m.PlayAnimate(
            F,
            I,
            {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
                Position = UDim2.new(0.300000012, 0, 0.5, 0),
            }
        )
    end
end)
q.SetVisible = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        q.SetUI(g.Default)
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 24)} )
        m.PlayAnimate(
        F,
        I,
        {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.300000012, 0, 0.5, 0),
        }
    )
end
end)
q.SetUI(g.Default)
q.SetVisible(Y:GetValue())
m:CreateInput(
H,
LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    g.Default = not g.Default
    q.SetUI(g.Default)
    g.Callback(g.Default)
end)
)
q.Signal = Y:Connect(q.SetVisible)
function q.GetValue(C)
    return g.Default
end
function q.SetValue(m, C)
    g.Default = C
    if Y:GetValue()then
        q.SetUI(g.Default)
    end
    g.Callback(g.Default)
end
if g.Flag then
    m.Flags[g.Flag] = q
end
return q
end
function new.AddSlider(new, g)
    g = m:ProcessParams(
    g,
    {Default = 50, Min = 0, Max = 10, Type = "", Rounding = 0, Nums = {} , Flag = nil, Size = 125, Callback = handleG}
)
local u = {}
u.GetSize = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    return(g.Default - g.Min) / (g.Max - g.Min)
end)
local q = F:GetTextSize(
string.rep("0", (g.Rounding + #tostring(g.Max)) + 1) .. tostring(g.Type),
10,
Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge)
)
u.MaximumSize = q.X
if g.Nums then
    local C = 0
    for Y, g in next, g.Nums do
        local m = F:GetTextSize(
        string.rep("m", string.len(tostring(g))),
        10,
        Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
        Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge)
    )
    if C < m.X then
        C = m.X
    end
end
if u.MaximumSize < C then
    u.MaximumSize = C
end
end
