handleY.Parent = s
local U = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if g.Nums[g.Default]then
        R.Text = g.Nums[g.Default]
    else
        R.Text = tostring(g.Default) .. tostring(g.Type)
    end
end)
R.FocusLost:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    local C = m:ParseInput(R.Text, true)
    if C then
        local Y = math.clamp(C, g.Min, g.Max)
        local new = m.Rounding(Y, g.Rounding)
        if new then
            g.Default = new;
            (H:Create(X, p, {Size = UDim2.new(u.GetSize(), 0, 1, 0)} )):Play()
            U()
            g.Callback(g.Default)
        else
            U()
        end
    else
        U()
    end
end))
u.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        m.PlayAnimate(
        isTValid,
        I,
        {BackgroundTransparency = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, u.MaximumSize + e, 0, 18)}
    )
    m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
    m.PlayAnimate(R, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
    m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
    m.PlayAnimate(X, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, Size = UDim2.new(u.GetSize(), 0, 1, 0)} )
    m.PlayAnimate(s, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
else
    m.PlayAnimate(isTValid, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {Transparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(R, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(X, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)} )
    m.PlayAnimate(s, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
end
end)
u.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
u.Signal = Y:Connect(u.SetRender)
local updateDefault = function(C)
    local Y = math.clamp(((C.Position.X - Q.AbsolutePosition.X) / Q.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
    local new = ((g.Max - g.Min) * Y) + g.Min
    local M = m.Rounding(new, g.Rounding)
    local A = UDim2.fromScale(Y, 1)
    local F = ((M - g.Min) / (g.Max - g.Min)) + 0.02
    g.Default = M;
    (H:Create(X, p, {Size = UDim2.new(u.GetSize(), 0, 1, 0)} )):Play()
    U()
    g.Callback(M)
end
local k = false
do
    Q.InputBegan:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            k = true
            updateDefault(C)
        end
    end))
    Q.InputEnded:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            if A.TouchEnabled then
                if not m:IsMouseOverFrame(Q)then
                    k = false
                end
            else
                k = false
            end
        end
    end))
    A.InputChanged:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if k then
            if
            C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                if A.TouchEnabled then
                    if not m:IsMouseOverFrame(Q)then
                        k = false
                    else
                        updateDefault(C)
                    end
                else
                    updateDefault(C)
                end
            end
        end
    end))
end
function u.GetValue(C)
    return g.Default
end
function u.SetValue(new, C)
    g.Default = C
    if Y:GetValue()then
        m.PlayAnimate(X, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, Size = UDim2.new(u.GetSize(), 0, 1, 0)} )
    end
    U()
    g.Callback(g.Default)
end
if g.Flag then
    m.Flags[g.Flag] = u
end
return u
end
function new.AddOption(new, g)
    local H = Instance.new("Frame")
    local F = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
    H.Name = m.RandomString()
    H.Parent = C
    H.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 28, 58)
    H.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    H.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    H.BorderSizePixel = 0
    H.ClipsDescendants = true
    H.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 18)
    H.ZIndex = M + 13
    H.LayoutOrder = - (#C:GetChildren() + 5)
    F.Name = m.RandomString()
    F.Parent = H
    F.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    F.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    F.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    F.BorderSizePixel = 0
    F.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    F.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    F.ZIndex = M + 14
    F.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
    F.Text = (g == 1 and "gear") or (g == 2 and "chevron-large-right") or "three-dots-horizontal"
    F.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
    F.TextSize = 16
    F.TextTransparency = 0.4
    F.TextWrapped = true
    u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    u.Parent = H
    local q = m:CreateOptionWindow(H, M + 13)
    local P
    q.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 0.4} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    q.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
    Y:Connect(q.SetRender)
    local z = m:CreateInput(
    H,
    LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if P then
            P:Disconnect()
            P = nil
        end
        q.Signal:SetValue(true)
        P = A.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
            if
            C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                if not m:IsMouseOverFrame(q.Root) and not m:IsMouseOverFrame(H)then
                    if P then
                        P:Disconnect()
                        P = nil
                    end
                    q.Signal:SetValue(false)
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
)
m:AddSignal(z.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5} )
    m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
end)))
m:AddSignal(z.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 0.4} )
end)))
return q
end
function new.AddColorPicker(new, g)
    g = m:ProcessParams(g, {Default = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255), Callback = handleG} )
    if typeof(g.Default) == "string" then
        g.Default = Color3.fromHex(g.Default:gsub("#", ""))
    end
    local H = {}
    local F = Instance.new("Frame")
    local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local q = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local P = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    local z = Instance.new("UICorner")
    F.Name = m.RandomString()
    F.Parent = C
    F.BackgroundColor3 = g.Default
    F.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    F.BorderSizePixel = 0
    F.ClipsDescendants = true
    F.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
    F.ZIndex = M + 13
    F.LayoutOrder = - (#C:GetChildren() + 5)
    u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    u.Parent = F
    q.Transparency = 0.65
    q.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    q.Parent = F
    P.Parent = F
    P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    P.BorderSizePixel = 0
    P.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    P.ZIndex = M + 11
    P.Image = "rbxasset://textures/meshPartFallback.png"
    P.ImageTransparency = 0.9
    P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    P.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    z.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    z.Parent = P
    local isTValid = m:CreateColorPicker(F)
    isTValid:SetValue(g.Default)
    isTValid.Callback = function(C)
        F.BackgroundColor3 = C
        g.Default = C
        g.Callback(g.Default)
    end
    local T
    m:CreateInput(
    F,
    LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if T then
            T:Disconnect()
            T = nil
        end
        isTValid.SetRender(true)
        T = A.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
            if
            C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                if not m:IsMouseOverFrame(F) and not m:IsMouseOverFrame(isTValid.Root)then
                    if T then
                        T:Disconnect()
                        T = nil
                    end
                    isTValid.SetRender(false)
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
)
H.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {ImageTransparency = 0.9} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {ImageTransparency = 1} )
    end
end)
H.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
Y:Connect(H.SetRender)
function H.GetValue(C)
    return g.Default
end
function H.SetValue(Y, C)
    g.Default = C
    isTValid:SetValue(g.Default)
end
if g.Flag then
    m.Flags[g.Flag] = H
end
return H
end
function new.AddKeybind(new, g)
    g = m:ProcessParams(g, {Default = nil, Blacklist = {} , Callback = handleG, Flag = nil} )
    local H = {}
    local u = Instance.new("Frame")
    local q = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local P = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local z = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    u.Name = m.RandomString()
    u.Parent = C
    u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 18, 40)
    u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    u.BorderSizePixel = 0
    u.ClipsDescendants = true
    u.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 18)
    u.ZIndex = M + 13
    u.LayoutOrder = - (#C:GetChildren() + 5)
    q.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    q.Parent = u
    P.Transparency = 0.65
    P.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    P.Parent = u
    z.Name = m.RandomString()
    z.Parent = u
    z.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    z.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    z.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    z.BorderSizePixel = 0
    z.ClipsDescendants = true
    z.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    z.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    z.ZIndex = M + 14
    z.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    z.Text = m:KeyCodeToStr(g.Default or "None")
    z.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    z.TextSize = 10
    z.TextTransparency = 0.5
    H.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
            m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    function H.Update(C)
        local Y = F:GetTextSize(z.Text, z.TextSize, z.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, Y.X + 7, 0, 18)} )
    end
    local isTValid = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        return g.Blacklist and (g.Blacklist[C] or table.find(g.Blacklist, C))
    end)
    H:Update()
    H.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
    Y:Connect(H.SetRender)
    local T = false
    m:CreateInput(u, function()
        if T then
            return
        end
        T = true
        z.Text = "..."
        H:Update()
        local C = nil
        while not C do
            local Y = A.InputBegan:Wait()
            if
            Y.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and (not isTValid(Y.KeyCode) and not isTValid(Y.KeyCode.Name))
            then
                C = Y.KeyCode
            else
                if
                Y.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                and (not isTValid(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and not isTValid("M1B"))
                then
                    C = "M1B"
                elseif
                Y.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
                and (not isTValid(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) and not isTValid("M2B"))
                then
                    C = "M2B"
                end
            end
        end
        T = false
        local Y = typeof(C) == "string" and C or C.Name
        g.Default = Y
        z.Text = m:KeyCodeToStr(Y)
        H:Update()
        g.Callback(Y)
    end)
    function H.GetValue(C)
        return g.Default
    end
    function H.SetValue(Y, C)
        g.Default = C
        z.Text = m:KeyCodeToStr(C)
        H:Update()
        g.Callback(g.Default)
    end
    if g.Flag then
        m.Flags[g.Flag] = H
    end
    return H
end
function new.AddTextInput(new, g)
    g = m:ProcessParams(
    g,
    {Default = "", Placeholder = "Placeholder", Callback = print, Flag = nil, Size = 100, Numeric = false}
)
local H = {}
local A = Instance.new("Frame")
local F = Instance.new("UICorner")
local u = Instance.new("UIStroke")
local q = Instance.new("TextBox")
A.Name = m.RandomString()
A.Parent = C
A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 18, 40)
A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
A.BorderSizePixel = 0
A.ClipsDescendants = true
A.Size = UDim2.new(0, g.Size, 0, 18)
A.ZIndex = M + 13
A.LayoutOrder = - (#C:GetChildren() + 5)
F.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
F.Parent = A
u.Transparency = 0.65
u.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
u.Parent = A
q.Parent = A
q.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
q.BorderSizePixel = 0
q.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, 0)
q.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 5, 0, 17)
q.ZIndex = M + 14
q.ClearTextOnFocus = false
q.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
q.PlaceholderText = g.Placeholder
q.Text = tostring(g.Default)
q.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
q.TextSize = 11
q.TextTransparency = 0.35
q.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
H.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    end
end)
m:AddSignal((q:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    local C = m:ParseInput(q.Text, g.Numeric)
    if g.Numeric then
        q.Text = string.gsub(q.Text, "[^0-9.]", "")
    end
    if C then
        g.Default = C
        g.Callback(C)
    end
end)))
H.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
Y:Connect(H.SetRender)
function H.GetValue(C)
    return g.Default
end
function H.SetValue(Y, C)
    g.Default = C
    q.Text = tostring(C)
    g.Callback(g.Default)
end
if g.Flag then
    m.Flags[g.Flag] = H
end
return H
end
function new.AddDropdown(new, g)
    g = m:ProcessParams(
    g,
    {Default = nil, Values = {} , Multi = false, Callback = handleG, AutoUpdate = false, Flag = nil, Size = 100}
)
g.Default = m.ProcessDropdown(g.Default)
local H = Instance.new("Frame")
local u = Instance.new("TextLabel")
local q = Instance.new("UICorner")
local P = Instance.new("UIStroke")
local z = Instance.new("TextLabel")
H.Name = m.RandomString()
H.Parent = C
H.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 18, 40)
H.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
H.BorderSizePixel = 0
H.ClipsDescendants = true
H.Size = UDim2.new(0, g.Size, 0, 18)
H.ZIndex = M + 13
H.LayoutOrder = - (#C:GetChildren() + 5)
u.Name = m.RandomString()
u.Parent = H
u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
u.BorderSizePixel = 0
u.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 2, 0.5, 0)
u.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
u.ZIndex = M + 14
u.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
u.Text = "chevron-small-down"
u.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
u.TextSize = 16
u.TextTransparency = 0.25
u.TextWrapped = true
q.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
q.Parent = H
P.Transparency = 0.65
P.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
P.Parent = H
z.Name = m.RandomString()
z.Parent = H
z.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
z.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
z.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
z.BorderSizePixel = 0
z.ClipsDescendants = true
z.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, 0)
z.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 25, 0, 15)
z.ZIndex = M + 14
z.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
z.Text = m.ParseDropdown(g.Default)
z.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
z.TextSize = 12
z.TextTransparency = 0.5
z.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
do
    local C = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    C.Transparency = NumberSequence.new( {
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.85, 0.23),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    } )
    C.Parent = z
end
m:AddSignal(H.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
end)))
m:AddSignal(H.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
end)))
local isTValid = {OpenSignal = m:CreateSignal(false), Signals = {} , Refuse = {} }
isTValid.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
        m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    end
end)
isTValid.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
Y:Connect(isTValid.SetRender)
isTValid.ExtentSize = 0
do
    local C = Instance.new("Frame")
    local Y = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local new = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local A = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    local F = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local u = m:CreateShadow(C)
    C.Name = m.RandomString()
    C.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    C.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    C.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 14, 32)
    C.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
    C.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    C.BorderSizePixel = 0
    C.ClipsDescendants = true
    C.Position = UDim2.new(255, 255, 255, 255)
    C.Size = UDim2.new(0, 125, 0, 50)
    C.ZIndex = M + 125
    isTValid.BlockRoot = C
    m:AddSignal((C:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(function()
        if C.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            C.Visible = false
            C.Parent = nil
        else
            C.Visible = true
            if m.Global3DRenderMode then
                C.Parent = m.GlobalSurfaceGui
            else
                C.Parent = m.ScreenGui
            end
        end
    end))
    Y.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    Y.Parent = C
    new.Transparency = 0.65
    new.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    new.Parent = C
    A.Name = m.RandomString()
    A.Parent = C
    A.Active = true
    A.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    A.BorderSizePixel = 0
    A.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    A.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 5, 1, - 5)
    A.ZIndex = M + 127
    A.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    isTValid.RootItem = A
    F.Parent = A
    F.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    F.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    m:AddSignal((F:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        A.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, F.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
        m.PlayAnimate(
        C,
        I,
        {
            Size = UDim2.new(
            0,
            (H.AbsoluteSize.X + 5) + isTValid.ExtentSize,
            0,
            math.min(F.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 5, 250)
        ),
    }
)
end)))
local q = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if m:MoreThanHalfY(H.AbsolutePosition.Y + 85)then
        C.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    else
        C.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    end
    C.Position =
    UDim2.fromOffset(H.AbsolutePosition.X + (C.AbsoluteSize.X / 2), H.AbsolutePosition.Y + 85)
end)
isTValid.SetFrameRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Y)
    isTValid.OpenSignal:SetValue(Y)
    if Y then
        u:Render(true)
        C.Size = UDim2.new(
        0,
        (H.AbsoluteSize.X + 5) + isTValid.ExtentSize,
        0,
        math.min(F.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 5, 250)
    )
    q()
    m.PlayAnimate(C, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.035} )
    if g.AutoUpdate then
        isTValid:Generate()
    end
else
    m.PlayAnimate(C, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    u:Render(false)
end
end)
isTValid.SetFrameRender(false)
end
local T
m:CreateInput(
H,
LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if T then
        T:Disconnect()
        T = nil
    end
    isTValid.SetFrameRender(true)
    m.IsMosueOverOtherFrame = true
    T = A.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            if not m:IsMouseOverFrame(isTValid.BlockRoot) and not m:IsMouseOverFrame(H)then
                if T then
                    T:Disconnect()
                    T = nil
                end
                m.IsMosueOverOtherFrame = false
                isTValid.SetFrameRender(false)
            end
        end
    end)
end)
)
isTValid.IsMatch = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if typeof(g.Default) == "table" then
        if g.Default[C] or table.find(g.Default, C)then
            return true
        end
    end
    if g.Default == C then
        return true
    end
end)
function isTValid.Generate(C)
    for C, Y in next, isTValid.RootItem:GetChildren()do
        if Y:IsA("Frame")then
            Y:Destroy()
        end
    end
    for C, Y in next, isTValid.Signals do
        Y:Disconnect()
    end
    table.clear(isTValid.Signals)
    table.clear(isTValid.Refuse)
    local Y
    for C, new in next, g.Values do
        local H = Instance.new("Frame")
        local A = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
        H.Name = m.RandomString()
        H.Parent = isTValid.RootItem
        H.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(29, 31, 38)
        H.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        H.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        H.BorderSizePixel = 0
        H.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25)
        H.ZIndex = M + 1258
        A.Name = m.RandomString()
        A.Parent = H
        A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        A.BorderSizePixel = 0
        A.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 4)
        A.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 15)
        A.ZIndex = M + 1258
        A.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        A.Text = tostring(new)
        A.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        A.TextSize = 13
        A.TextTransparency = 0.2
        A.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        u.Parent = H
        local q = F:GetTextSize(A.Text, A.TextSize, A.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
        isTValid.ExtentSize = math.max(isTValid.ExtentSize, q.X)
        local P, T = nil, nil
        if g.Multi then
            local C = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            C.Parent = H
            C.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            C.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
            C.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            C.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            C.BorderSizePixel = 0
            C.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, 0)
            C.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
            C.ZIndex = M + 1259
            C.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
            C.Text = "check"
            C.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
            C.TextSize = 18
            C.TextTransparency = 1
            C.TextWrapped = true
            local g = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
                if isTValid.IsMatch(new)then
                    m.PlayAnimate(A, X, {TextTransparency = 0.2, Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 4)} )
                    m.PlayAnimate(C, vs, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
                    Y = A
                else
                    m.PlayAnimate(C, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
                    m.PlayAnimate(A, X, {TextTransparency = 0.5, Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 4)} )
                end
            end)
            P = C
            T = g
        else
            local C = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
                if isTValid.IsMatch(new)then
                    m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
                    Y = A
                else
                    m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
                end
            end)
            T = C
        end
        T()
        table.insert(isTValid.Refuse, T)
        table.insert(
        isTValid.Signals,
        H.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.1} )
        end))
    )
    table.insert(
    isTValid.Signals,
    H.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    end))
)
table.insert(
isTValid.Signals,
isTValid.OpenSignal:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        T()
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        if P then
            m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        end
    end
end))
)
if g.Multi then
    local C, Y = m:CreateInput(
    H,
    LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        g.Default[new] = not g.Default[new]
        T()
        z.Text = m.ParseDropdown(g.Default)
        g.Callback(g.Default)
    end)
)
table.insert(isTValid.Signals, Y)
else
    local C, Y = m:CreateInput(
    H,
    LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        g.Default = new
        for C, Y in next, isTValid.Refuse do
            task.spawn(Y)
        end
        z.Text = m.ParseDropdown(g.Default)
        g.Callback(g.Default)
    end)
)
table.insert(isTValid.Signals, Y)
end
end
end
isTValid:Generate()
function isTValid.GetValue(C)
    return g.Default
end
function isTValid.SetValue(Y, C)
    g.Default = C
    z.Text = m.ParseDropdown(g.Default)
    for C, Y in next, isTValid.Refuse do
        task.spawn(Y)
    end
    g.Callback(g.Default)
end
function isTValid.SetValues(Y, C)
    g.Values = C
    if not g.AutoUpdate then
        isTValid:Generate()
    end
end
if g.Flag then
    m.Flags[g.Flag] = isTValid
end
return isTValid
end
return new
end
m.ProcessDropdown = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if typeof(C) == "table" then
        local Y = {}
        for C, g in next, C do
            if typeof(g) == "boolean" and typeof(C) ~= "number" then
                Y[C] = g
            else
                Y[g] = true
            end
        end
        return Y
    else
        return C
    end
end)
m.ParseDropdown = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if not C then
        return "Select"
    end
    local Y
    if typeof(C) == "table" then
        if#C > 0 then
            local g = {}
            for C, Y in next, C do
                table.insert(g, tostring(Y))
            end
            Y = table.concat(g, " , ")
            table.clear(g)
        else
            local g = {}
            for C, Y in next, C do
                if Y == true then
                    table.insert(g, tostring(C))
                end
            end
            Y = table.concat(g, " , ")
            table.clear(g)
            if not Y:byte()then
                Y = "Select"
            end
        end
    else
        Y = tostring(C or "Select")
    end
    return Y
end)
function m.ParseInput(g, C, Y)
    if not C then
        return(Y and nil) or ""
    end
    if Y then
        local Y = string.gsub(tostring(C), "[^0-9.%-]", "")
        if tonumber(Y)then
            return tonumber(Y)
        end
        return nil
    end
    return C
end
function m.CreateToolTips(new, C, Y, g)
    local M = Instance.new("Frame")
    local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local A = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local u = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local q = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local P = m:CreateShadow(M)
    M.Name = m.RandomString()
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 14, 32)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.075
    M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.ClipsDescendants = true
    M.Position = UDim2.new(255, 255, 255, 255)
    M.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    M.ZIndex = 130
    H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    H.Parent = M
    A.Transparency = 0.65
    A.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    A.Parent = M
    u.Name = m.RandomString()
    u.Parent = M
    u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    u.BorderSizePixel = 0
    u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 5)
    u.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 20)
    u.ZIndex = 132
    u.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    u.Text = Y
    u.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    u.TextSize = 15
    u.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    q.Name = m.RandomString()
    q.Parent = M
    q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    q.BorderSizePixel = 0
    q.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 30)
    q.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 15)
    q.ZIndex = 132
    q.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    q.Text = g
    q.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    q.TextSize = 12
    q.TextTransparency = 0.65
    q.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    q.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    local z = {}
    z.Update = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local C = F:GetTextSize(u.Text, u.TextSize, u.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
        local Y = F:GetTextSize(q.Text, q.TextSize, q.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
        local g = math.max(C.X, Y.X) + 65
        local new = (C.Y + Y.Y) + 30
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, g, 0, new)} )
    end)
    m:AddSignal((M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if M.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            M.Visible = false
            M.Active = false
            M.Parent = nil
        else
            M.Visible = true
            M.Active = true
            if m.Global3DRenderMode then
                M.Parent = m.GlobalSurfaceGui
            else
                M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
            end
        end
    end)))
    z.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Y)
        if Y then
            M.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
            C.AbsolutePosition.X + C.AbsoluteSize.X,
            C.AbsolutePosition.Y + (C.AbsoluteSize.Y + 25)
        )
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.075} )
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 0.65} )
        z.Update()
        P:Render(true)
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        P:Render(false)
    end
end)
z.SetRender(false)
z.Update()
local isTValid
m:AddSignal(C.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if isTValid then
        task.cancel(isTValid)
        isTValid = nil
    end
    isTValid = task.delay(1, z.SetRender, true)
end)))
m:AddSignal(C.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if isTValid then
        task.cancel(isTValid)
        isTValid = nil
    end
    z.SetRender(false)
    z.Update()
end)))
return z
end
function m.RegisiterItem(g, C, Y)
    local new = {}
    local M = C.ZIndex
    function new.AddLabel(H, g, new)
        local A = Instance.new("Frame")
        local u = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local q = Instance.new("Frame")
        local P = Instance.new("Frame")
        local z = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        local isTValid = Instance.new("UICorner")
        A.Name = m.RandomString()
        A.Parent = C
        A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 22, 48)
        A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        A.BorderSizePixel = 0
        A.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
        A.ZIndex = M + 8
        m:AddQuery(A, g)
        u.Name = m.RandomString()
        u.Parent = A
        u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        u.BorderSizePixel = 0
        u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 6)
        u.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 15)
        u.ZIndex = M + 9
        u.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        u.Text = g
        u.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        u.TextSize = 13
        u.TextTransparency = 0.35
        u.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        u.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        q.Name = m.RandomString()
        q.Parent = A
        q.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
        q.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
        q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        q.BorderSizePixel = 0
        q.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
        q.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 1)
        q.ZIndex = M + 11
        P.Name = m.RandomString()
        P.Parent = A
        P.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        P.BorderSizePixel = 0
        P.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 11, 0, 2)
        P.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 25)
        P.ZIndex = M + 12
        z.Parent = P
        z.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        z.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
        z.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        z.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        z.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
        isTValid.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        isTValid.Parent = A
        local T
        local f = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if new or not A.Parent then
                return
            end
            local C = A.AbsoluteSize.X
            if C <= 1 then
                return
            end
            local Y = (F:GetTextSize(u.Text, u.TextSize, u.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, 15))).X
            local g = z.AbsoluteContentSize.X
            local m = g > 0 and ((Y + g) + 27 > C)
            if T ~= m then
                T = m
                if m then
                    A.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
                    u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 5)
                    P.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 11, 0, 24)
                    P.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 22, 0, 25)
                else
                    A.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
                    u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 6)
                    P.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 11, 0, 2)
                    P.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 25)
                end
            end
            if m then
                u.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 22, 0, 15)
            else
                u.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, (C - g) - 27), 15)
            end
        end)
        m:AddSignal((A:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize")):Connect(f))
        m:AddSignal((z:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(f))
        task.defer(f)
        local R = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local C = F:GetTextSize(u.Text, u.TextSize, u.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, C.Y + 13)} )
            u.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 35, 1, 0)
            u.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        end)
        if new then
            R()
        end
        local Q = m:RegisiterHandler(P, Y)
        Q.Root = A
        Q.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
            if C then
                m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
                m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
            else
                m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            end
        end)
        function Q.SetVisible(Y, C)
            A.Visible = C
        end
        m:AddSignal(A.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.35} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
        end)))
        m:AddSignal(A.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
        end)))
        function Q.SetText(Y, C)
            local g = u.Text
            u.Text = C
            if new and g ~= C then
                R()
            end
        end
        function Q.ToolTip(Y, C)
            Q.ToolTip = m:CreateToolTips(A, g, C)
            return Q
        end
        Q.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
        Y:Connect(Q.SetRender)
        return Q
    end
    function new.AddButton(new, g)
        g = m:ProcessParams(g, {Icon = "chevron-large-left", Name = "Button", Callback = handleG, ToolTip = nil} )
        local H = {}
        local A = Instance.new("Frame")
        local F = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local u = Instance.new("Frame")
        local q = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local P = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        m:AddQuery(A, g.Name)
        A.Name = m.RandomString()
        A.Parent = C
        A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 22, 48)
        A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        A.BorderSizePixel = 0
        A.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
        A.ZIndex = M + 8
        F.Name = m.RandomString()
        F.Parent = A
        F.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        F.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        F.BorderSizePixel = 0
        F.Position = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 6)
        F.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 15)
        F.ZIndex = M + 9
        F.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        F.Text = g.Name
        F.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        F.TextSize = 13
        F.TextTransparency = 0.2
        F.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        u.Name = m.RandomString()
        u.Parent = A
        u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
        u.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
        u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        u.BorderSizePixel = 0
        u.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
        u.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 1)
        u.ZIndex = M + 11
        q.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        q.Parent = A
        P.Name = m.RandomString()
        P.Parent = A
        P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        P.BorderSizePixel = 0
        P.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 5)
        P.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
        P.ZIndex = M + 9
        P.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
        P.Text = g.Icon
        P.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
        P.TextSize = 16
        P.TextTransparency = 0.25
        P.TextWrapped = true
        function H.SetText(Y, C)
            F.Text = C
        end
        function H.SetIcon(Y, C)
            P.Text = C
        end
        local z = m:CreateInput(
        A,
        LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            g.Callback()
        end)
    )
    m:AddSignal(z.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.35} )
    end)))
    m:AddSignal(z.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    end)))
    H.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
            m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    if g.ToolTip then
        H.ToolTip = m:CreateToolTips(A, g.Name, g.ToolTip)
    end
    H.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
    Y:Connect(H.SetRender)
    H.Root = A
    H.Label = F
    H.Icon = P
    return H
end
function new.AddUserFrame(A, g, new, H)
    local F = Instance.new("Frame")
    local u = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local q = Instance.new("Frame")
    local P = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local z = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    local isTValid = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local T = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    F.Name = m.RandomString()
    F.Parent = C
    F.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 22, 48)
    F.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    F.BorderSizePixel = 0
    F.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60)
    F.ZIndex = M + 8
    u.Name = m.RandomString()
    u.Parent = F
    u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    u.BorderSizePixel = 0
    u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 65, 0, 10)
    u.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 35, 0, 15)
    u.ZIndex = M + 9
    u.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    u.Text = g or "User"
    u.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    u.TextSize = 13
    u.TextTransparency = 0.2
    u.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    q.Name = m.RandomString()
    q.Parent = F
    q.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    q.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
    q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    q.BorderSizePixel = 0
    q.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
    q.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 1)
    q.ZIndex = M + 11
    P.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    P.Parent = F
    z.Name = m.RandomString()
    z.Parent = F
    z.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    z.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    z.BorderSizePixel = 0
    z.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 5)
    z.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
    z.ZIndex = M + 9
    z.Image = new or "rbxasset://textures/ui/clb_robux_20@3x.png"
    isTValid.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    isTValid.Parent = z
    T.Name = m.RandomString()
    T.Parent = F
    T.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    T.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    T.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    T.BorderSizePixel = 0
    T.Position = UDim2.new(0, 65, 0, 25)
    T.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 35, 0, 15)
    T.ZIndex = M + 9
    T.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    T.Text = tostring(H or "Never")
    T.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    T.TextSize = 13
    T.TextTransparency = 0.2
    T.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    m:SetMemberStatusLabel(T, H)
    local f = {}
    f.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
            m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
            m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {ImageTransparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(T, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {ImageTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(T, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    f.SetRender(Y:GetValue())
    Y:Connect(f.SetRender)
    function f.SetUsername(Y, C)
        u.Text = C or "User"
    end
    function f.SetProfile(Y, C)
        z.Image = C or "rbxasset://textures/ui/clb_robux_20@3x.png"
    end
    function f.SetExpires(Y, C)
        m:SetMemberStatusLabel(T, C)
    end
    return f
end
return new
end
function m.CreateWindow(Y, C)
    C = m:ProcessParams(
    C,
    {
        Logo = m.VeteranoIcon,
        TitleImage = m.VeteranoWordmark,
        Name = "VETERANO TEAM",
        Content = "Ui Library V2",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 640, 0, 480),
        ConfigFolder = m.ConfigNamespace,
        Enable3DRenderer = false,
        Keybind = "Insert",
        Responsive = true,
    }
)
local g = {
    Logo = m:ResolveImage(C.Logo, "window-icon.png", m.VeteranoIcon),
    TitleImage = m:ResolveImage(C.TitleImage, "window-wordmark.png", m.VeteranoWordmark),
    Name = C.Name,
    Content = C.Content,
    Size = C.Size,
    BaseSize = C.Size,
    ConfigFolder = C.ConfigFolder,
    Signal = m:CreateSignal(true),
    Tabs = {} ,
    CurrentTab = 1,
    Keybind = C.Keybind,
    Responsive = C.Responsive ~= false,
    Enable3DRenderer = C.Enable3DRenderer,
}
m.GlobalLogo = g.Logo
local new = m:CreateLogger()
if m.ConfigSupported and not isfolder(g.ConfigFolder)then
    m:EnsureFolder(g.ConfigFolder)
end
local M = Instance.new("Frame")

M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.055
M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
M.BorderSizePixel = 0
M.ClipsDescendants = true
M.Position = UDim2.new(255, 0, 255, 0)
M.Size = g.Size
M.Active = true
if not m.EnabledBlur then
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.0255
end
local G = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if g.__3DRender then
        if M.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            M.Visible = false
            M.Active = false
            M.Parent = nil
        else
            M.Visible = true
            M.Active = true
            m.PlayAnimate(M, X, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)} )
            M.Parent = g.SurfaceGui
        end
    else
        if M.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            M.Visible = false
            M.Active = false
            M.Parent = nil
        else
            M.Visible = true
            M.Active = true
            M.Parent = m.ScreenGui
        end
    end
end)
m:AddSignal((M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(G))
g.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    if Y then
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = (m.EnabledBlur and 0.055) or 0.0255, Size = g.Size} )
        m.PlayAnimate(isTValid, I, {ImageTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {TextTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(Q, I, {ImageTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {TextTransparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(p, I, {TextTransparency = 0.05} )
        m.PlayAnimate(O, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(e, I, {ImageTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(L, I, {TextTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(k, I, {TextTransparency = 0.12} )
        m.PlayAnimate(n, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(b, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
        m.PlayAnimate(y, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.6} )
        m.PlayAnimate(x, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(Z, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(D, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.75} )
        m.PlayAnimate(r, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(j, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
        m.PlayAnimate(W, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(v, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
        m.PlayAnimate(J, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
        m.PlayAnimate(a, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
        m.PlayAnimate(i, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
        g.Shadow:Render(true)
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = g.Size + UDim2.fromOffset( - 15, - 15)} )
        m.PlayAnimate(isTValid, I, {ImageTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(Q, I, {ImageTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(p, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(O, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(e, I, {ImageTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(L, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(k, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(n, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(b, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(y, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(x, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(Z, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(D, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(r, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(j, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(W, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(v, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(J, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(a, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(i, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        g.Shadow:Render(false)
    end
end)

            m.PlayAnimate(w, X, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 30)} )

            m.PlayAnimate(i, X, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(w, X, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)} )

            m.PlayAnimate(i, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            i.Text = ""
        end
    end)

local Y = tick()
local new
local M = 0.2
m:AddSignal((i:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if not i.Text:byte()then
        for C, Y in next, m.NameRegisitry do
            Y.Root.Visible = true
        end
        return
    end
    Y = tick()
    if new then
        task.cancel(new)
        new = nil
    end
    new = task.delay(M, function()
        if i.Text:byte() and (tick() - Y) > M then
            for C, Y in next, m.NameRegisitry do
                if string.find(string.lower(Y.Idx), string.lower(i.Text), 1, true)then
                    Y.Root.Visible = true
                else
                    Y.Root.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)))
m:AddSignal(C.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(a, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
end)))
m:AddSignal(C.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if g.Searching then
        m.PlayAnimate(a, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(a, I, {TextTransparency = 0.45} )
    end
end)))
end
if g.Enable3DRenderer then
    local C = Instance.new("Part")
    C.Name = m.RandomString()
    C.Anchored = true
    C.Transparency = 1
    C.CanCollide = false
    C.CanTouch = false
    C.AudioCanCollide = false
    C.CollisionGroup = m.RandomString()
    C.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
    C.Size = Vector3.zero
    local Y = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
    Y.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    Y.Adornee = C
    Y.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    Y.AlwaysOnTop = true
    Y.LightInfluence = 1
    Y.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    Y.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize
    Y.PixelsPerStud = 40
    g.SurfaceGui = Y
    m.GlobalSurfaceGui = Y
    local new = Vector2.new(1920, 1380)
    g.Load3DBlock = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not g.Signal:GetValue()then
            local Y, g = R:WorldToViewportPoint(C.Position)
            if g then
                m.PlayAnimate(
                C,
                X,
                {CFrame = (R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, - 15)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)}
            )
        end
        return
    end
    local Y = 50
    local M = Vector2.new(new.X + 5, new.Y * 1.35) / (Y / 2)
    local H = M.Magnitude
    local A = H / 1.35
    C.Parent = m.BlurModuleParent or workspace
    m.PlayAnimate(
    C,
    X,
    {CFrame = (R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, - 25)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)}
)
C.Size = Vector3.new(new.X / A, new.Y / A, 0)
end)
function g.Set3DRender(new, Y)
    g.__3DRender = Y
    m.Global3DRenderMode = Y
    if Y then
        g.Load3DBlock()
    else
        C.Parent = nil
    end
    G()
end
end
function g.AddTabLabel(Y, C)
    local new = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local M = Instance.new("Frame")
    new.Name = m.RandomString()
    new.Parent = s
    new.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    new.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    new.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    new.BorderSizePixel = 0
    new.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 7, 0, 15)
    new.ZIndex = 8
    new.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    new.Text = C
    new.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    new.TextSize = 11
    new.TextTransparency = 0.5
    new.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    local H = (F:GetTextSize(C, new.TextSize, new.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, 15))).X
    M.Name = "TabDividerLine"
    M.Parent = new
    M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    M.Position = UDim2.new(0, H + 8, 0.5, 0)
    M.Size = UDim2.new(1, - (H + 18), 0, 1)
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.ZIndex = 8
    local A = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(new, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(new, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    A(g.Signal:GetValue())
    return g.Signal:Connect(A)
end
function g.AddTab(Y, C)
    C = m:ProcessParams(C, {Icon = "crosshairs", Name = "Tab", Type = "Double"} )
    local new = {Signal = m:CreateSignal(false), Sections = {} }
    local M = Instance.new("Frame")
    local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local A = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local F = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    new.Idx = M
    M.Name = m.RandomString()
    M.Parent = s
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(41, 45, 49)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
    M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 1, 0, 30)
    M.ZIndex = 8
    H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    H.Parent = M
    A.Name = m.RandomString()
    A.Parent = M
    A.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    A.BorderSizePixel = 0
    A.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
    A.Size = UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 25)
    A.ZIndex = 9
    A.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
    A.Text = C.Icon
    A.TextColor3 = m.AccentColor
    A.TextSize = 16
    A.TextWrapped = true
    F.Name = m.RandomString()
    F.Parent = M
    F.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    F.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    F.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    F.BorderSizePixel = 0
    F.Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0.5, 0)
    F.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 7, 0, 15)
    F.ZIndex = 9
    F.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    F.Text = C.Name
    F.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    F.TextSize = 12
    F.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    local u = Instance.new("Frame")
    local q = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    local P = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local z = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    local isTValid = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    u.Name = m.RandomString()
    u.Parent = d
    u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    u.BorderSizePixel = 0
    u.ClipsDescendants = true
    u.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    u.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    u.Visible = true
    q.Name = m.RandomString()
    q.Parent = u
    q.Active = true
    q.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    q.BorderSizePixel = 0
    q.ClipsDescendants = false
    q.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.5, 0)
    q.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, - 5)
    q.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    P.Parent = q
    P.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
    P.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    P.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    m:AddSignal((P:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        q.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, P.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 1)
    end)))
    z.Name = m.RandomString()
    z.Parent = u
    z.Active = true
    z.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    z.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    z.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    z.BorderSizePixel = 0
    z.ClipsDescendants = false
    z.Position = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 0.5, 0)
    z.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, - 5)
    z.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    isTValid.Parent = z
    isTValid.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    isTValid.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    if C.Type == "Single" then
        isTValid:Destroy()
        z:Destroy()
        z = q
        isTValid = P
        q.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, - 5)
        q.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    else
        m:AddSignal(
        (isTValid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            z.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, isTValid.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 1)
        end))
    )
end
local function onGetPropertyChangedSignal()
    if C.Type == "Single" or not u.Parent then
        return
    end
    local Y = u.AbsoluteSize.X < 500
    if new.Compact == Y then
        return
    end
    new.Compact = Y
    if Y then
        q.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        q.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, - 5)
        z.Visible = false
        for C, Y in ipairs(new.Sections)do
            Y.Frame.Parent = q
        end
    else
        q.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.5, 0)
        q.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, - 5)
        z.Position = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 0.5, 0)
        z.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, - 5)
        z.Visible = true
        for C, Y in ipairs(new.Sections)do
            Y.Frame.Parent = Y.Column == "left" and q or z
        end
    end
end
new.UpdateResponsiveLayout = onGetPropertyChangedSignal
m:AddSignal((u:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize")):Connect(onGetPropertyChangedSignal))
task.defer(onGetPropertyChangedSignal)
m:AddSignal((A:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if A.TextTransparency > 0.4 then
        P.Parent = nil
        isTValid.Parent = nil
        u.Visible = false
        u.Parent = nil
    else
        P.Parent = q
        isTValid.Parent = z
        u.Visible = true
        u.Parent = d
    end
end)))
new.SetValue = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    new.Signal:SetValue(C)
    if C then
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5} )
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {TextTransparency = 0, TextColor3 = m.AccentColor} )
        m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 0} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(252, 252, 252)} )
        m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
    end
end)
table.insert(g.Tabs, new)
if g.Tabs[g.CurrentTab] == new then
    new.SetValue(true)
else
    new.SetValue(false)
end
local f = m:CreateInput(
M,
LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    for C, Y in next, g.Tabs do
        if Y.Idx == M then
            Y.SetValue(true)
            g.CurrentTab = C
        else
            Y.SetValue(false)
        end
    end
end)
)
m:AddSignal(f.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if g.Tabs[g.CurrentTab] == new then
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.8} )
    end
end)))
m:AddSignal(f.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if g.Tabs[g.CurrentTab] == new then
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5} )
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    end
end)))
g.Signal:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C then
        if g.Tabs[g.CurrentTab] == new then
            new.SetValue(true)
        else
            new.SetValue(false)
        end
    else
        new.SetValue(false)
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    end
end))
function new.AddSection(Y, C)
    C = m:ProcessParams(C, {Name = "SECTION", Position = "left"} )
    local g = Instance.new("Frame")
    local M = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local H = Instance.new("Frame")
    local A = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local F = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local u = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local P = string.lower(C.Position) == "left" and "left" or "right"
    g.Name = m.RandomString()
    g.Parent = (new.Compact or P == "left") and q or z
    g.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    g.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    g.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    g.BorderSizePixel = 0
    g.ClipsDescendants = true
    g.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 5, 0, 0)
    g.ZIndex = 9
    g.LayoutOrder = #new.Sections + 1
    table.insert(new.Sections, {Frame = g, Column = P} )
    M.Name = m.RandomString()
    M.Parent = g
    M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
    M.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 35, 0, 15)
    M.ZIndex = 9
    M.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    M.Text = C.Name
    M.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    M.TextSize = 11
    M.TextTransparency = 0.5
    M.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    H.Name = m.RandomString()
    H.Parent = g
    H.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    H.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 14, 32)
    H.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
    H.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    H.BorderSizePixel = 0
    H.ClipsDescendants = true
    H.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 20)
    H.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 10, 1, - 21)
    H.ZIndex = 9
    A.Transparency = 0.65
    A.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    A.Parent = H
    F.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    F.Parent = H
    u.Parent = H
    u.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    u.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder;
    (u:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if u.AbsoluteContentSize.Y <= 1 then
            m.PlayAnimate(g, X, {Size = UDim2.new(1, - 5, 0, 0)} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(g, X, {Size = UDim2.new(1, - 5, 0, u.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 19.5)} )
        end
    end))
    local isTValid = m:RegisiterItem(H, new.Signal)
    isTValid.Root = H
    isTValid.Frame = g
    isTValid.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {TextTransparency = 0.5} )
            m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5} )
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        end
    end)
    isTValid.SetRender(new.Signal:GetValue())
    new.Signal:Connect(isTValid.SetRender)
    return isTValid
end
return new
end
function g._InitConfig(C)
    local Y = m:CreateSignal(false)
    local M = {Signals = {} }
    local H = Instance.new("Frame")
    local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local q = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local z = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local isTValid = Instance.new("Frame")
    local T = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    local R = Instance.new("Frame")
    local Q = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local V = Instance.new("Frame")
    local p = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local O = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local X = Instance.new("TextBox")
    local s = Instance.new("Frame")
    local handleY = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local handleG = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local e = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local U = m:CreateShadow(H)
    M.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        if C then
            H.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(D.AbsolutePosition.X + 110, D.AbsolutePosition.Y + 96)
            m.PlayAnimate(
            H,
            I,
            {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.035,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(D.AbsolutePosition.X + 110, D.AbsolutePosition.Y + 95),
            }
        )
        m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(T, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
        m.PlayAnimate(O, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65} )
        m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(X, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
        m.PlayAnimate(handleY, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
        m.PlayAnimate(J, I, {Rotation = 180} )
        U:Render(true)
    else
        m.PlayAnimate(J, I, {Rotation = 0} )
        m.PlayAnimate(
        H,
        I,
        {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(D.AbsolutePosition.X + 110, D.AbsolutePosition.Y + 96),
        }
    )
    m.PlayAnimate(O, I, {Transparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {Transparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(T, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(X, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    m.PlayAnimate(handleY, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
    U:Render(false)
end
end)
m:AddSignal((H:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if H.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
        H.Visible = false
        q.Parent = nil
        H.Parent = nil
    else
        H.Visible = true
        q.Parent = H
        if m.Global3DRenderMode then
            H.Parent = m.GlobalSurfaceGui
        else
            H.Parent = m.ScreenGui
        end
    end
end)))

function M.GetData(Y, C)
    local g = {}
    local new = 0
    for Y, m in next, m.Flags do
        if m and m.GetValue then
            local C = m:GetValue()
            if typeof(C) == "Color3" then
                table.insert(g, {Idx = Y, Value = C:ToHex()} )
            else
                table.insert(g, {Idx = Y, Value = C} )
            end
        end
        if C then
            if new % 35 == 1 then
                task.wait()
            end
        end
        new = new + 1
    end
    return m.Base64Encode(B.new(P:JSONEncode(g)))
end
function M.LoadData(Y, C)
    local g = P:JSONDecode(B.reverse(m.Base64Decode(C)))
    for C, Y in next, g do
        if Y.Idx then
            if m.Flags[Y.Idx]then
                task.spawn(function()
                    m.Flags[Y.Idx]:SetValue(Y.Value)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end
function M.RefreshConfig(C)
    if not isfolder(g.ConfigFolder)then
        m:EnsureFolder(g.ConfigFolder)
    end
    if not isfile(g.ConfigFolder .. "/Default")then
        writefile(g.ConfigFolder .. "/Default", M:GetData())
    end
    for C, Y in next, H:GetChildren()do
        if Y:GetAttribute("ConfigItem")then
            Y:Destroy()
        end
    end
    for C, Y in next, M.Signals do
        Y:Disconnect()
    end
    table.clear(M.Signals)
    local A = {}
    for C, Y in next, listfiles(g.ConfigFolder)do
        local m = string.sub(Y, #g.ConfigFolder + 2)
        table.insert(A, m)
    end
    for C, A in next, A do
        local F = Instance.new("Frame")
        local u = Instance.new("Frame")
        local q = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        local P = Instance.new("Frame")
        local z = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local isTValid = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local T = Instance.new("Frame")
        local f = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local R = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local Q = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local V = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local p = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        F.Name = m.RandomString()
        F.Parent = H
        F.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(21, 20, 27)
        F.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        F.BorderSizePixel = 0
        F.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 10, 0, 30)
        F.ZIndex = 153
        F:SetAttribute("ConfigItem", true)
        u.Name = m.RandomString()
        u.Parent = F
        u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        u.BorderSizePixel = 0
        u.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 11, 0, 2)
        u.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 0, 25)
        u.ZIndex = 153
        q.Parent = u
        q.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        q.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
        q.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        q.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        q.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
        P.Name = m.RandomString()
        P.Parent = u
        P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 28, 58)
        P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        P.BorderSizePixel = 0
        P.ClipsDescendants = true
        P.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 18)
        P.ZIndex = 153
        z.Name = m.RandomString()
        z.Parent = P
        z.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        z.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        z.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        z.BorderSizePixel = 0
        z.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        z.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        z.ZIndex = 153
        z.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
        z.Text = "trash-can"
        z.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
        z.TextSize = 16
        z.TextTransparency = 0.4
        z.TextWrapped = true
        isTValid.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        isTValid.Parent = P
        T.Name = m.RandomString()
        T.Parent = u
        T.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 28, 58)
        T.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        T.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        T.BorderSizePixel = 0
        T.ClipsDescendants = true
        T.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 18)
        T.ZIndex = 153
        f.Name = m.RandomString()
        f.Parent = T

        f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        f.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0

        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        f.ZIndex = 153
        f.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
        f.Text = "arrow-right-from-portrait-rectangle"
        f.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
        f.TextSize = 16
        f.TextTransparency = 0.4
        f.TextWrapped = true
        R.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        R.Parent = T
        Q.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        Q.Parent = F
        V.Name = m.RandomString()
        V.Parent = F
        V.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        V.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        V.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        V.BorderSizePixel = 0
        V.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 7)
        V.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 15)
        V.ZIndex = 153
        V.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        V.Text = A
        V.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        V.TextSize = 13
        V.TextTransparency = 0.2
        V.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        p.Transparency = 0.5
        p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
        p.Parent = F
        local O = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
            if C then
                m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
                m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 0.4} )
                m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {TextTransparency = 0.4} )
                m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
                m.PlayAnimate(p, I, {Transparency = 0.5} )
            else
                m.PlayAnimate(F, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(f, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(V, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(p, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            end
        end)
        O(Y:GetValue())
        table.insert(M.Signals, Y:Connect(O))
        table.insert(
        M.Signals,
        F.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            m.PlayAnimate(p, I, {Transparency = 0.25} )
        end))
    )
    table.insert(
    M.Signals,
    F.MouseLeave:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        m.PlayAnimate(p, I, {Transparency = 0.5} )
    end))
)

    if A == "Default" then
        new.new("trash-can", "You can't delete default config!", 3.5)
        return
    end

    n()
    M:RefreshConfig()

end)

task.delay(1, function()
    if M.SelectedConfig == "Default" then
        local C = g.ConfigFolder .. "/Default"
        local Y = "Default"
        if isfile(C)then
            local m = readfile(C)
            M:LoadData(m)
            M.SelectedConfig = Y
            v.Text = Y
            n()
            M:RefreshConfig()
            new.new("folder", "Loaded Default Config", 3.5)
            task.spawn(function()
                while true do
                    task.wait(5.75)
                    if isfile(C) and M.SelectedConfig == "Default" then
                        writefile(g.ConfigFolder .. "/Default", M:GetData(true))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
local b = m:CreateInput(j, function()
    local C = g.ConfigFolder .. ("/" .. (M.SelectedConfig or "Default"))
    if isfile(C)then
        writefile(g.ConfigFolder .. ("/" .. (M.SelectedConfig or "Default")), M:GetData())
        new.new("folder", "Saved " .. tostring(M.SelectedConfig), 3.5)
    end
end)
m:AddSignal(b.MouseEnter:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(j, I, {TextTransparency = 0.1} )
end)))

    m.PlayAnimate(j, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
end)))
local y = m:CreateInput(s, function()
    local C = X.Text
    if C and (C:byte() and (not C:find("/", 1, true) and not C:find("\\", 1, true)))then
        C = string.sub(C, 1, 24)
        writefile(g.ConfigFolder .. ("/" .. C), M:GetData())
        M.SelectedConfig = C
        v.Text = C
        new.new("folder", "Created " .. tostring(C), 3.5)
        X.Text = ""
        n()
        M:RefreshConfig()
    end
end)

    m.PlayAnimate(handleY, I, {TextTransparency = 0.1} )
end))
m:AddSignal(y.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    m.PlayAnimate(handleY, I, {TextTransparency = 0.35} )
end))
M:RefreshConfig()
L.MouseButton1Click:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if M.UnsafeThread then
        M.UnsafeThread:Disconnect()
        M.UnsafeThread = nil
    end
    Y:SetValue(true)
    M.UnsafeThread = A.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            if not m:IsMouseOverFrame(H)then
                if M.UnsafeThread then
                    M.UnsafeThread:Disconnect()
                    M.UnsafeThread = nil
                end
                Y:SetValue(false)
            end
        end
    end)
end))
return M
