local Mz
m:CreateInput(
handleG,
LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if Mz then
        Mz:Disconnect()
        Mz = nil
    end
    Ez.Signal:SetValue(true)
    Mz = A.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            if
            not m:IsMouseOverFrame(Ez.Root)
            and (not m:IsMouseOverFrame(handleG) and not m.IsMosueOverOtherFrame)
            then
                if Mz then
                    Mz:Disconnect()
                    Mz = nil
                end
                Ez.Signal:SetValue(false)
            end
        end
    end)
end)
)
g.UserSettings = Ez
m:AddSignal(g.Signal:Connect(function(C)
    if C then
        return
    end
    if Mz then
        Mz:Disconnect()
        Mz = nil
    end
    Ez.Signal:SetValue(false)
end))
function g.SetAccount(Y, C)
    C = m:ProcessParams(C, {Profile = m.UserProfile, Username = z.DisplayName, Expires = "Never"} )
    L.Text = C.Username
    e.Image = C.Profile
    m:SetMemberStatusLabel(k, C.Expires)
    g.Username = C.Username or g.Username
    g.Profile = C.Profile or g.Profile
    g.Expires = C.Expires or g.Expires
    if g.UserSettings.UserFrame then
        g.UserSettings.UserFrame:SetUsername(g.Username)
        g.UserSettings.UserFrame:SetProfile(g.Profile)
        g.UserSettings.UserFrame:SetExpires(g.Expires)
    else
        g.UserSettings.UserFrame = Ez:AddUserFrame(g.Username, g.Profile, g.Expires)
    end
end
function g.SetSize(Y, C)
    g.BaseSize = C or g.BaseSize
    g.Size = g.BaseSize
    if g.Responsive and (R and (g.BaseSize.X.Scale == 0 and g.BaseSize.Y.Scale == 0))then
        local C = R.ViewportSize
        local Y = math.max(320, C.X - 24)
        local m = math.max(260, C.Y - 24)
        local new = math.min(1, Y / g.BaseSize.X.Offset, m / g.BaseSize.Y.Offset)
        g.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(g.BaseSize.X.Offset * new), math.floor(g.BaseSize.Y.Offset * new))
    end
    if g.Signal:GetValue()then
        m.PlayAnimate(M, X, {Size = g.Size} )
    end
end
g:SetAccount()
g:SetSize(g.BaseSize)
if R then
    m:AddSignal((R:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize")):Connect(function()
        if not g.__Destroyed then
            g:SetSize(g.BaseSize)
        end
    end))
end
if g.Keybind then
    m:AddSignal(A.InputBegan:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
        if
        C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
        and (C.KeyCode == g.Keybind or C.KeyCode.Name == g.Keybind)
        then
            if not Y then
                g:ToggleInterface()
            end
        end
    end)))
end
function g.ToggleInterface(C)
    g.Signal:SetValue(not g.Signal:GetValue())
    if g.__3DRender then
        g.Load3DBlock()
    end
end
function g.SetVisible(Y, C)
    g.Signal:SetValue(C == true)
    if g.__3DRender then
        g.Load3DBlock()
    end
end
function g.Toggle(C)
    g:ToggleInterface()
end
function g.Notify(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    return(m:CreateNotification()).new( {
        Title = C.Title or "VETERANO TEAM",
        Content = C.Content or C.Text or "",
        Duration = C.Duration or 4,
    } )
end
function g.Watermark(C)
    if m.__WatermarkCache then
        return m.__WatermarkCache
    end
    local Y = {}
    local g = Instance.new("Frame")
    local new = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local M = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    local H = m:CreateShadow(g)
    g.Name = m.RandomString()
    g.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    g.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    g.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 6, 16)
    g.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    g.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    g.BorderSizePixel = 0
    g.ClipsDescendants = true
    g.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 10, 0, 10)
    g.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 30)
    g.ZIndex = 16
    new.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 25)
    new.Parent = g
    M.Parent = g
    M.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    M.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    M.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
    local A = Instance.new("Frame")
    A.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 0)
    A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    A.Parent = g
    A.LayoutOrder = 5
    (g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if g.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
            g.Visible = false
            g.Parent = nil
        else
            g.Parent = m.ScreenGui
            g.Visible = true
        end
    end));
    (M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        m.PlayAnimate(g, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, M.AbsoluteContentSize.X + 5, 0, 30)} )
    end))
    m.__WatermarkCache = Y
    H:Render(true)
    Y.Renders = {}
    Y.Status = true
    function Y.SetRender(new, C)
        Y.Status = C
        if C then
            m.PlayAnimate(g, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.2} )
            H:Render(true)
            for C, Y in next, Y.Renders do
                pcall(Y, true)
            end
        else
            m.PlayAnimate(g, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            H:Render(false)
            for C, Y in next, Y.Renders do
                pcall(Y, false)
            end
        end
    end
    function Y.AddBlock(M, C, new)
        local H = {}
        local A = Instance.new("Frame")
        local u = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local q = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        A.Parent = g
        A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
        A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        A.BorderSizePixel = 0
        A.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 30)
        u.Name = m.RandomString()
        u.Parent = A
        u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        u.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        u.BorderSizePixel = 0
        u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0.5, 0)
        u.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 25)
        u.ZIndex = 17
        u.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        u.Text = new
        u.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        u.TextSize = 15
        u.TextTransparency = 0.2
        u.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        q.Name = m.RandomString()
        q.Parent = A
        q.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        q.BorderSizePixel = 0
        q.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)
        q.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
        q.ZIndex = 17
        q.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
        q.Text = C
        q.TextColor3 = m.AccentColor
        q.TextSize = 18
        q.TextTransparency = 0.25
        q.TextWrapped = true
        H.Update = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
            local Y = F:GetTextSize(u.Text, u.TextSize, u.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
            if H.Visible then
                m.PlayAnimate(A, X, {Size = UDim2.new(0, Y.X + 35, 0, 30)} )
            else
                m.PlayAnimate(A, X, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30)} )
            end
        end)
        H.Visible = true
        H.Update()
        function H.SetVisible(g, C)
            H.Visible = C
            if Y.Status then
                H.SetRender(C)
            end
            H.Update()
        end
        H.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
            if C and H.Visible then
                m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.2} )
                m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
            else
                m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            end
        end)
        table.insert(Y.Renders, H.SetRender)
        function H.SetText(Y, C)
            u.Text = C
            H.Update()
        end
        function H.Input(Y, C)
            local g, new = m:CreateInput(A, C)
            return new
        end
        return H
    end
    return Y
end
g.Frame = M
table.insert(m.Windows, g)
function g.Destroy(C)
    if g.__Destroyed then
        return
    end
    g.__Destroyed = true
    m:Unload()
end
g:SetRender(false)
return g
end
function m.CreateNotification(C)

        }
    )

        m.PlayAnimate(g, I, {Position = UDim2.new(1, - 25, 0, 55)} )
    end
    local Y = Instance.new("Frame")
    local new = Instance.new("Frame")
    local M = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local H = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local A = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local q = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local P = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local z = m:CreateShadow(new, true)
    Y.Name = m.RandomString()

    Y.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    Y.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Y.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

    new.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 55)
    new.ZIndex = 130
    M.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    M.Parent = new
    H.Transparency = 0.65
    H.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    H.Parent = new
    A.Name = m.RandomString()
    A.Parent = new
    A.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    A.BorderSizePixel = 0
    A.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)
    A.Size = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 35)
    A.ZIndex = 131
    A.Image = C.Logo
    A.ImageColor3 = m.IconColor
    u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
    u.Parent = A
    q.Name = m.RandomString()
    q.Parent = new
    q.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    q.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    q.BorderSizePixel = 0
    q.Position = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 7)
    q.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 20)
    q.ZIndex = 132
    q.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    q.Text = C.Title
    q.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    q.TextSize = 17
    q.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    P.Name = m.RandomString()
    P.Parent = new
    P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    P.BorderSizePixel = 0
    P.Position = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 28)
    P.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 15)
    P.ZIndex = 132
    P.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    P.Text = C.Content
    P.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    P.TextSize = 12
    P.TextTransparency = 0.65
    P.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    local isTValid = F:GetTextSize(q.Text, q.TextSize, q.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
    local T = F:GetTextSize(P.Text, P.TextSize, P.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
    local f = math.max(isTValid.X, T.X)
    new.Size = UDim2.new(0, f + 65, 0, 55)
    z:Render(true)
    m.PlayAnimate(new, X, {Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)} )
    Y.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 65)
    task.delay(
    C.Duration or 5,
    LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if m.__WatermarkCache then
            m.PlayAnimate(g, I, {Position = UDim2.new(1, - 25, 0, 55)} )
        end
        z:Render(false)
        m.PlayAnimate(new, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(H, I, {Transparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {ImageTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        m.PlayAnimate(P, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
        task.wait(0.125)
        m.PlayAnimate(Y, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)} )
        task.wait(0.125)
        Y:Destroy()
    end)
)
end
return Y
end
function m.CreateLogger(C)

    local Y = {}

        z.Size = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 15)
        z.ZIndex = 133

        z.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 210, 255)
        z.TextSize = 13
        z.TextTransparency = 1
        z.TextWrapped = true
        local T = F:GetTextSize(u.Text, u.TextSize, u.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
        m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, T.X + 35, 0, 20), BackgroundTransparency = 0.075} )
        task.delay(
        0.15,
        LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            isTValid:Render(true)
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 0.65} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
            m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0} )
            m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 0.25} )
            task.wait(new + 0.1)
            isTValid:Render(false)
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {Transparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(u, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(q, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
            m.PlayAnimate(z, I, {TextTransparency = 1} )
            task.wait(0.25)
            M:Destroy()
        end)
    )
end
return Y
end
function m.CreateIndicator(C)
    local Y = Instance.new("Frame")
    local g = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    Y.Name = m.RandomString()
    Y.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    Y.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    Y.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    Y.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Y.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    Y.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Y.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0.5, 0)
    Y.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
    Y.ZIndex = 15
    g.Parent = Y
    g.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    g.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local new = {}
    new.Color = {
        Red = Color3.fromRGB(255, 102, 105),
        Green = Color3.fromRGB(135, 255, 143),
        White = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186),
    }
    new.Root = Y
    function new.new(C)
        C = m:ProcessParams(C, {Name = "Indicator", Icon = "crosshairs", Color = "Red"} )
        local g = {CurrentColor = C.Color, Visible = false}
        local M = Instance.new("Frame")
        local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local A = Instance.new("Frame")
        local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local q = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        local P = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local z = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local isTValid = m:CreateShadow(M)
        M.Name = m.RandomString()
        M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 6, 16)
        M.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        M.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        M.BorderSizePixel = 0
        M.ClipsDescendants = true
        M.Size = UDim2.new(0, 85, 0, 40)
        M.ZIndex = 16
        M.Visible = false
        (M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency")):Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if M.BackgroundTransparency > 0.9 then
                M.Parent = nil
                M.Visible = false
            else
                M.Parent = Y
                M.Visible = true
            end
        end))
        H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 25)
        H.Parent = M
        A.Name = m.RandomString()
        A.Parent = M
        A.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        A.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        A.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        A.BorderSizePixel = 0
        A.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
        A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        A.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.649999976, 0)
        A.ZIndex = 17
        u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 25)
        u.Parent = A
        q.Rotation = 90
        q.Transparency = NumberSequence.new( {
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        } )
        q.Parent = A
        P.Name = m.RandomString()
        P.Parent = M
        P.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        P.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        P.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        P.BorderSizePixel = 0
        P.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)
        P.Size = UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 25)
        P.ZIndex = 17
        P.FontFace = m.BuiltInBold
        P.Text = C.Icon
        P.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        P.TextSize = 21
        P.TextTransparency = 1
        P.TextWrapped = true
        z.Name = m.RandomString()
        z.Parent = M
        z.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        z.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        z.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        z.BorderSizePixel = 0
        z.Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0.5, 0)
        z.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 40, 0, 25)
        z.ZIndex = 17
        z.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        z.Text = C.Name
        z.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 186)
        z.TextSize = 20
        z.TextTransparency = 1
        z.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        g.Update = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local C = F:GetTextSize(z.Text, z.TextSize, z.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
            m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {Size = UDim2.new(0, C.X + 60, 0, 40)} )
        end)
        g.SetRender = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
            g.Visible = Y
            if Y then
                m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.2} )
                m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = new.Color[g.CurrentColor]} )
                m.PlayAnimate(P, X, {TextTransparency = 0.25, TextColor3 = new.Color[g.CurrentColor]} )
                m.PlayAnimate(z, X, {TextTransparency = 0.2, TextColor3 = new.Color[g.CurrentColor]} )
                isTValid:Render(true)
            else
                m.PlayAnimate(M, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1} )
                m.PlayAnimate(A, I, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, BackgroundColor3 = new.Color[g.CurrentColor]} )
                m.PlayAnimate(P, X, {TextTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = new.Color[g.CurrentColor]} )
                m.PlayAnimate(z, X, {TextTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = new.Color[g.CurrentColor]} )
                isTValid:Render(false)
            end
            g.Update()
        end)
        g.Update()
        g:SetRender(false)
        function g.SetColor(Y, C)
            g.CurrentColor = C
            if g.Visible then
                g:SetRender(true)
            end
        end
        function g.SetText(m, Y)
            C.Name = Y
            z.Text = C.Name
            g.Update()
        end
        return g
    end
    return new
end

V.ZIndex = 34
m.Drag(q, new, 0.1)
local I = false
local O = C.NameSide
local function handleM()
    local C = isTValid.AbsoluteSize
    local Y = C.X > 0 and C.X or 226
    local g = C.Y > 0 and C.Y or 268
    local m = F:GetTextSize(V.Text, V.TextSize, V.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, 18))
    local new = 7
    return {
        Top = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.14 - (m.Y / 2 + new) / g),
        Right = Vector2.new(0.79 + (m.X / 2 + new) / Y, 0.5),
        Bottom = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.86 + (m.Y / 2 + new) / g),
        Left = Vector2.new(0.21 - (m.X / 2 + new) / Y, 0.5),
    }
end
local function updateInstanceProperties(Y)
    Y = tostring(Y or "Top")
    Y = string.upper(string.sub(Y, 1, 1)) .. string.lower(string.sub(Y, 2))
    local m = handleM()
    if not m[Y]then
        Y = "Top"
    end
    g.NameSide = Y
    g.NamePosition = m[Y]
    V.Position = UDim2.fromScale(g.NamePosition.X, g.NamePosition.Y)
    if type(C.NameSideChanged) == "function" then
        C.NameSideChanged(Y)
    end
end
local function updatePosition(C)
    local Y = isTValid.AbsoluteSize
    if Y.X <= 0 or Y.Y <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local g = Vector2.new(
    math.clamp((C.Position.X - isTValid.AbsolutePosition.X) / Y.X, 0.02, 0.98),
    math.clamp((C.Position.Y - isTValid.AbsolutePosition.Y) / Y.Y, 0.035, 0.965)
)
V.Position = UDim2.fromScale(g.X, g.Y)
local m = g - Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
O = math.abs(m.X / 0.29) > math.abs(m.Y / 0.36) and (m.X > 0 and "Right" or "Left")
or (m.Y > 0 and "Bottom" or "Top")
end
updateInstanceProperties(C.NameSide)
m:AddSignal(V.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
    if C.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and C.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return
    end
    I = true
    O = g.NameSide
    updatePosition(C)
    local Y
    Y = C.Changed:Connect(function()
        if C.UserInputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.End then
            return
        end
        I = false
        updateInstanceProperties(O)
        Y:Disconnect()
    end)
end))
m:AddSignal(A.InputChanged:Connect(function(C)
    if
    I and (
    C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
    or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
)
then
    updatePosition(C)
end
end))
function g.SetVisible(Y, C)
    g.Visible = C == true
    new.Visible = g.Visible
    g.Signal:SetValue(g.Visible)
    p:Render(g.Visible)
end
function g.SetBox(Y, C)
    R.Visible = C == true
end
function g.SetNameVisible(Y, C)
    V.Visible = C == true
end
function g.SetName(Y, C)
    V.Text = tostring(C or "PLAYER")
    updateInstanceProperties(g.NameSide)
end
function g.SetNamePosition(Y, C)
    local g = C - Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    updateInstanceProperties(
    math.abs(g.X) > math.abs(g.Y) and (g.X > 0 and "Right" or "Left") or (g.Y > 0 and "Bottom" or "Top")
)
end
function g.GetNamePosition(C)
    return g.NamePosition
end
function g.SetNameSide(Y, C)
    updateInstanceProperties(C)
end
function g.GetNameSide(C)
    return g.NameSide
end
function g.SetColor(Y, C)
    Q.Color = C
    V.TextColor3 = C
end
function g.SetImage(Y, C)
    f.Image = m:ResolveImage(C, "esp-preview-custom.webp", m.ESPPreviewAvatar)
end
function g.Destroy(C)
    g.Signal:SetValue(false)
    p:Render(false)
    if new then
        new:Destroy()
    end
end
g.Instance = new
g.Panel = M
g.Avatar = f
return g
end
function m.CreateKeybindList(Y, C)
    C = m:ProcessParams(C, {Title = "KEYBIND", Visible = true, Position = UDim2.new(1, - 250, 0, 55)} )
    local g = {Items = {} , Visible = C.Visible == true, Signal = m:CreateSignal(C.Visible == true)}
    local new = Instance.new("Frame")
    local M = Instance.new("Frame")
    local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
    local A = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    local F = Instance.new("Frame")
    local u = Instance.new("Frame")
    local q = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    local P = Instance.new("Frame")
    local z = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    new.Name = "VeteranoKeybindHUD"
    new.Parent = m.ScreenGui
    new.Position = C.Position
    new.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 38)
    new.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    new.BorderSizePixel = 0
    new.Active = true
    new.Visible = g.Visible
    new.ZIndex = 29
    M.Name = "Panel"
    M.Parent = new
    M.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
    M.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 38)
    M.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 6, 16)
    M.BackgroundTransparency = m.EnabledBlur and 0.055 or 0.0255
    M.BorderSizePixel = 0
    M.Active = true
    M.ClipsDescendants = true
    M.ZIndex = 30
    H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    H.Parent = M
    A.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
    A.Transparency = 0.65
    A.Thickness = 1
    A.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    A.Parent = M
    local isTValid = m:CreateShadow(M)
    isTValid:Render(g.Visible)
    if m.EnabledBlur then
        m:CreateBlurModule(M, g.Signal)
    end
    F.Name = "Header"
    F.Parent = M
    F.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    F.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    F.Active = true
    F.ZIndex = 31
    u.Name = "Divider"
    u.Parent = F
    u.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    u.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
    u.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    u.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 45, 56)
    u.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    u.BorderSizePixel = 0
    u.ZIndex = 32
    q.Parent = F
    q.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
    q.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    q.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    q.Text = C.Title
    q.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 230, 255)
    q.TextSize = 12
    q.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    q.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    q.ZIndex = 32
    P.Parent = M
    P.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 38)
    P.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    P.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    P.ClipsDescendants = true
    P.ZIndex = 31
    z.Parent = P
    z.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    local function updateSize()
        local C = 0
        for Y, g in next, g.Items do
            if g.Visible then
                C = C + 1
            end
        end
        P.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, C * 28)
        new.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 38 + C * 28)
        M.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 38 + C * 28)
    end
    m.Drag(F, new, 0.1)
    function g.Add(M, C, Y, new)
        local H = {Visible = true, Active = new == true}
        local A = Instance.new("Frame")
        local F = Instance.new("Frame")
        local u = Instance.new("UICorner")
        local q = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local z = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        A.Parent = P
        A.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        A.BackgroundColor3 = m.MainColor
        A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        A.BorderSizePixel = 0
        A.ZIndex = 31
        F.Parent = A
        F.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 11)
        F.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
        F.BorderSizePixel = 0
        F.ZIndex = 32
        u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        u.Parent = F
        q.Parent = A
        q.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0)
        q.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 96, 1, 0)
        q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        q.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        q.Text = tostring(C or "Bind")
        q.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 222, 230)
        q.TextSize = 11
        q.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        q.ZIndex = 32
        z.Parent = A
        z.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 72, 0, 0)
        z.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 28)
        z.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        z.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        z.Text = string.upper(tostring(Y or "NONE"))
        z.TextColor3 = m.AccentColor
        z.TextSize = 10
        z.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        z.ZIndex = 32
        local function updateInstanceProperties()
            F.BackgroundColor3 = H.Active and m.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(68, 70, 80)
            q.TextTransparency = H.Active and 0 or 0.4
            z.TextTransparency = H.Active and 0 or 0.45
        end
        function H.SetActive(Y, C)
            H.Active = C == true
            updateInstanceProperties()
        end
        function H.SetKey(Y, C)
            z.Text = string.upper(tostring(C or "NONE"))
        end
        function H.SetText(Y, C)
            q.Text = tostring(C or "Bind")
        end
        function H.SetVisible(Y, C)
            H.Visible = C == true
            A.Visible = H.Visible
            updateSize()
        end
        H.Instance = A
        table.insert(g.Items, H)
        updateInstanceProperties()
        updateSize()
        return H
    end
    function g.SetVisible(Y, C)
        g.Visible = C == true
        new.Visible = g.Visible
        g.Signal:SetValue(g.Visible)
        isTValid:Render(g.Visible)
    end
    function g.Destroy(C)
        g.Signal:SetValue(false)
        isTValid:Render(false)
        if new then
            new:Destroy()
        end
    end
    g.Instance = new
    g.Panel = M
    return g
end
function m.Unload(Y)
    if m.__Unloaded then
        return
    end
    m.__Unloaded = true
    for C, Y in next, m.Windows do
        Y.__Destroyed = true
        if Y.Set3DRender then
            pcall(Y.Set3DRender, Y, false)
        end
    end
    for C, Y in next, m.GlobalSignals do
        pcall(Y.Disconnect, Y)
    end
    table.clear(m.GlobalSignals)
    table.clear(m.Windows)
    table.clear(m.Flags)
    if m.ScreenGui and m.ScreenGui.Parent then
        m.ScreenGui:Destroy()
    end
    if C.__VETERANO_UI_V2_LIBRARY == m then
        C.__VETERANO_UI_V2_LIBRARY = nil
    end
end
m.DestroyAll = m.Unload
C.__VETERANO_UI_V2_LIBRARY = m
C.VeteranoTeamUIV2 = m
end
do
    local C = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local Y = C.VeteranoTeamUIV2 or C.__VETERANO_UI_V2_LIBRARY
    assert(Y, "VeteranoTeam UI Lib V2 nao carregada")
    local g = {Config = {Folder = "VeteranoTeam/sintonia"} }
    local m = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local E = game:GetService("TextService")
    local M = {
        user = "person",
        cross = "mascara",
        ["repeat"] = "two-arrows-spin-clockwise",
        ["trash-2"] = "trash-can",
        wrench = "gear",
        target = "crosshairs",
        crosshair = "crosshairs",
        globe = "globe-detailed",
        ["map-pin"] = "location-pin-map",
        hammer = "hammer-code",
        car = "compass",
        settings = "gear",
    }
    local function resolveIcon(C)
        return M[C] or C or "crosshairs"
    end
    g.ResolveIcon = resolveIcon
    local function isCValid(C)
        return(getgenv and (getgenv())[C]) or (_G and _G[C])
    end
    local function handler(C)
        local Y, g = isCValid("isfolder"), isCValid("makefolder")
        if not Y or not g then
            return false
        end
        local m = ""
        for C in string.gmatch(C, "[^/]+")do
            m = m == "" and C or (m .. ("/" .. C))
            if not Y(m)then
                g(m)
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    function g.Config.isSupported()
        return isCValid("writefile") ~= nil
        and (
        isCValid("readfile") ~= nil
        and (isCValid("listfiles") ~= nil and (isCValid("isfolder") ~= nil and isCValid("makefolder") ~= nil))
    )
end
function g.Config._sanitize(C)
    return(((tostring(C or "")):gsub("[^%w%-_ ]", "")):gsub("^%s+", "")):gsub("%s+$", "")
end
function g.Config.list()
    local C, Y = {} , isCValid("listfiles")
    if not Y or not handler(g.Config.Folder)then
        return C
    end
    local m, E = pcall(Y, g.Config.Folder)
    if not m then
        return C
    end
    for Y, g in ipairs(E)do
        local m = (tostring(g)):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
        if m then
            C[#C + 1] = m
        end
    end
    table.sort(C)
    return C
end
function g.Config.save(C, Y)
    C = g.Config._sanitize(C)
    if C == "" then
        return false, "nome invalido"
    end
    if not g.Config.isSupported() or not handler(g.Config.Folder)then
        return false, "executor sem filesystem"
    end
    local m, E = pcall(function()
        return(game:GetService("HttpService")):JSONEncode(Y or {} )
    end)
    if not m then
        return false, "falha ao serializar"
    end
    local M = pcall(isCValid("writefile"), g.Config.Folder .. ("/" .. (C .. ".json")), E)
    return M, M and nil or "falha ao escrever"
end
function g.Config.load(C)
    C = g.Config._sanitize(C)
    local Y = isCValid("readfile")
    if C == "" or not Y then
        return nil, "config invalida"
    end
    local m, E = pcall(Y, g.Config.Folder .. ("/" .. (C .. ".json")))
    if not m then
        return nil, "falha ao ler"
    end
    local M, resolveIcon = pcall(function()
        return(game:GetService("HttpService")):JSONDecode(E)
    end)
    return M and resolveIcon or nil, M and nil or "arquivo corrompido"
end
function g.Config.delete(C)
    C = g.Config._sanitize(C)
    local Y = isCValid("delfile")
    if C == "" or not Y then
        return false, "falha ao apagar"
    end
    local m = pcall(Y, g.Config.Folder .. ("/" .. (C .. ".json")))
    return m, m and nil or "falha ao apagar"
end
local function secondaryHandler(C, Y)
    if Y and (Y ~= "" and type(C.ToolTip) == "function")then
        C:ToolTip(Y)
    end
end
local function alternateHandler(C, Y, g, m)
    local E = {Instance = Y.Root, Native = C}
    function E.Get(Y)
        local m = C:GetValue()
        return g and g(m) or m
    end
    function E.Set(g, Y)
        C:SetValue(m and m(Y) or Y)
    end
    function E.SetOptions(g, Y)
        if C.SetValues then
            C:SetValues(Y or {} )
        end
    end
    return E
end
local P = {}
P.__index = P
function P._order(C)
    C._nextOrder = (C._nextOrder or 0) + 1
    return C._nextOrder
end
local function updateLayoutOrder(C, Y)
    if Y then
        Y.LayoutOrder = C:_order()
    end
end
function P._row(Y, C)
    local g = Y.Native:AddLabel(tostring(C.Text or C.Name or "Controle"))
    updateLayoutOrder(Y, g.Root)
    secondaryHandler(g, C.Hint or C.ToolTip)
    return g
end
function P.AddToggle(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    local g = Y:_row(C)
    return alternateHandler(
    g:AddToggle( {Default = C.Default == true, Callback = C.Callback or function()end, Flag = C.Flag} ),
    g
)
end
function P.AddSlider(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    local g = Y:_row(C)
    local m = tonumber(C.Step) or 1
    local E = (tostring(m)):match("%.(%d+)")
    return alternateHandler(
    g:AddSlider( {
        Default = tonumber(C.Default) or 0,
        Min = tonumber(C.Min) or 0,
        Max = tonumber(C.Max) or 100,
        Rounding = E and #E or 0,
        Type = C.Suffix or "",
        Callback = C.Callback or function()end,
        Flag = C.Flag,
    } ),
    g
)
end
function P.AddDropdown(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    local g = Y:_row(C)
    return alternateHandler(
    g:AddDropdown( {
        Default = C.Default,
        Values = C.Options or C.Values or {} ,
        Multi = C.Multi == true,
        Callback = C.Callback or function()end,
        Flag = C.Flag,
    } ),
    g
)
end
function P.AddTextbox(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    local g = Y:_row(C)
    return alternateHandler(
    g:AddTextInput( {
        Default = C.Default or "",
        Placeholder = C.Placeholder or "",
        Numeric = C.Numeric == true,
        Callback = C.Callback or function()end,
        Flag = C.Flag,
    } ),
    g
)
end
P.AddTextInput = P.AddTextbox
function P.AddKeybind(g, C)
    C = C or {}
    local E = g:_row(C)
    local M = C.Default
    local function isMValid(C)
        if typeof(C) == "EnumItem" then
            return C
        end
        local Y, g = pcall(function()
            return Enum.KeyCode[tostring(C)]
        end)
        return Y and g or C
    end
    local isCValid = E:AddKeybind( {
        Default = typeof(M) == "EnumItem" and M.Name or M,
        Callback = function(Y)
            M = isMValid(Y)
            if C.OnChanged then
                C.OnChanged(M)
            end
        end,
        Flag = C.Flag,
    } )
    Y:AddSignal(m.InputBegan:Connect(function(Y, g)
        if
        not g
        and (
        Y.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
        and (not m:GetFocusedTextBox() and (C.Callback and Y.KeyCode == isMValid(M)))
    )
    then
        C.Callback()
    end
end))
return alternateHandler(isCValid, E, function()
    return isMValid(M)
end, function(C)
    M = isMValid(C)
    return typeof(M) == "EnumItem" and M.Name or M
end)
end
function P.AddButton(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    local g = Y.Native:AddButton( {
        Name = tostring(C.Text or C.Name or "Button"),
        Icon = "mouse-button-left",
        ToolTip = C.Hint or C.ToolTip,
        Callback = C.Callback or function()end,
    } )
    updateLayoutOrder(Y, g.Root)
    return {Instance = g.Root, Label = g.Label, Icon = g.Icon, Native = g}
end
function P.AddLabel(g, C)
    local m = g.Native:AddLabel(tostring(C or ""), true)
    updateLayoutOrder(g, m.Root)
    local M = m.Root and m.Root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
    if not M then
        return {
            Instance = m.Root,
            Native = m,
            SetText = function(C, Y)
                m:SetText(Y)
            end,
        }
    end
    M.TextWrapped = true
    M.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
    M.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    local resolveIcon = M.Position.Y.Offset
    if resolveIcon < 0 then
        resolveIcon = 0
    end
    M.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 22, 1, - resolveIcon * 2)
    local isCValid = m.Root.Size.Y.Offset
    if isCValid < 2 then
        isCValid = 30
    end
    local function updateSize()
        if not m.Root.Parent then
            return
        end
        local C = M.AbsoluteSize.X
        if C <= 1 then
            C = math.max(1, m.Root.AbsoluteSize.X - 22)
        end
        local Y = M.RichText and (M.Text:gsub("<[^>]->", "")) or M.Text
        local g = (E:GetTextSize(Y, M.TextSize, M.Font, Vector2.new(C, math.huge))).Y
        m.Root.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.max(isCValid, (g + resolveIcon * 2) + 4))
    end
    Y:AddSignal((M:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text")):Connect(updateSize))
    Y:AddSignal((m.Root:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize")):Connect(updateSize))
    task.defer(updateSize)
    task.delay(0.25, updateSize)
    return {
        Instance = M,
        Native = m,
        SetText = function(C, Y)
            M.Text = tostring(Y)
        end,
    }
end
function g.MergeKeybindRow(C, Y)
    local g = C and C.Instance
    local m = Y and Y.Instance
    if not g or not m then
        return Y
    end
    local function handleM(C)
        for C, Y in ipairs(C:GetChildren())do
            if Y:IsA("Frame") and Y:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIListLayout")then
                return Y
            end
        end
    end
    local M, resolveIcon = handleM(m), handleM(g)
    if not M or not resolveIcon then
        return Y
    end
    local isCValid = 0
    for C, Y in ipairs(resolveIcon:GetChildren())do
        if Y:IsA("GuiObject") and Y.LayoutOrder < isCValid then
            isCValid = Y.LayoutOrder
        end
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(M:GetChildren())do
        if Y:IsA("GuiObject")then
            Y.LayoutOrder = isCValid - 1
            Y.Parent = resolveIcon
        end
    end
    m:Destroy()
    return Y
end
function P.AddDivider(Y, C)
    return Y:AddLabel("\226\128\148 " .. (tostring(C or "") .. " \226\128\148"))
end
local o = {}
o.__index = o

    return setmetatable( {Native = g, Body = g.Root, Container = g.Frame, _nextOrder = 0} , P)
end
local T = {}
T.__index = T
function T.AddColumn(C)
    C.ColumnCount = C.ColumnCount + 1
    return setmetatable( {Tab = C, Position = C.ColumnCount % 2 == 1 and "left" or "right"} , o)
end
function T.AddSection(Y, C)
    if not Y.DefaultColumn then
        Y.DefaultColumn = Y:AddColumn()
    end
    return Y.DefaultColumn:AddSection(C)
end
local f = {}
f.__index = f

    C = C or {}
    return setmetatable(
    {Native = Y.Window.Native:AddTab( {Name = C.Name or "Tab", Icon = resolveIcon(C.Icon)} ), ColumnCount = 0} ,
    T
)
end
local R = {}
R.__index = R
function R._track(g, C)
    return Y:AddSignal(C)
end
function R.SetVisible(Y, C)
    C = C == true
    Y._targetVisible = C
    Y.Native:SetVisible(C)
end
function R.Toggle(C)
    local Y = os.clock()
    if Y - (C._lastToggleAt or 0) < 0.22 then
        return
    end
    C._lastToggleAt = Y
    C:SetVisible(not C._targetVisible)
end
R.ToggleInterface = R.Toggle
function R.SetUser(g, C)
    C = C or {}
    g.Native:SetAccount( {
        Username = C.Username or (game:GetService("Players")).LocalPlayer.DisplayName,
        Profile = C.Profile or Y.UserProfile,
        Expires = "VT MEMBRO",
    } )
end
function R.Notify(Y, C)
    return Y.Native:Notify(C)
end
function R.Destroy(C)
    return C.Native:Destroy()
end
function R.AddSideTab(Y, C)
    C = C or {}
    Y.Native:AddTabLabel(C.Name or C.Group or "SECAO")
    return setmetatable( {Window = Y} , f)
end
local Q = {
    Background = Color3.fromRGB(10, 6, 16),
    Panel = Color3.fromRGB(22, 14, 32),
    Card = Color3.fromRGB(32, 22, 48),
    Border = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140),
    Accent = Y.AccentColor,
    Text = Color3.fromRGB(240, 230, 255),
    TextMuted = Color3.fromRGB(160, 140, 190),
    Cyan = Color3.fromRGB(200, 120, 255),
}
function g.CreateWindow(g, C)
    C = C or {}
    local m = Y:ResolveImage(C.LogoUrl, "sintonia-logo.png", Y.VeteranoIcon)
    local E = Y:CreateWindow( {
        Name = "VETERANO TEAM",
        Content = C.Subtitle or "Sintonia V2",
        Logo = m,
        TitleImage = Y.VeteranoWordmark,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tonumber(C.Width) or 900, tonumber(C.Height) or 560),
        ConfigFolder = "VeteranoTeam/configs/sintonia-v2",
        Enable3DRenderer = false,
        Responsive = true,
        Keybind = false,
    } )
    local M = tonumber(C.ToggleButtonSize) or 48
    local resolveIcon = tonumber(C.ToggleButtonImageInset) or 0
    local isCValid = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    isCValid.Name = "VeteranoSintoniaToggle"
    isCValid.Parent = Y.ScreenGui
    isCValid.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, - M / 2)
    isCValid.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(M, M)
    isCValid.BackgroundColor3 = Q.Panel
    isCValid.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    isCValid.BorderSizePixel = 0
    isCValid.Image = ""
    isCValid.ZIndex = 80
    local handler = Instance.new("UICorner", isCValid)
    handler.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local secondaryHandler = Instance.new("UIStroke", isCValid)
    secondaryHandler.Color = Q.Border
    secondaryHandler.Thickness = 1
    local alternateHandler = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    alternateHandler.Name = "Glyph"
    alternateHandler.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    alternateHandler.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(resolveIcon, resolveIcon)
    alternateHandler.Size = UDim2.new(1, - resolveIcon * 2, 1, - resolveIcon * 2)
    alternateHandler.Image = Y:ResolveImage(C.ToggleButtonImage, "sintonia-toggle.png", m)
    alternateHandler.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    alternateHandler.ZIndex = 81
    alternateHandler.Parent = isCValid;
    (Instance.new("UICorner", alternateHandler)).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local P = setmetatable(
    {Native = E, Root = E.Frame, ScreenGui = Y.ScreenGui, ToggleButton = isCValid, Theme = Q, _targetVisible = true} ,
    R
)
Y:AddSignal(isCValid.Activated:Connect(function()
    P:Toggle()
end))
return P
end
function g.CreateLoader(Y, C)
    local g = {Closed = false}
    function g.Close(C)
        C.Closed = true
    end
    task.defer(function()
        if not g.Closed and (C and C.OnLoad)then
            C.OnLoad()
        end
    end)
    return g
end
function g.DestroyAll(C)
    Y:Unload()
end
C.VeteranoTeamUI = g
C.__VETERANO_UI_LIBRARY = g
C.__KNWARE_UI_LIBRARY = g
end

local f = {}
local function handleIz(C, Y)
    if Y then
        f[C] = Y
    end
    return Y
end
local function handleReady(C)
    table.insert(o.connections, C)
    return C
end
local V = game:GetService("GuiService")
local function handleM(C)
    local Y = M.CurrentCamera
    local g = Y and Y.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
    local m = C:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui")
    local E = V:GetGuiInset()
    if m and m.IgnoreGuiInset then
        return E, g
    end
    return Vector2.zero, Vector2.new(g.X, g.Y - E.Y)
end
local function updateInstancePropertiesPosition(C, Y, m)
    Y = Y or C
    local E, M, H
    handleReady(Y.InputBegan:Connect(function(Y)
        local g = Y.UserInputType
        if g ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and g ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        E, M, H = g, Y.Position, C.AbsolutePosition
        handleReady(Y.Changed:Connect(function()
            if Y.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                E = nil
                if m then
                    m(C.Position)
                end
            end
        end))
    end))
    handleReady(g.InputChanged:Connect(function(Y)
        if not E then
            return
        end
        local g = Y.UserInputType
        if
        not(
        (E == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and g == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
        or (E == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and g == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
    )
    then
        return
    end
    local m, A = handleM(C)
    local F = C.AbsoluteSize
    local u = (H.X + (Y.Position.X - M.X)) + m.X
    local q = (H.Y + (Y.Position.Y - M.Y)) + m.Y
    C.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
    math.clamp(u, m.X + 8, math.max(m.X + 8, (A.X - F.X) - 8)),
    math.clamp(q, m.Y + 8, math.max(m.Y + 8, (A.Y - F.Y) - 8))
)
end))
end
local O = Color3.fromRGB(10, 6, 16)
local X = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 140)
local B = Color3.fromRGB(42, 45, 56)
local s = Color3.fromRGB(240, 230, 255)
local function createDivisor(C, Y)
    Y = Y or {}
    local g = A.VeteranoTeamUIV2 or A.__VETERANO_UI_V2_LIBRARY
    local m = g and g.EnabledBlur
    C.BackgroundColor3 = O
    C.BackgroundTransparency = m and 0.055 or 0.0255
    C.BorderSizePixel = 0
    local E = C:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") or Instance.new("UICorner")
    E.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    E.Parent = C
    local M = C:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") or Instance.new("UIStroke")
    M.Color = X
    M.Transparency = 0.65
    M.Thickness = 1
    M.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    M.Parent = C
    if Y.Divisor then
        local g = Instance.new("Frame")
        g.Name = "Divisor"
        g.BackgroundColor3 = B
        g.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        g.BorderSizePixel = 0
        g.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, Y.Divisor)
        g.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        g.ZIndex = (C.ZIndex or 1) + 1
        g.Parent = C
    end
    if Y.Titulo then
        Y.Titulo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        Y.Titulo.TextSize = 12
        Y.Titulo.TextColor3 = s
    end
    local H = {Frame = C}
    local F = g and g:CreateSignal(false)
    local u = g and g:CreateShadow(C)
    if m and F then
        g:CreateBlurModule(C, F)
    end
    function H.SetVisible(g, Y)
        Y = Y == true
        C.Visible = Y
        if F then
            F:SetValue(Y)
        end
        if u then
            u:Render(Y)
        end
    end
    H:SetVisible(C.Visible)
    return H
end
(function()
    local C = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local function isEValid(C)
        return(getgenv and (getgenv())[C]) or (_G and _G[C])
    end
    local g = isEValid("filtergc")
    local m = isEValid("hookfunction")
    local E = isEValid("newcclosure")
    local M = debug or {}
    local A, F = M.getconstants, M.getprotos
    local u, q = M.getupvalues, M.setupvalue
    if not(g and (m and (E and (A and (F and (u and q))))))then
        o.acStatus = "Executor sem filtergc/hookfunction/debug.* \226\128\148 AC intacto."
        return
    end
    local P = {}
    local function secondaryZ(C, isEValid, g)
        g = g or 0
        if g > 8 then
            return false
        end
        local m, E = pcall(A, C)
        if m and type(E) == "table" then
            for C, g in next, E do
                if type(g) == "string" and isEValid(g)then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        local M, H = pcall(F, C)
        if M and type(H) == "table" then
            for C, m in next, H do
                if secondaryZ(m, isEValid, g + 1)then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function alternateZ(C)
        return secondaryZ(C, function(C)
            return string.find(C, "__ACK__", 1, true) ~= nil
        end)
    end
    local f = {"Hitbox Expander", "ESP Detectado"}
    local function isYValid(C)
        local isEValid, g = false, true
        secondaryZ(C, function(C)
            if#C < 4 or not string.find(C, " ", 1, true)then
                return false
            end
            isEValid = true
            for isEValid, g in ipairs(f)do
                if string.find(C, g, 1, true)then
                    return false
                end
            end
            g = false
            return true
        end)
        return isEValid and g
    end
    local V = {
        "CFrame TP: ",
        "Tween TP: ",
        "TP Absurdo",
        "Velocity",
        "VFly",
        "WalkFling IY",
        "Noclip",
        "WalkSpeed: ",
        "JumpPower: ",
        "Fly mal feito",
    }
    local handleM = {FireServer = function()end}
    local updateInstancePropertiesPosition = {
        WaitForChild = function()
            return handleM
        end,
        FindFirstChild = function()
            return handleM
        end,
    }
    local O = {"CFrame TP: ", "TP Absurdo", "VFly"}
    local function handleY(C)
        local isEValid, g = pcall(A, C)
        if not isEValid or type(g) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        for C, isEValid in next, g do
            if type(isEValid) == "string" then
                for C, g in ipairs(O)do
                    if isEValid == g then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function handleG()
        local C, isEValid = pcall(g, "function", {Constants = {"VFly"} , IgnoreExecutor = true} )
        if not C or type(isEValid) ~= "table" then
            return nil
        end
        return isEValid
    end
    local function handler(isEValid)
        local g, m = pcall(u, isEValid)
        if not g or type(m) ~= "table" then
            return true
        end
        for isEValid, g in next, m do
            if g == C then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function secondaryHandleG(C)
        local isEValid = H.Character
        local g = isEValid and isEValid:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not g then
            return false
        end
        local m, E = pcall(u, C)
        if not m or type(E) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        for C, isEValid in next, E do
            if isEValid == g then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isValid(isEValid)
        local g, m = pcall(u, isEValid)
        if not g or type(m) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        for g, m in next, m do
            if m == C then
                return(pcall(q, isEValid, g, updateInstancePropertiesPosition)) and true or false
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    local e = isEValid("getconnections")
    local function handleM(C, isEValid)
        isEValid = isEValid or 0
        if isEValid > 6 then
            return nil
        end
        local g, m = pcall(A, C)
        if g and type(m) == "table" then
            for C, isEValid in next, m do
                if type(isEValid) == "string" then
                    for C, g in ipairs(V)do
                        if isEValid == g then
                            return g
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local E, M = pcall(F, C)
        if E and type(M) == "table" then
            for C, g in next, M do
                local m = handleM(g, isEValid + 1)
                if m then
                    return m
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function alternateHandleG()
        local C = {}
        local isEValid = H.Character
        local g = isEValid and isEValid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if g then
            C[#C + 1] = g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed")
            C[#C + 1] = g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower")
            C[#C + 1] = g.StateChanged
        end
        if isEValid then
            C[#C + 1] = isEValid.DescendantAdded
            for isEValid, g in ipairs(isEValid:GetChildren())do
                if g:IsA("BasePart")then
                    C[#C + 1] = g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide")
                end
            end
        end
        C[#C + 1] = (game:GetService("RunService")).Heartbeat
        return C
    end
    local function handleAcReaplicar()
        if not e then
            return nil
        end
        local C, isEValid = {} , {}
        for g, m in ipairs(alternateHandleG())do
            local E, M = pcall(e, m)
            if E and type(M) == "table" then
                for g, m in ipairs(M)do
                    local E = m.Function
                    if type(E) == "function" and m.Connected then
                        local g = handleM(E)
                        if g and not C[g]then
                            C[g] = true
                            isEValid[#isEValid + 1] = g
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local g = handleG()
        if g == nil then
            return nil
        end
        local m, E = false, false
        for C, isEValid in next, g do
            if secondaryHandleG(isEValid)then
                if handler(isEValid)then
                    E = true
                else
                    m = true
                end
            end
        end
        local M = E or not m
        if M and not C["CFrame TP: "]then
            C["CFrame TP: "] = true
            isEValid[#isEValid + 1] = "CFrame TP: "
        end
        return isEValid
    end
    local function secondaryHandleY()
        if not C:FindFirstChild("SignalSendACK")then
            return 0
        end
        local isEValid, M =
        pcall(g, "function", {Constants = {"SignalSendACK", "FireServer"} , IgnoreExecutor = true} )
        if not isEValid or type(M) ~= "table" then
            return 0
        end
        local H, A = 0, 0
        for C, isEValid in next, M do
            if not P[isEValid]then
                -- So silencia funcoes que citam motivos conhecidos de AC (TP/VFly/WalkSpeed).
                -- NUNCA troca FireServer generico por funcao vazia: isso quebra tiro/armas.
                if handleY(isEValid)then
                    if isValid(isEValid)then
                        P[isEValid] = true
                        H = H + 1
                    end
                else
                    P[isEValid] = true
                end
                A = A + 1
                if A >= 200 then
                    A = 0
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
        end
        return H
    end
    local b = false
    local function acReaplicar(C)
        if b or o.destroyed then
            return
        end
        b = true
        o.acSilenciados = 0
        o.acMotivosVivos = nil
        o.acStatus = "Aplicando (" .. (C .. ")...")
        task.spawn(function()
            local isEValid, g = 0, nil
            for C = 1, 120, 1 do
                if o.destroyed then
                    break
                end
                isEValid = isEValid + secondaryHandleY()
                g = handleAcReaplicar()
                if g == nil then
                    break
                end
                if#g == 0 then
                    break
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
            b = false
            o.acSilenciados = isEValid
            o.acMotivosVivos = g
            if g == nil then
                o.acStatus = isEValid
                .. (
                " silenciado(s) ("
                .. (C .. "), mas sem getconnections nao da pra provar \226\128\148 travas ligadas.")
            )
        elseif#g > 0 then
            o.acStatus = "AC PARCIAL ("
            .. (
            C
            .. (
            "): "
            .. (
            isEValid
            .. (
            " silenciado(s), mas ainda respondem: "
            .. (table.concat(g, ", ") .. ". As travas de velocidade continuam ligadas.")
        )
    )
)
)
elseif isEValid > 0 then
    o.acStatus = isEValid
    .. (" denunciante(s) silenciado(s) (" .. (C .. "); nenhum motivo critico responde."))
else
    o.acStatus = "Nenhum denunciante encontrado (" .. (C .. ") \226\128\148 AC pode estar intacto.")
end
end)
end
o.acReaplicar = acReaplicar
acReaplicar("carga")
handleReady(H.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    acReaplicar("respawn")
end))
if game:FindService("VirtualUser") or game:FindService("VirtualInputManager")then
    o.acAviso =
    'VirtualUser/VirtualInputManager existe neste cliente: o report "Auto-farming" sai pela closure que carrega o heartbeat e NAO e silenciado.'
end
end)()
do
    local function applyWorld()
        local applyWorld = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local Y = T.alwaysDay or T.fullbright or T.noShadow or T.noFog or T.noBlur
        if not o.worldOriginal and Y then
            o.worldOriginal = {
                Brightness = applyWorld.Brightness,
                ClockTime = applyWorld.ClockTime,
                FogEnd = applyWorld.FogEnd,
                FogStart = applyWorld.FogStart,
                GlobalShadows = applyWorld.GlobalShadows,
                Ambient = applyWorld.Ambient,
                OutdoorAmbient = applyWorld.OutdoorAmbient,
                ExposureCompensation = applyWorld.ExposureCompensation,
            }
        end
        local g = o.worldOriginal
        if not Y then
            if g then
                for Y, g in pairs(g)do
                    pcall(function()
                        applyWorld[Y] = g
                    end)
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if T.alwaysDay or T.fullbright then
            applyWorld.ClockTime = 14
        elseif g then
            applyWorld.ClockTime = g.ClockTime
        end
        if T.fullbright then
            applyWorld.Brightness = 2
            applyWorld.ExposureCompensation = 0
            applyWorld.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            applyWorld.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        elseif g then
            applyWorld.Brightness = g.Brightness
            applyWorld.ExposureCompensation = g.ExposureCompensation
            applyWorld.Ambient = g.Ambient
            applyWorld.OutdoorAmbient = g.OutdoorAmbient
        end
        if T.noShadow or T.fullbright then
            applyWorld.GlobalShadows = false
        elseif g then
            applyWorld.GlobalShadows = g.GlobalShadows
        end
        if T.noFog or T.fullbright then
            applyWorld.FogStart = 0
            applyWorld.FogEnd = 1000000000
            local Y = applyWorld:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
            if Y then
                Y.Density = 0
            end
        elseif g then
            applyWorld.FogStart = g.FogStart
            applyWorld.FogEnd = g.FogEnd
        end
        if T.noBlur then
            for applyWorld, Y in ipairs(applyWorld:GetDescendants())do
                if Y:IsA("BlurEffect")then
                    Y.Enabled = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
    o.applyWorld = applyWorld
    local g = 0
    handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(function(Y)
        if T.alwaysDay or T.fullbright then
            pcall(function()
                (game:GetService("Lighting")).ClockTime = 14
            end)
        end
        g = g + Y
        if g < 0.25 then
            return
        end
        g = 0
        if T.alwaysDay or T.fullbright or T.noShadow or T.noFog or T.noBlur then
            pcall(applyWorld)
        end
    end))
end
do
    local C = 0
    local function updateInstanceProperties()
        local C = H:FindFirstChild("Status")
        local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Cinto")
        if not Y or Y.Value then
            return
        end
        local updateInstanceProperties = H.Character
        local m = updateInstanceProperties and updateInstanceProperties:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local E = m and m.SeatPart
        if not E or E.Name ~= "DriveSeat" then
            return
        end
        Y.Value = true
        pcall(function()
            H.PlayerGui.OrpheusCarInterface.Velocimetro.Icons.Cinto.Visible = true
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local C = E:FindFirstChild("ColocaCinto")
            if C then
                C:Play()
            end
        end)
    end
    handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(function(Y)
        if not T.autoCintoEnabled then
            return
        end
        C = C + Y
        if C < 0.3 then
            return
        end
        C = 0
        pcall(updateInstanceProperties)
    end))
end
local function isCValid()
    local C = H.Character
    return C and C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or nil
end
local function isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    local C = H.Character
    return C and C:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local U = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
task.spawn(function()
    while not o.destroyed do
        local C = isCValid()
        if C then
            U = C.MoveDirection
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end)
local L = 28
local function isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()
    local C = o.acMotivosVivos
    return(o.acSilenciados or 0) > 0 and (C ~= nil and #C == 0)
end
local function updateInstanceProperties(C)
    if not C then
        return
    end
    if T.spoofWalkEnabled then
        local Y = math.min(T.spoofWalkSpeed, L)
        if C.WalkSpeed ~= Y then
            C.WalkSpeed = Y
        end
    end
    if T.spoofJumpEnabled then
        if C.JumpPower ~= 50 then
            C.JumpPower = 50
        end
        if not C.UseJumpPower then
            C.UseJumpPower = true
        end
    end
end
local b = {
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = true,
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead] = true,
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing] = true,
}
local y = 0.5
local x = 18
local c = 34
local l = 36
local Z = 0.08
local D = 16
local function handleG(C, Y)
    local g = 0
    for m = #C, 1, - 1 do
        if Y - C[m].t > y then
            table.remove(C, m)
        else
            g = g + C[m].passo
        end
    end
    return g
end
local function handleInstanceProperties(C, Y, g, m)
    if not g then
        return
    end
    local E = ((m - g)).Magnitude
    if E > 0 then
        table.insert(C, {t = Y, passo = E} )
    end
end
local j = {root = nil, posicao = nil, ultimaAmostra = 0, historico = {} }
local function updateG(C, Y)
    local g = j
    if g.root ~= C or Y - g.ultimaAmostra > y then
        g.root = C
        g.posicao = C.Position
        g.ultimaAmostra = Y
        table.clear(g.historico)
        return 0
    end
    handleInstanceProperties(g.historico, Y, g.posicao, C.Position)
    g.posicao = C.Position
    g.ultimaAmostra = Y
    return handleG(g.historico, Y)
end
local function isGhValid(C, Y)
    local g = isCValid()
    if C.Anchored or not g or g.Health <= 0 or Y >= Z then
        return true
    end
    local m = g:GetState()
    return C.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 12
    and (m ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and m ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
end
local function handleQ(C, Y, g, m)
    local E = math.clamp(Y or 0.016666666666667, 0.0041666666666667, y)
    local M = updateG(C, os.clock())
    local H = not m or isGhValid(C, E)
    local A = m and (H and M > x)
    local F = m and (H and x or c) or x
    if A then
        F = c
    end
    local u = math.max(0, F - M)
    local q = math.min(math.max(g or 0, 0), u / E)
    local P = math.max(0, u - q * E)
    if A then
        P = 0
    end
    return E, q, P, M, H
end
local w = 4
local a = 8
local i = 105
local d = 65
local G = 0.05
local t = 0.6
local K = 14
local Cz = 20
local Yz = 14
local gz = 0.55
local mz = Vector3.zero
local Ez = 0
local Mz = 0
local Hz = 4
local Az = 0
local function Fz(C)
    if not T.spoofWalkEnabled then
        Az = 0
        return
    end
    if T.flyEnabled or T.farmPartesEnabled then
        Az = 0
        return
    end
    local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    if not Y then
        return
    end
    local g = isCValid()
    if not g then
        return
    end
    if g.Sit or b[g:GetState()]then
        Az = 0
        return
    end
    if U.Magnitude <= 0 then
        Az = 0
        if os.clock() < Ez and mz.Magnitude > 0 then
            local C = math.max(g.WalkSpeed, 1)
            local Y = math.clamp(Yz / C, 0, 1)
            pcall(g.Move, g, mz * Y, false)
        end
        return
    end
    mz = U.Unit
    Ez = os.clock() + gz
    local m = Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local E = (Vector3.new(m.X, 0, m.Z)).Magnitude
    if(C or 0) >= G or Y.Anchored or E < 12 then
        Mz = os.clock() + t
    end
    local M = isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()
    local H = math.min(T.spoofWalkSpeed, M and T.spoofWalkSpeed or (os.clock() < Mz and d or i))
    local A = H - L
    if A <= 0 then
        Az = 0
        return
    end
    local F = 0.9 + math.random() * 0.1
    local u = (A * C) * F + Az
    local q = math.min(u, A * C, M and a or w)
    Az = math.clamp(u - q, 0, Hz)
    local P = U.Unit
    Y.CFrame = Y.CFrame + P * q
    local z = Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    if(Vector3.new(z.X, 0, z.Z)).Magnitude < K then
        Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(P.X * Cz, z.Y, P.Z * Cz)
    end
end
local function uz()
    if not T.spoofJumpEnabled then
        return
    end
    local C = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    if not C then
        return
    end
    local Y = T.spoofJumpPower - 50
    if Y > 0 then
        C.Velocity = C.Velocity + Vector3.new(0, Y, 0)
    end
end
local qz = {
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = true,
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = true,
    [Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding] = true,
}
local Pz = {recovering = false}
local function zz()
    local C = H.Character
    return C and C:FindFirstChild("IsRagdoll")
end
local function oz()
    local C = H.Character
    return C and C:FindFirstChild("RagdollClient")
end
local function Tz(C, Y)
    if not C then
        return
    end
    C:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, not Y)
    C:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, not Y)
end
local function fz(C)
    if not C or C.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    if not C:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)then
        C:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
    end
    if C.PlatformStand then
        C.PlatformStand = false
    end
    local Y = zz()
    if Y and Y.Value then
        Y.Value = false
    end
    if qz[C:GetState()]then
        C:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
    end
end
local function Rz()
    if Pz.recovering then
        return
    end
    Pz.recovering = true
    task.delay(T.antiRagdollDelay / 1000, function()
        Pz.recovering = false
        if not T.antiRagdollEnabled or o.destroyed then
            return
        end
        pcall(fz, isCValid())
    end)
end
local Qz
do
    local C = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("InGameRemotes")
    Qz = C and C:FindFirstChild("RagdollService")
end
local function Vz()
    if not(getconnections and (Qz and (debug and debug.getupvalues)))then
        return {}
    end
    local C = {}
    local g, m = pcall(getconnections, Y.Heartbeat)
    if not g then
        return {}
    end
    for Y, g in ipairs(m)do
        local m, E = pcall(function()
            return g.Function
        end)
        if m and type(E) == "function" then
            local Y, m = pcall(debug.getupvalues, E)
            if Y and type(m) == "table" then
                for Y, m in pairs(m)do
                    if m == Qz then
                        table.insert(C, g)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return C
end
local function pz()
    local C = T.antiRagdollEnabled
    for Y, g in ipairs(Vz())do
        pcall(function()
            if C then
                g:Disable()
            else
                g:Enable()
            end
        end)
    end
    local Y = oz()
    if Y then
        Y.Disabled = C
    end
    local g = isCValid()
    if not g then
        return
    end
    Tz(g, C and T.antiRagdollDelay <= 0)
    if C then
        fz(g)
    else
        g:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
    end
end
local function Iz()
    local C = isCValid()
    if not C or C.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    if C.SeatPart or H:GetAttribute("QuedaLivre") == true then
        return
    end
    if not C:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)then
        C:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
    end
    local Y = T.antiRagdollDelay <= 0
    if Y then
        Tz(C, true)
    end
    local g = zz()
    local m = C.PlatformStand or qz[C:GetState()] ~= nil or (g ~= nil and g.Value == true)
    if not m then
        return
    end
    if Y then
        fz(C)
    else
        Rz()
    end
end
local Oz = {"Head", "Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg", "HumanoidRootPart"}
local Xz = {
    Head = true,
    Torso = true,
    ["Left Arm"] = false,
    ["Right Arm"] = false,
    ["Left Leg"] = false,
    ["Right Leg"] = false,
    HumanoidRootPart = false,
}
local Bz = nil
local function sz(C)
    local Y = H.Character
    if not Y then
        return
    end
    for g, m in ipairs(Oz)do
        local E = Y:FindFirstChild(m)
        if E and E:IsA("BasePart")then
            local Y = not C and Xz[m] or false
            if E.CanCollide ~= Y then
                E.CanCollide = Y
            end
        end
    end
end
local function Nz()
    local C = H.Character
    if not C then
        return
    end
    local Y = C:FindFirstChild("IsRagdoll")
    local g = C:FindFirstChild("RagdollClient")
    if T.noclipEnabled then
        if g then
            g.Disabled = true
        end
        if Y and not Y.Value then
            Y.Value = true
        end
        sz(true)
    else
        Bz = nil
        sz(false)
        if Y and Y.Value then
            Y.Value = false
        end
        if g and not T.antiRagdollEnabled then
            g.Disabled = false
        end
    end
end
local Sz = 50
local function ez()
    local C = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    if not C then
        return
    end
    if not Bz then
        Bz = C.Position.Y
        return
    end
    local Y = C.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local m = C.Position.Y <= Bz + 0.5
    local E = isCValid()
    local M = (E and E.Jump) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)
    if m and M then
        C.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(Y.X, Sz, Y.Z)
        return
    end
    if Y.Y < 0 and C.Position.Y <= Bz then
        C.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(Y.X, 0, Y.Z)
    end
end
local Uz = 60
local Lz = 30
local kz = 14
local nz = Uz
local bz = Vector3.zero
local yz = false
local function xz(C)
    local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    local m = isCValid()
    if not Y or not m then
        return
    end
    if not T.flyEnabled then
        m:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming, true)
        m:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
        if m:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
            local C = m.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air and Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
            or Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running
            pcall(m.ChangeState, m, C)
        end
        if T.farmPartesEnabled then
            yz = false
            return
        end
        if isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()then
            yz = false
            Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return
        end
        local g, E, M, H = handleQ(Y, C, kz, true)
        if yz and (H > D and bz.Magnitude > 0)then
            Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = bz * E
            return
        end
        yz = false
        Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        return
    end
    if m.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    if T.farmPartesEnabled then
        o.farmPartesRunId = o.farmPartesRunId + 1
        T.farmPartesEnabled = false
    end
    m:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
    m:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming)
    local E = 0
    if g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)then
        E = 1
    elseif g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)then
        E = - 1
    end
    local M = m.MoveDirection + Vector3.new(0, E, 0)
    if M.Magnitude <= 0 then
        if isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()then
            Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return
        end
        local g, m, E, M = handleQ(Y, C, kz, true)
        if M > D and bz.Magnitude > 0 then
            Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = bz * m
        else
            Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        return
    end
    M = M.Unit
    bz = M
    yz = true
    if isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()then
        Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = M * T.flySpeed
        return
    end
    local H = m.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 and Uz or nz
    local A = math.min(T.flySpeed, H)
    local F = math.min(A, Lz)
    local u, q, P, z, f = handleQ(Y, C, F, true)
    local handleIz = math.min(A, f and l or H)
    local handleReady = math.max(0, handleIz - q)
    if handleReady > 0 and P > 0 then
        Y.CFrame = Y.CFrame + M * math.min(handleReady * u, P)
    end
    Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = M * q
end
local cz = {track = nil}
local function lz()
    local C = H.Character
    local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("mainClient")
    if not Y then
        return nil
    end
    return Y:FindFirstChild("QuedaAnim") or Y:FindFirstChild("QuedaAnim2")
end
local function Zz()
    if cz.track then
        pcall(function()
            cz.track:Stop(0.2)
        end)
        cz.track = nil
    end
    if not T.rolarEnabled then
        return
    end
    local C = isCValid()
    local Y = C and C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    local g = lz()
    if not Y or not g then
        return
    end
    local m, E = pcall(function()
        return Y:LoadAnimation(g)
    end)
    if not m or not E then
        return
    end
    cz.track = E
    E.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
    E.Looped = true
    E:Play(0.15)
end
local function Dz(C)
    if type(getconnections) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end
    if type(debug) ~= "table" or type(debug.getupvalue) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end
    for Y, g in ipairs(getconnections(Y.Stepped))do
        local m = g.Function
        if type(m) == "function" then
            local Y, g
            for E = 1, 8, 1 do
                local M, H = pcall(debug.getupvalue, m, E)
                if not M then
                    break
                end
                if H == C then
                    Y = true
                end
                if type(H) == "table" and (H.RemapMultiplier ~= nil and type(H.BuildHPCache) == "function")then
                    g = H
                end
            end
            if Y and g then
                return g
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function rz()
    local C = isCValid()
    local Y = C and C.SeatPart
    if not Y or Y.Name ~= "DriveSeat" then
        return nil
    end
    local g = M:FindFirstChild("CarrosSpawnados")
    if not g then
        return nil
    end
    local m = Y
    while m and m.Parent ~= g do
        m = m.Parent
    end
    return m
end
do
    local C = {
        carro = nil,
        originais = {} ,
        raiz = nil,
        assento = nil,
        relativoAssento = nil,
        altura = nil,
        piso = nil,
        raycast = nil,
        velocidade = 0,
        frente = nil,
        personagemAtivo = false,
        altitude = nil,
        vFlyAtivo = false,
        vFlyReduzidoAte = 0,
    }
    local function handleG(C)
        local Y = C:FindFirstChild("DriveSeat", true) or C:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
        return(Y and Y.AssemblyRootPart) or C.PrimaryPart or Y, Y
    end
    local function restaurarNoclipCar()
        for C, Y in pairs(C.originais)do
            if C.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    C.CanCollide = Y
                end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(C.originais)
        if C.personagemAtivo and not T.noclipEnabled then
            pcall(sz, false)
        end
        C.carro = nil
        C.raiz = nil
        C.assento = nil
        C.relativoAssento = nil
        C.altura = nil
        C.piso = nil
        C.raycast = nil
        C.velocidade = 0
        C.frente = nil
        C.personagemAtivo = false
        C.altitude = nil
        C.vFlyAtivo = false
        C.vFlyReduzidoAte = 0
    end
    local function updateAplicarNoclipCar(Y)
        local g, restaurarNoclipCar = handleG(Y)
        if not g or not restaurarNoclipCar then
            return
        end
        local updateAplicarNoclipCar = RaycastParams.new()
        updateAplicarNoclipCar.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        updateAplicarNoclipCar.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Y, H.Character}
        local F = M:Raycast(g.Position + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), Vector3.new(0, - 120, 0), updateAplicarNoclipCar)
        C.raiz = g
        C.assento = restaurarNoclipCar
        C.relativoAssento = ((g.CFrame - g.Position)):ToObjectSpace(
        restaurarNoclipCar.CFrame - restaurarNoclipCar.Position
    )
    C.raycast = updateAplicarNoclipCar
    C.piso = F and F.Position.Y or (g.Position.Y - 3)
    C.altura = F and math.max(1, g.Position.Y - F.Position.Y) or 3
    local u = Vector3.new(restaurarNoclipCar.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, restaurarNoclipCar.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
    C.frente = u.Magnitude > 0 and u.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, - 1)
    C.altitude = g.Position.Y
    C.velocidade = u.Magnitude > 0 and g.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Dot(u.Unit) or 0
end
local function max(C, Y, g)
    if C < Y then
        return math.min(C + g, Y)
    end
    return math.max(C - g, Y)
end
local function updateInstanceProperties(Y)
    local handleG, restaurarNoclipCar = C.raiz, C.assento
