    local H, updateAplicarNoclipCar, updateInstanceProperties = C.raycast, C.altura, C.piso
    local q = C.relativoAssento
    if
    not handleG
    or not handleG.Parent
    or not restaurarNoclipCar
    or not restaurarNoclipCar.Parent
    or not H
    or not updateAplicarNoclipCar
    or not updateInstanceProperties
    or not q
    then
        return
    end
    local P = math.min(Y or 0.016666666666667, 0.1)
    local z = T.vFlyEnabled
    local o = false
    if z and not C.vFlyAtivo then
        C.altitude = handleG.Position.Y
        C.vFlyReduzidoAte = os.clock() + 0.6
        C.velocidade = math.clamp(C.velocidade, - 24, 36)
    elseif not z and C.vFlyAtivo then
        local Y = M:Raycast(handleG.Position + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), Vector3.new(0, - 520, 0), H)
        if Y then
            updateInstanceProperties = Y.Position.Y
            C.piso = updateInstanceProperties
        end
    end
    C.vFlyAtivo = z
    local f = 0
    if z then
        if P >= 0.05 or handleG.Anchored or handleG.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 12 then
            C.vFlyReduzidoAte = os.clock() + 0.6
        end
    else
        local Y = Vector3.new(handleG.Position.X, updateInstanceProperties + 20, handleG.Position.Z)
        local g = M:Raycast(Y, Vector3.new(0, - 60, 0), H)
        if g and g.Position.Y - updateInstanceProperties <= 4 then
            updateInstanceProperties = g.Position.Y
            C.piso = updateInstanceProperties
        end
        f = (updateInstanceProperties + updateAplicarNoclipCar) - handleG.Position.Y
        o = math.abs(f) > 1
        if o then
            f = math.clamp(f, - 20 * P, 20 * P)
        end
    end
    local handleIz = C.frente or Vector3.new(0, 0, - 1)
    local handleReady = 0
    if handleIz.Magnitude > 0.001 then
        handleIz = handleIz.Unit
        local Y = restaurarNoclipCar.ThrottleFloat
        local M = restaurarNoclipCar.SteerFloat
        if g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Up)then
            Y = 1
        elseif g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Down)then
            Y = - 1
        end
        if g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Left)then
            M = - 1
        elseif g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Right)then
            M = 1
        end
        local H = isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()
        local updateAplicarNoclipCar = z and (not H and os.clock() < C.vFlyReduzidoAte)
        local updateInstanceProperties = math.clamp(T.boostFactor, 1, 2)
        local V = z
        and (H and T.vFlyHorizontalSpeed or math.min(
        T.vFlyHorizontalSpeed,
        updateAplicarNoclipCar and 36 or 60
    ))
    or (o and 60 or 80 * updateInstanceProperties)
    local handleM = z and V * 0.65 or (o and 40 or 45 * updateInstanceProperties)
    local updateInstancePropertiesPosition = Y > 0.05 and V or (Y <- 0.05 and- handleM or 0)
    local O = (math.abs(Y) > 0.05 and 75 or 110) * P
    C.velocidade = max(C.velocidade, updateInstancePropertiesPosition, O)
    if math.abs(M) > 0.05 and math.abs(C.velocidade) > 0.5 then
        local Y = C.velocidade >= 0 and 1 or- 1
        local g = (( - M * Y) * math.rad(85)) * P
        handleIz = (CFrame.Angles(0, g, 0)):VectorToWorldSpace(handleIz)
    end
    C.frente = handleIz.Unit
    if z then
        local Y = 0
        if g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E)then
            Y = 1
        elseif g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or g:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q)then
            Y = - 1
        end
        local restaurarNoclipCar = H and T.vFlyVerticalSpeed
        or math.min(T.vFlyVerticalSpeed, updateAplicarNoclipCar and 20 or 30)
        if Y ~= 0 then
            handleReady = Y * restaurarNoclipCar
            C.altitude = handleG.Position.Y + handleReady * P
        else
            local Y = (C.altitude or handleG.Position.Y) - handleG.Position.Y
            handleReady = math.clamp(Y * 10, - restaurarNoclipCar, restaurarNoclipCar)
        end
    end
    local X = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, handleIz.Unit, Vector3.yAxis)
    local B = X * q:Inverse()
    handleG.CFrame = CFrame.new(handleG.Position + Vector3.new(0, f, 0)) * B
elseif math.abs(f) > 0.01 then
    handleG.CFrame = handleG.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, f, 0)
end
if handleIz.Magnitude > 0.001 then
    handleG.AssemblyLinearVelocity = handleIz.Unit * C.velocidade + Vector3.new(0, handleReady, 0)
else
    handleG.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, handleReady, 0)
end
handleG.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
local function aplicarNoclipCar()
    if not T.noclipCarEnabled and not T.vFlyEnabled then
        restaurarNoclipCar()
        return
    end
    local Y = rz()
    if Y ~= C.carro then
        restaurarNoclipCar()
        C.carro = Y
        if Y then
            updateAplicarNoclipCar(Y)
        end
    end
    if not Y then
        return
    end
    sz(true)
    C.personagemAtivo = true
    for Y, g in ipairs(Y:GetDescendants())do
        if g:IsA("BasePart")then
            if C.originais[g] == nil then
                C.originais[g] = g.CanCollide
            end
            if g.CanCollide then
                g.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end
end
o.aplicarNoclipCar = aplicarNoclipCar
o.restaurarNoclipCar = restaurarNoclipCar
handleReady(Y.Stepped:Connect(function()
    if not o.destroyed then
        pcall(aplicarNoclipCar)
    end
end))
handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(function(C)
    if not o.destroyed and (T.noclipCarEnabled or T.vFlyEnabled)then
        pcall(updateInstanceProperties, C)
    end
end))
end
local function hz(C)
    local Y = Dz(C)
    if not Y then
        return false
    end
    Y.RemapMultiplier = T.boostFactor
    pcall(function()
        Y:BuildHPCache()
    end)
    return true
end
o.state = T
A.__KNWARE_SINTONIA_RUNTIME = o
local function jz(C)
    local Y = isCValid()
    local g = Y and Y.Parent
    if not g then
        return
    end
    for Y, g in ipairs(g:GetChildren())do
        if g:IsA("BasePart")then
            g.CanQuery = not C
        end
    end
end
local function Wz(C)
    if o.destroyed then
        return
    end
    local Y = isCValid()
    if Y then
        updateInstanceProperties(Y)
    end
    pcall(Fz, C)
    if T.antiRagdollEnabled then
        pcall(Iz)
    end
    if T.antiTiroEnabled and Y then
        local C = Y.Parent
        if C then
            for C, Y in ipairs(C:GetChildren())do
                if Y:IsA("BasePart") and Y.CanQuery then
                    Y.CanQuery = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if T.noclipEnabled then
        pcall(Nz)
        if not T.flyEnabled and not T.farmPartesEnabled then
            pcall(ez)
        end
    end
    if T.flyEnabled or yz then
        pcall(xz, C)
    end
end
handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(Wz))
handleReady(Y.Stepped:Connect(function()
    if not o.destroyed and T.noclipEnabled then
        pcall(sz, true)
    end
end))
task.spawn(function()
    local C
    while not o.destroyed do
        local Y = rz()
        if Y then
            o.lastDrivenCar = Y
        end
        if T.boostFactor > 1.001 then
            if Y and Y ~= C then
                if hz(Y)then
                    C = Y
                end
            elseif not Y then
                C = nil
            end
        else
            C = nil
        end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)
local function vz(C)
    task.wait(0.5)
    local Y = C:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if Y then
        updateInstanceProperties(Y)
        Y.Jumping:Connect(uz)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        C:WaitForChild("RagdollClient", 5)
        if o.destroyed then
            return
        end
        if T.antiRagdollEnabled then
            pcall(pz)
        end
        if T.rolarEnabled then
            pcall(Zz)
        end
    end)
end
handleReady(H.CharacterAdded:Connect(vz))
if H.Character then
    vz(H.Character)
end
local Jz = {}
do
    local Y = game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local g = 254
    local m = Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 120)
    local E = Color3.fromRGB(60, 210, 255)
    local M = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    local F = {
        "admin",
        "staff",
        "moder",
        "owner",
        "founder",
        "creator",
        "developer",
        "dono",
        "fundador",
        "gerente",
        "supervis",
        "diretor",
        "gestor",
    }
    local function handleF(C)
        local Y = string.lower(tostring(C or ""))
        for C, g in ipairs(F)do
            if string.find(Y, g, 1, true)then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isFValid(C)
        local Y = typeof(C)
        if Y == "boolean" then
            return C
        end
        if Y == "number" then
            return C > 0
        end
        if Y == "string" then
            local Y = string.lower(C)
            return Y ~= "" and (Y ~= "false" and (Y ~= "0" and (Y ~= "none" and (Y ~= "guest" and Y ~= "member"))))
        end
        return C ~= nil
    end
    local function isValid(C)
        local Y = A.KNWARE_SINTONIA_STAFF_USER_IDS
        if type(Y) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        return Y[C.UserId] == true or Y[tostring(C.UserId)] == true or table.find(Y, C.UserId) ~= nil
    end
    local function handleInstanceProperties(C)
        if isValid(C)then
            return true, "configured"
        end
        if game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.User and C.UserId == game.CreatorId then
            return true, "creator"
        end
        local g, m = pcall(function()
            return C:GetAttributes()
        end)
        if g then
            for C, Y in pairs(m)do
                if handleF(C) and isFValid(Y)then
                    return true, "attribute:" .. C
                end
            end
        end
        local E, M = pcall(Y.GetTags, Y, C)
        if E then
            for C, Y in ipairs(M)do
                if handleF(Y)then
                    return true, "tag:" .. Y
                end
            end
        end
        return false, nil
    end
    local f = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        local Y = o.relationships[C]
        if Y then
            return Y
        end
        Y = {ready = false, checking = false, friend = false, admin = false, rank = 0, role = "", source = nil}
        o.relationships[C] = Y
        return Y
    end)
    local function updateReady(Y)
        if Y == H or Y.Parent ~= C then
            return
        end
        local m = f(Y)
        if m.checking then
            return
        end
        m.checking = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local E = false
            local M, A = pcall(function()
                return H:IsFriendsWith(Y.UserId)
            end)
            if M then
                E = A == true
            end
            local F, isFValid = handleInstanceProperties(Y)
            local isValid, T = 0, ""
            if game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group and game.CreatorId > 0 then
                local C, m = pcall(function()
                    return Y:GetRankInGroup(game.CreatorId)
                end)
                if C and type(m) == "number" then
                    isValid = m
                end
                local E, M = pcall(function()
                    return Y:GetRoleInGroup(game.CreatorId)
                end)
                if E and type(M) == "string" then
                    T = M
                end
                if isValid >= g then
                    F, isFValid = true, "group-rank:" .. tostring(isValid)
                elseif handleF(T)then
                    F, isFValid = true, "group-role:" .. T
                end
            end
            if Y.Parent == C and o.relationships[Y] == m then
                m.friend = E
                m.admin = F
                m.rank = isValid
                m.role = T
                m.source = isFValid
                m.ready = true
                m.checking = false
            end
        end)
    end
    local V = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        local Y = f(C)
        if not Y.ready and not Y.checking then
            updateReady(C)
        end
        return Y
    end)
    function Jz.Protected(C)
        local Y = V(C)
        if(T.friendCheck or T.adminCheck) and not Y.ready then
            return true
        end
        if T.adminCheck and Y.admin then
            return true
        end
        if T.friendCheck and Y.friend then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    Jz.Admin = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        local Y = V(C)
        return Y.ready and Y.admin
    end)
    Jz.Appearance = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        local Y = V(C)
        if Y.ready and (T.adminCheck and Y.admin)then
            return m, " [STAFF]"
        end
        if Y.ready and (T.friendCheck and Y.friend)then
            return E, " [AMIGO]"
        end
        return M, ""
    end)
    local function onPlayerAdded(C)
        if C == H then
            return
        end
        updateReady(C)
        local Y = C.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(Y)
            if handleF(Y)then
                local Y = o.relationships[C]
                if Y then
                    Y.ready = false
                end
                updateReady(C)
            end
        end)
        o.relationshipConnections[C] = Y
        handleReady(Y)
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(C:GetPlayers())do
        onPlayerAdded(Y)
    end
    handleReady(C.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayerAdded))
    handleReady(C.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(C)
        o.relationships[C] = nil
        o.relationshipConnections[C] = nil
    end))
end
local wz = {medido = false, drawing = false, texto = false}
function wz.set(C, Y, g)
    local m = pcall(function()
        C[Y] = g
    end)
    return m
end
function wz.medir()
    if wz.medido then
        return wz
    end
    if Drawing == nil or type(Drawing.new) ~= "function" then
        return wz
    end
    local C, Y = pcall(Drawing.new, "Square")
    if not C then
        return wz
    end
    wz.drawing = true
    wz.filled = wz.set(Y, "Filled", false)
    wz.thickness = wz.set(Y, "Thickness", 1)
    pcall(function()
        Y:Remove()
    end)
    local g, m = pcall(Drawing.new, "Text")
    if g then
        wz.texto = true
        wz.center = wz.set(m, "Center", true)
        wz.outline = wz.set(m, "Outline", true)
        wz.outlineColor = wz.set(m, "OutlineColor", Color3.new())
        wz.font = wz.set(m, "Font", 2)
        pcall(function()
            m.Text = "MMM"
        end)
        local C, Y = pcall(function()
            return m.TextBounds
        end)
        wz.textBounds = C and (typeof(Y) == "Vector2" and Y.X > 0)
        pcall(function()
            m:Remove()
        end)
    end
    local E, M = pcall(Drawing.new, "Triangle")
    wz.triangle = E == true
    if E then
        pcall(function()
            M:Remove()
        end)
    end
    wz.medido = true
    return wz
end
function wz.ok()
    return(wz.medir()).drawing
end
local function az(C)
    if not wz.ok()then
        return nil
    end
    if C == "Triangle" and not wz.triangle then
        return nil
    end
    local Y, g = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new(C)
    end)
    if not Y then
        return nil
    end
    wz.set(g, "Visible", false)
    wz.set(g, "Color", Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255))
    wz.set(g, "Transparency", 1)
    wz.set(g, "Thickness", 1)
    return g
end
local function iz(C)
    if not wz.ok() or not wz.texto then
        return nil
    end
    local Y, g = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new("Text")
    end)
    if not Y then
        return nil
    end
    wz.set(g, "Visible", false)
    wz.set(g, "Color", C or Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255))
    wz.set(g, "Transparency", 1)
    wz.set(g, "Size", 13)
    if wz.center then
        wz.set(g, "Center", true)
    end
    if wz.outline then
        wz.set(g, "Outline", true)
    end
    if wz.outlineColor then
        wz.set(g, "OutlineColor", Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
    end
    if wz.font then
        wz.set(g, "Font", 2)
    end
    return g
end
function wz.largura(C)
    if not C then
        return 0
    end
    if wz.textBounds then
        local Y, g = pcall(function()
            return C.TextBounds
        end)
        if Y and g then
            return g.X
        end
    end
    local Y, g = pcall(function()
        return C.Text
    end)
    if not Y or type(g) ~= "string" then
        return 0
    end
    local m, E = pcall(function()
        return C.Size
    end)
    return(#g * (m and E or 13)) * 0.52
end
local dz = {
    {"Head", "UpperTorso"} ,
    {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"} ,
    {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"} ,
    {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"} ,
    {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"} ,
    {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"} ,
    {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"} ,
    {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"} ,
    {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"} ,
    {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"} ,
    {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"} ,
    {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"} ,
    {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"} ,
    {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"} ,
}
local Gz = { {"Head", "Torso"} , {"Torso", "Left Arm"} , {"Torso", "Right Arm"} , {"Torso", "Left Leg"} , {
        "Torso",
        "Right Leg",
    } }
local tz = #dz
local Kz =
{ {1, 2} , {1, 3} , {1, 5} , {2, 4} , {2, 6} , {3, 4} , {3, 7} , {4, 8} , {5, 6} , {5, 7} , {6, 8} , {
        7,
        8,
    } }
local CF = 300
local YF = 0.016666666666667
local gF = 0.033333333333333
local mF = 0.05
local EF = 4
local MF = 5
local HF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    if C <= 0 or Y - C > g then
        return Y + g
    end
    return C + g
end)
local AF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    local Y = {
        player = C,
        box = az("Square"),
        name = iz(Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)),
        distance = iz(Color3.fromRGB(190, 200, 225)),
        item = iz(Color3.fromRGB(190, 200, 225)),
        team = iz(Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)),
        lines = {} ,
        tracer = nil,
        arrow = nil,
        box3dLines = {} ,
        highlight = nil,
        character = nil,
        humanoid = nil,
        root = nil,
        bodyParts = {} ,
        skeletonPairs = {} ,
        relationshipColor = nil,
        relationshipLabel = "",
        nextVisualUpdate = 0,
        nextSkeletonUpdate = 0,
    }
    if Y.box and wz.filled then
        Y.box.Filled = false
    end
    for C = 1, tz, 1 do
        table.insert(Y.lines, az("Line"))
    end
    T.bundles[C] = Y
    return Y
end)
local FF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C.box then
        C.box.Visible = false
    end
    if C.name then
        C.name.Visible = false
    end
    if C.distance then
        C.distance.Visible = false
    end
    if C.item then
        C.item.Visible = false
    end
    if C.team then
        C.team.Visible = false
    end
    if C.tracer then
        C.tracer.Visible = false
    end
    if C.arrow then
        C.arrow.Visible = false
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(C.lines)do
        Y.Visible = false
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(C.box3dLines)do
        Y.Visible = false
    end
end)
local uF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    FF(C)
    if C.highlight then
        C.highlight.Enabled = false
    end
end)
local qF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if not C then
        return
    end
    local function handler(C)
        if C then
            pcall(function()
                C:Remove()
            end)
        end
    end
    handler(C.box)
    handler(C.name)
    handler(C.distance)
    handler(C.item)
    handler(C.team)
    handler(C.tracer)
    handler(C.arrow)
    for C, g in ipairs(C.lines)do
        handler(g)
    end
    for C, g in ipairs(C.box3dLines)do
        handler(g)
    end
    if C.highlight then
        pcall(function()
            C.highlight:Destroy()
        end)
        C.highlight = nil
    end
end)
local PF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    for C, Y in pairs(T.bundles)do
        uF(Y)
    end
end)
local zF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    local g, m = C:WorldToViewportPoint(Y)
    return Vector2.new(g.X, g.Y), m, g.Z
end)
local oF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C.tracer then
        return C.tracer
    end
    C.tracer = az("Line")
    if C.tracer then
        if wz.thickness then
            C.tracer.Thickness = 1.5
        end
        C.tracer.Color = C.relationshipColor or Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    end
    return C.tracer
end)
local TF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if C.arrow then
        return C.arrow
    end
    C.arrow = az("Triangle")
    if C.arrow then
        if wz.filled then
            C.arrow.Filled = true
        end
        if wz.thickness then
            C.arrow.Thickness = 1
        end
        C.arrow.Color = C.relationshipColor or Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    end
    return C.arrow
end)
local fF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    if#C.box3dLines > 0 then
        return C.box3dLines
    end
    for Y = 1, #Kz, 1 do
        local g = az("Line")
        if g then
            if wz.thickness then
                g.Thickness = 1
            end
            g.Color = C.relationshipColor or Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
            table.insert(C.box3dLines, g)
        end
    end
    return C.box3dLines
end)
local RF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    local g = C.highlight
    if not g or not g.Parent then
        local Y, m = pcall(function()
            local Y = Instance.new("Highlight")
            Y.Name = "KnwareESP_" .. tostring(C.player.UserId)
            Y.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            Y.FillTransparency = 0.78
            Y.OutlineTransparency = 0
            Y.Parent = M.CurrentCamera or M
            return Y
        end)
        if not Y then
            return nil
        end
        g = m
        C.highlight = g
    elseif g.Parent ~= (M.CurrentCamera or M)then
        g.Parent = M.CurrentCamera or M
    end
    local m = C.relationshipColor or Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
    g.Adornee = Y
    g.FillColor = m
    g.OutlineColor = m
    return g
end)
local QF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g, m)
    local E = TF(C)
    if not E then
        return
    end
    local M = Y.ViewportSize
    local H = Vector2.new(M.X * 0.5, M.Y * 0.5)
    local A = g - H
    if m <= 0 then
        A = - A
    end
    if A.Magnitude < 0.001 then
        A = Vector2.new(0, - 1)
    end
    local F = A.Unit
    local u = math.max(40, math.min(M.X, M.Y) * 0.42)
    local q = H + F * u
    local P = q - F * 14
    local z = Vector2.new( - F.Y, F.X) * 8
    E.PointA = q
    E.PointB = P + z
    E.PointC = P - z
    E.Visible = true
end)
local VF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g, m)
    if C.character == Y and (C.humanoid == g and C.root == m)then
        return
    end
    C.character = Y
    C.humanoid = g
    C.root = m
    table.clear(C.bodyParts)
    table.clear(C.skeletonPairs)
    for Y, g in ipairs(Y:GetChildren())do
        if g:IsA("BasePart")then
            table.insert(C.bodyParts, g)
        end
    end
    local E = (g.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15) and dz or Gz
    for g, m in ipairs(E)do
        table.insert(C.skeletonPairs, {Y:FindFirstChild(m[1]), Y:FindFirstChild(m[2])} )
    end
end)
local pF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    local Y = C.Team
    local g = Y and Y.Name or nil
    if Jz.Admin(C) and g ~= "STAFF" then
        return g and ("STAFF | " .. g) or "STAFF"
    end
    return g
end)
local IF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
    local Y = C.Team
    local g, m = pcall(function()
        return Y.TeamColor.Color
    end)
    return(g and m) or Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)
end)
local OF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y)
    local g, m = Jz.Appearance(C)
    Y.relationshipLabel = m
    if Y.relationshipColor == g then
        return
    end
    Y.relationshipColor = g
    if Y.box then
        Y.box.Color = g
    end
    if Y.name then
        Y.name.Color = g
    end
    if Y.distance then
        Y.distance.Color = g
    end
    if Y.item then
        Y.item.Color = g
    end
    if Y.tracer then
        Y.tracer.Color = g
    end
    if Y.arrow then
        Y.arrow.Color = g
    end
    if Y.highlight then
        Y.highlight.FillColor = g
        Y.highlight.OutlineColor = g
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(Y.lines)do
        if Y then
            Y.Color = g
        end
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(Y.box3dLines)do
        if Y then
            Y.Color = g
        end
    end
end)
local XF = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g, m, E)
    local M = C.Character
    local H = M and M:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local A = M and M:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not M or not H or H.Health <= 0 or not A then
        Y.character = nil
        Y.nextVisualUpdate = E + mF
        uF(Y)
        return
    end
    VF(Y, M, H, A)
    local F = ((A.Position - m)).Magnitude
    if F > T.maxDistance then
        Y.nextVisualUpdate = E + mF
        uF(Y)
        return
    end
    OF(C, Y)
    if T.highlight then
        local C = RF(Y, M)
        if C then
            C.Enabled = true
        end
    elseif Y.highlight then
        Y.highlight.Enabled = false
    end
    local u, q, P = zF(g, A.Position)
    if not q or P <= 0 then
        Y.nextVisualUpdate = E + mF
        FF(Y)
        if T.arrow then
            QF(Y, g, u, P)
        end
        return
    end
    local z = F <= CF
    local o = z and YF or gF
    Y.nextVisualUpdate = HF(Y.nextVisualUpdate, E, o)
    if Y.arrow then
        Y.arrow.Visible = false
    end
    local f, handleIz, handleReady, V = math.huge, math.huge, - math.huge, - math.huge
    local handleM = false
    local updateInstancePropertiesPosition, O = {} , {}
    if T.box or T.name or T.distance or T.box3d or T.teamTag then
        local C, m
        for Y, g in ipairs(Y.bodyParts)do
            if g.Parent == M then
                local Y = g.Size * 0.5
                local E, M = g.Position - Y, g.Position + Y
                if C then
                    C = Vector3.new(math.min(C.X, E.X), math.min(C.Y, E.Y), math.min(C.Z, E.Z))
                    m = Vector3.new(math.max(m.X, M.X), math.max(m.Y, M.Y), math.max(m.Z, M.Z))
                else
                    C, m = E, M
                end
            end
        end
        if C then
            local Y = 0
            for E = 0, 1, 1 do
                for M = 0, 1, 1 do
                    for H = 0, 1, 1 do
                        Y = Y + 1
                        local A = Vector3.new(E == 0 and C.X or m.X, M == 0 and C.Y or m.Y, H == 0 and C.Z or m.Z)
                        local F, u, q = zF(g, A)
                        updateInstancePropertiesPosition[Y] = F
                        O[Y] = q
                        if q > 0.5 then
                            handleM = true
                            f = math.min(f, F.X)
                            handleIz = math.min(handleIz, F.Y)
                            handleReady = math.max(handleReady, F.X)
                            V = math.max(V, F.Y)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if handleM then
            local C = g.ViewportSize
            f = math.clamp(f, - 32, C.X + 32)
            handleReady = math.clamp(handleReady, - 32, C.X + 32)
            handleIz = math.clamp(handleIz, - 32, C.Y + 32)
            V = math.clamp(V, - 32, C.Y + 32)
            if handleReady - f < 1 or V - handleIz < 1 then
                handleM = false
            end
        end
    end
    if T.box and Y.box then
        if handleM then
            Y.box.Position = Vector2.new(f, handleIz)
            Y.box.Size = Vector2.new(handleReady - f, V - handleIz)
            Y.box.Visible = true
        else
            Y.box.Visible = false
        end
    elseif Y.box then
        Y.box.Visible = false
    end
    if T.name and Y.name then
        if handleM then
            Y.name.Text = C.Name .. Y.relationshipLabel
            Y.name.Position = Vector2.new((f + handleReady) / 2, handleIz - 18)
            Y.name.Visible = true
        else
            Y.name.Visible = false
        end
    elseif Y.name then
        Y.name.Visible = false
    end
    if T.teamTag and Y.team then
        local g = handleM and pF(C) or nil
        if g then
            Y.team.Text = g
            Y.team.Color = IF(C)
            Y.team.Position = Vector2.new(
            (f + handleReady) / 2,
            (T.name and (Y.name and Y.name.Visible)) and (handleIz - 32) or (handleIz - 18)
        )
        Y.team.Visible = true
    else
        Y.team.Visible = false
    end
elseif Y.team then
    Y.team.Visible = false
end
if T.distance and Y.distance then
    if handleM then
        Y.distance.Text = string.format("%dm", math.floor(F))
        if wz.center then
            Y.distance.Center = true
        end
        Y.distance.Position = Vector2.new((f + handleReady) / 2, V + 3)
        Y.distance.Visible = true
    else
        Y.distance.Visible = false
    end
elseif Y.distance then
    Y.distance.Visible = false
end
if T.heldItem and Y.item then
    local g = Y.character or C.Character
    local m = handleM and (g and g:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool"))
    if m then
        Y.item.Text = m.Name
        local C = (f + handleReady) / 2
        local g = T.distance and (Y.distance and Y.distance.Visible)
        if g then
            local g = wz.largura(Y.distance)
            local m = wz.largura(Y.item)
            local E = C - ((g + MF) + m) / 2
            if wz.center then
                Y.distance.Center = false
            end
            Y.distance.Position = Vector2.new(E, V + 3)
            if wz.center then
                Y.item.Center = false
            end
            Y.item.Position = Vector2.new((E + g) + MF, V + 3)
        else
            if wz.center then
                Y.item.Center = true
            end
            Y.item.Position = Vector2.new(C, V + 3)
        end
        Y.item.Visible = true
    else
        Y.item.Visible = false
    end
elseif Y.item then
    Y.item.Visible = false
end
if T.tracer then
    local C = oF(Y)
    if C then
        local Y = g.ViewportSize
        C.From = Vector2.new(Y.X * 0.5, Y.Y - 4)
        C.To = handleM and Vector2.new((f + handleReady) * 0.5, (handleIz + V) * 0.5) or u
        C.Visible = true
    end
elseif Y.tracer then
    Y.tracer.Visible = false
end
if T.box3d then
    local C = fF(Y)
    for Y, g in ipairs(Kz)do
        local m = C[Y]
        local E, M = updateInstancePropertiesPosition[g[1]], updateInstancePropertiesPosition[g[2]]
        local H, A = O[g[1]], O[g[2]]
        if m and (E and (M and (H and (A and (H > 0.5 and A > 0.5)))))then
            m.From = E
            m.To = M
            m.Visible = true
        elseif m then
            m.Visible = false
        end
    end
else
    for C, Y in ipairs(Y.box3dLines)do
        Y.Visible = false
    end
end
if not T.skeleton then
    for C, Y in ipairs(Y.lines)do
        Y.Visible = false
    end
elseif E >= Y.nextSkeletonUpdate then
    local C = z and YF or gF
    Y.nextSkeletonUpdate = HF(Y.nextSkeletonUpdate, E, C)
    for C, m in ipairs(Y.lines)do
        local E = Y.skeletonPairs[C]
        if E and m then
            local C, Y = E[1], E[2]
            if C and (Y and (C.Parent == M and Y.Parent == M))then
                local E, M, H = zF(g, C.Position)
                local A, F, u = zF(g, Y.Position)
                if M and (F and (H > 0.5 and u > 0.5))then
                    m.From = E
                    m.To = A
                    m.Visible = true
                else
                    m.Visible = false
                end
            else
                m.Visible = false
            end
        else
            m.Visible = false
        end
    end
end
end)
handleReady(C.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(C)
    local Y = T.bundles[C]
    if Y then
        qF(Y)
        T.bundles[C] = nil
    end
end))
local BF = false
handleReady(Y.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if not T.espEnabled then
        if BF then
            PF()
        end
        BF = false
        return
    end
    BF = true
    local Y = M.CurrentCamera
    if not Y then
        PF()
        return
    end
    local g = H.Character
    local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local E = m and m.Position or Y.CFrame.Position
    local A = C:GetPlayers()
    local F = os.clock()
    local u = EF
    for C, g in ipairs(A)do
        if g ~= H then
            local C = T.bundles[g]
            if not C and u > 0 then
                local Y, m = pcall(AF, g)
                if Y then
                    C = m
                elseif not o.espErro then
                    o.espErro = tostring(m)
                end
                u = u - 1
            end
            if C and F >= C.nextVisualUpdate then
                local m, M = pcall(XF, g, C, Y, E, F)
                if not m and not o.espErro then
                    o.espErro = tostring(M)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)))
local sF
local function kF()
    -- legacy no-op (silent aim removido)
end
local function LF()
    -- legacy no-op
end
local function NF(cam, part, char)
    local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {H.Character, char, cam}
    local hit = M:Raycast(origin, part.Position - origin, params)
    return hit == nil
end
local SF = {
    Cabeca = {"Head"},
    Tronco = {"Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "HumanoidRootPart"},
    Bracos = {"Right Arm", "Left Arm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
    Pernas = {"Right Leg", "Left Leg", "RightUpperLeg", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
}
local function eF(char)
    local parts = SF[T.silentAimBodyPart] or SF.Cabeca
    for _, name in ipairs(parts) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
            return p
        end
    end
    return char:FindFirstChild("Head")
end
local function playerTemArma()
    local char = H.Character
    if not char then
        return false
    end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    return tool ~= nil
end
-- Aimbot: alvo atual + lista ordenada por FOV para trocar facil
local aimbotLockedPlayer = nil
local aimbotLockIndex = 0
local function listarAlvosAimbot(cam)
    local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
    local center = cam.ViewportSize / 2
    local lista = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(C:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= H and not Jz.Protected(plr) then
            local char = plr.Character
            local part = char and eF(char)
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if part and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude
                if dist <= T.silentAimDistance then
                    local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                    if onScreen and sp.Z > 0 then
                        local fovDist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
                        if fovDist <= T.silentAimFov then
                            if not T.silentAimVisibleOnly or NF(cam, part, char) then
                                lista[#lista + 1] = {
                                    player = plr,
                                    part = part,
                                    fov = fovDist,
                                    dist = dist,
                                }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(lista, function(a, b)
        return a.fov < b.fov
    end)
    return lista
end
local function alvoAindaValido(cam, plr)
    if not plr or not plr.Parent then
        return false
    end
    if Jz.Protected(plr) then
        return false
    end
    local char = plr.Character
    local part = char and eF(char)
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not part or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    local dist = (part.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
    if dist > T.silentAimDistance then
        return false
    end
    local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
    if not onScreen or sp.Z <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    local center = cam.ViewportSize / 2
    local fovDist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
    if fovDist > T.silentAimFov * 1.35 then
        return false
    end
    if T.silentAimVisibleOnly and not NF(cam, part, char) then
        return false
    end
    return true, part
end
local function escolherAlvoAimbot(cam, forcarProximo)
    local lista = listarAlvosAimbot(cam)
    if #lista == 0 then
        aimbotLockedPlayer = nil
        aimbotLockIndex = 0
        return nil
    end
    if forcarProximo then
        aimbotLockIndex = (aimbotLockIndex % #lista) + 1
        local item = lista[aimbotLockIndex]
        aimbotLockedPlayer = item.player
        return item.part, item.player
    end
    if T.aimbotSticky and aimbotLockedPlayer then
        local ok, part = alvoAindaValido(cam, aimbotLockedPlayer)
        if ok then
            return part, aimbotLockedPlayer
        end
        aimbotLockedPlayer = nil
    end
    local item = lista[1]
    aimbotLockedPlayer = item.player
    aimbotLockIndex = 1
    return item.part, item.player
end
local function aplicarAimbot(dt)
    if not T.aimbotEnabled then
        return
    end
    if T.aimbotRequireWeapon and not playerTemArma() then
        aimbotLockedPlayer = nil
        return
    end
    local cam = M.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then
        return
    end
    local part = escolherAlvoAimbot(cam, false)
    if not part then
        return
    end
    local goal = CFrame.lookAt(cam.CFrame.Position, part.Position)
    local smooth = tonumber(T.aimbotSmooth) or 8
    if smooth <= 1 then
        cam.CFrame = goal
    else
        local alpha = math.clamp((dt or 0.016) * smooth, 0.05, 1)
        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(goal, alpha)
    end
end
handleReady(Y.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    pcall(aplicarAimbot, dt)
end))
-- Tecla para trocar de alvo (default Q)
handleReady(g.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then
        return
    end
    if not T.aimbotEnabled then
        return
    end
    local key = T.aimbotSwitchKey
    if not key then
        return
    end
    local match = false
    if typeof(key) == "EnumItem" and input.KeyCode == key then
        match = true
    elseif type(key) == "string" and input.KeyCode.Name == key then
        match = true
    end
    if not match then
        return
    end
    if T.aimbotRequireWeapon and not playerTemArma() then
        return
    end
    local cam = M.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        escolherAlvoAimbot(cam, true)
    end
end))
local nF
if wz.ok() then
    local ok, circle = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new("Circle")
    end)
    if ok then
        nF = circle
        wz.set(nF, "Visible", false)
        wz.set(nF, "Color", Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255))
        wz.set(nF, "Thickness", 2)
        wz.set(nF, "Transparency", 0)
        wz.set(nF, "Filled", false)
        wz.set(nF, "NumSides", 64)
    end
end
handleReady(Y.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not nF then
        return
    end
    local cam = M.CurrentCamera
    if not T.silentAimFovVisible or not cam then
        nF.Visible = false
        return
    end
    nF.Position = cam.ViewportSize / 2
    nF.Radius = T.silentAimFov
    nF.Visible = true
end))
local function bF()
    local C = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Lockpick")
    local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("Lockpick")
    if not g or not g.Visible then
        return nil
    end
    local m = g:FindFirstChild("Bloco")
    local E = g:FindFirstChild("Objetivo")
    local M = g:FindFirstChild("Btn")
    if m and (E and (M and (m.Visible and E.Visible)))then
        return m, E, M
    end
    return nil
end
local function yF(C, Y)
    local g, m = C.AbsolutePosition, C.AbsoluteSize
    local E, M = Y.AbsolutePosition, Y.AbsoluteSize
    if g.X + m.X < E.X or g.X > E.X + M.X then
        return false
    end
    local H = g.Y + m.Y / 2
    local A = M.Y * 0.25
    return H >= E.Y + A and H <= (E.Y + M.Y) - A
end
local function xF(C)
    if type(getconnections) == "function" then
        local Y, g = pcall(getconnections, C.MouseButton1Click)
        if Y and (type(g) == "table" and #g > 0)then
            for C, Y in ipairs(g)do
                local g = Y.Function
                if type(g) == "function" then
                    task.spawn(g)
                end
            end
            return
        end
    end
    if type(firesignal) == "function" then
        pcall(firesignal, C.MouseButton1Click)
    end
end
local cF = 1.15
local lF = 0
handleReady(Y.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not T.perfectLockpick then
        return
    end
    local C, Y, g = bF()
    if not C then
        return
    end
    if os.clock() - lF < cF then
        return
    end
    if not yF(C, Y)then
        Y.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, C.Position.Y.Scale, C.Position.Y.Offset)
        return
    end
    lF = os.clock()
    xF(g)
end))
local ZF = 20
local DF = 14
local rF = 1.5
local hF = D
local jF = 18
local WF = 65
local vF = WF
local JF = 160
local function wF()
    return M:FindFirstChild("DropadosInv")
end
local function aF(C)
    if C:IsA("BasePart")then
        return C
    end
    if C:IsA("Model")then
        return C.PrimaryPart or C:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    end
    return nil
end
local function iF()
    local C = {}
    local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    local g = wF()
    if not Y or not g then
        return C
    end
    for g, m in ipairs(g:GetChildren())do
        local E = aF(m)
        if E then
            table.insert(C, {item = m, parte = E, nome = m.Name, distancia = ((Y.Position - E.Position)).Magnitude} )
        end
    end
    table.sort(C, function(C, Y)
        return C.distancia < Y.distancia
    end)
    return C
end
local function dF(C)
    local Y = C:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    if not Y or not Y.Enabled then
        return false
    end
    if type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
        return(pcall(fireproximityprompt, Y, 0))
    end
    return(
    pcall(function()
        Y:InputHoldBegin()
        task.wait(math.max(0.15, Y.HoldDuration + 0.1))
        Y:InputHoldEnd()
    end)
)
end
local function GF(C, g)
    o.itemTeleportRunId = o.itemTeleportRunId + 1
    local m = o.itemTeleportRunId
    local E = isUpdateInstancePropertiesEValid()
    local M = T.noclipEnabled
    if not M then
        T.noclipEnabled = true
        pcall(Nz)
    end
    local H = false
    local A = Vector3.zero
    if E then
        local Y = os.clock() + 8
        while os.clock() < Y do
            if o.destroyed or m ~= o.itemTeleportRunId then
                break
            end
            if not C.item.Parent or not C.parte.Parent then
                H = true
                break
            end
            local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
            if not Y then
                break
            end
            local g = C.parte.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            if((g - Y.Position)).Magnitude <= 4 then
                H = true
                break
            end
            Y.CFrame = CFrame.new(g) * Y.CFrame.Rotation
            Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
    local F = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    local u = F and ((C.parte.Position - F.Position)).Magnitude or 0
    local q = C.parte.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    local P = math.max(F and F.Position.Y or q.Y, q.Y) + jF
    local z = math.min(T.itemTeleportSpeed, l)
    local f = (os.clock() + 20) + (u / math.max(z, 1)) * 2
    while not E and os.clock() < f do
        local g = Y.RenderStepped:Wait()
        if o.destroyed or m ~= o.itemTeleportRunId then
            break
        end
        local E, M = C.item, C.parte
        if not E.Parent or not M.Parent then
            H = true
            break
        end
        local F = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
        if not F then
            break
        end
        q = M.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        local u = Vector3.new(q.X - F.Position.X, 0, q.Z - F.Position.Z)
        local z = q
        if u.Magnitude > 4 then
            z = F.Position.Y < P - 1 and Vector3.new(F.Position.X, P, F.Position.Z) or Vector3.new(q.X, P, q.Z)
        end
        local f = z - F.Position
        local handleIz = f.Magnitude
        if((q - F.Position)).Magnitude <= 4 then
            H = true
            break
        end
        local handleReady, V, handleM, updateInstancePropertiesPosition, O = handleQ(F, g, ZF, true)
        local X = math.min(T.itemTeleportSpeed, O and l or WF)
        local B = math.min(handleIz, math.max(0, X - V) * g, handleM)
        local s = f.Unit
        A = s
        if B > 0 then
            F.CFrame = F.CFrame + s * B
        end
        F.AssemblyLinearVelocity = s * V
        local createDivisor = isCValid()
        if createDivisor then
            pcall(createDivisor.ChangeState, createDivisor, Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
        end
    end
    local handleIz = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    local handleReady = isCValid()
    local V = Vector3.new(A.X, 0, A.Z)
    if not E and (handleIz and handleReady)then
        if V.Magnitude <= 0 then
            local C = handleIz.CFrame.LookVector
            V = Vector3.new(C.X, 0, C.Z)
        end
        V = V.Magnitude > 0 and V.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, - 1)
        local C = os.clock() + rF
        while os.clock() < C and (not o.destroyed and m == o.itemTeleportRunId)do
            local C = Y.RenderStepped:Wait()
            local g, m, E, M = handleQ(handleIz, C, DF, true)
            if M <= hF then
                break
            end
            local H = math.clamp(m / math.max(handleReady.WalkSpeed, 1), 0, 1)
            pcall(handleReady.Move, handleReady, V * H, false)
            handleIz.AssemblyLinearVelocity = V * m
        end
    end
    if handleIz then
        handleIz.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    if H and m == o.itemTeleportRunId then
        local Y = g or function()
            return not C.item.Parent
        end
        for g = 1, 3, 1 do
            if Y()then
                break
            end
            dF(C.item)
            task.wait(0.35)
        end
    end
    if not M and m == o.itemTeleportRunId then
        T.noclipEnabled = false
        pcall(Nz)
    end
    return H
end
local function tF()
    local C = M:FindFirstChild("FarmPartesDeArma")
    C = C and C:FindFirstChild("Locais")
    if not C then
        return nil
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(C:GetChildren())do
        if Y:IsA("BasePart") and Y:FindFirstChild("GUI")then
            return Y
        end
    end
    local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    if not Y then
        return nil
    end
    local g, m
    for C, E in ipairs(C:GetChildren())do
        local M = E:IsA("BasePart") and E:FindFirstChild("Interagir")
        if M and M.Enabled then
            local C = ((Y.Position - E.Position)).Magnitude
            if not m or C < m then
                g, m = E, C
            end
        end
    end
    return g
end
local function KF()
    o.farmPartesRunId = o.farmPartesRunId + 1
    T.farmPartesEnabled = false
end
local Cf = 15
local function Yf()
    if T.flyEnabled then
        T.flyEnabled = false
        pcall(xz)
    end
    o.farmPartesRunId = o.farmPartesRunId + 1
    local C = o.farmPartesRunId
    local Y = 0
    local g = T.noclipEnabled
    if not g then
        T.noclipEnabled = true
        pcall(Nz)
    end
    while T.farmPartesEnabled and (not o.destroyed and C == o.farmPartesRunId)do
        local g = tF()
        if not g then
            task.wait(1)
        else
            local m = g
            local E = GF( {item = m, parte = m} , function()
                return tF() ~= m
            end)
            if C ~= o.farmPartesRunId then
                break
            end
            local M = E and tF() ~= m
            if M then
                Y = Y + 1
            end
            local H = M and Cf or 1
            local A = os.clock() + H
            while os.clock() < A do
                if not T.farmPartesEnabled or o.destroyed or C ~= o.farmPartesRunId then
                    break
                end
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end
    end
    if C == o.farmPartesRunId then
        T.farmPartesEnabled = false
        o.farmPartesColetadas = Y
    end
    if not g and (not T.farmPartesEnabled or C == o.farmPartesRunId)then
        T.noclipEnabled = false
        pcall(Nz)
    end
    return Y
end
local gf = {
    ArmaPDSecreta = 720,
    Barret = 700,
    ["FuzilTrov\195\163o"] = 340,
    FuzilPhantom = 335,
    FAL = 285,
    FALPolicial = 285,
    AK47 = 275,
    Fuzil = 265,
    FuzilPolicial = 265,
    G3 = 250,
    G3Policial = 250,
    Submetralhadora = 120,
    Uzi = 115,
    Glock = 185,
    GlockPolicial = 185,
    Pt = 155,
    ["Oit\195\163o"] = 145,
}
local mf = {}
local Ef = 0
local function Mf(C)
    if not C or not C:IsA("Tool")then
        return nil
    end
    local Y = C:FindFirstChild("Cfg")
    if not Y or not Y:IsA("ModuleScript")then
        return nil
    end
    local g, m = pcall(require, Y)
    if not g or type(m) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local E = tonumber(m.BaseDamage)
    local M = tonumber(m.FireRate)
    if not E or not M then
        return nil
    end
    local H = tonumber(m.Range) or 0
    local A = tonumber(m.AmmoPerMag) or 0
    return((E / math.max(0.05, M) + E * 2) + H * 0.01) + A * 0.1
end
local function Hf(Y)
    local g = os.clock()
    if not Y and g - Ef < 5 then
        return
    end
    Ef = g
    for C, Y in ipairs(C:GetPlayers())do
        local g = {Y:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), Y.Character}
        for C, Y in ipairs(g)do
            if Y then
                for C, Y in ipairs(Y:GetChildren())do
                    local g = Mf(Y)
                    if g then
                        mf[Y.Name] = g
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local function Af(C)
    return mf[C] or gf[C]
end
local function Ff()
    local C = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("InventoryUI")
    local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("InvFrame")
    local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("InvPessoal")
    return m and m:FindFirstChild("ConteudoInv")
end
local function uf()
    Hf(false)
    local C = {}
    local Y = Ff()
    if Y then
        for Y, g in ipairs(Y:GetChildren())do
            local m = g:FindFirstChild("In")
            if m then
                for Y, g in ipairs(m:GetChildren())do
                    if g:IsA("Frame") and Af(g.Name)then
                        C[g.Name] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for Y, g in ipairs( {H:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), H.Character} )do
        if g then
            for Y, g in ipairs(g:GetChildren())do
                if g:IsA("Tool")then
                    local Y = Mf(g)
                    if Y then
                        mf[g.Name] = Y
                        C[g.Name] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return C
end
local function qf()
    local C, Y = nil, - math.huge
    for g in pairs(uf())do
        local m = Af(g)
        if m and (m > Y or (m == Y and (not C or g < C)))then
            C, Y = g, m
        end
    end
    return C, Y
end
local function Pf(C)
    local Y = H:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local g = H.Character
    local m = Y and Y:FindFirstChild(C)
    if not m and g then
        m = g:FindFirstChild(C)
    end
    return m and (m:IsA("Tool") and m) or nil
end
local function zf()
    local C, Y = {} , {}
    for g, m in ipairs( {H:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), H.Character} )do
        if m then
            for g, m in ipairs(m:GetChildren())do
                if m:IsA("Tool") and not Y[m]then
                    Y[m] = true
                    table.insert(C, m)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return C
end
local function of()
    if not T.bestWeaponEnabled or o.destroyed then
        return
    end
    local C, Y = qf()
    if not C or Pf(C)then
        return
    end
    local g = os.clock()
    if o.bestWeaponLastName == C and g - o.bestWeaponLastAttempt < 2 then
        return
    end
    o.bestWeaponLastName = C
    o.bestWeaponLastAttempt = g
    local m = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local E = m:FindFirstChild("OrpheusShared")
    local M = E and E:FindFirstChild("InvRemotes")
    local A = M and M:FindFirstChild("InvRequest")
    if not A or not A:IsA("RemoteFunction")then
        return
    end
    local F = zf()
    local u = H:FindFirstChild("InvVals")
    local q = u and u:FindFirstChild("SlotsAtivos")
    local P = q and tonumber(q.Value) or 3
    if#F >= P then
        local g, m = nil, math.huge
        for Y, E in ipairs(F)do
            if E.Name ~= C then
                local C = Af(E.Name) or- 1
                if C < m then
                    g, m = E, C
                end
            end
        end
        if g and m < Y then
            pcall(function()
                A:InvokeServer("desequipainv", g)
            end)
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end
    if not Pf(C)then
        pcall(function()
            A:InvokeServer("semespacoinv", C)
        end)
    end
end
local function Tf(C)
    T.bestWeaponEnabled = C == true
    o.bestWeaponRunId = o.bestWeaponRunId + 1
    o.bestWeaponLastAttempt = 0
    o.bestWeaponLastName = nil
    local Y = o.bestWeaponRunId
    if not T.bestWeaponEnabled then
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while T.bestWeaponEnabled and (not o.destroyed and o.bestWeaponRunId == Y)do
            pcall(of)
            task.wait(0.8)
        end
    end)
end
do
    local C = 1500
    local Y = Color3.fromRGB(70, 190, 120)
    local g = Color3.fromRGB(232, 190, 70)
    local E = Color3.fromRGB(232, 92, 84)
    local A = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18)
    local F = Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 52)
    local u = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 43)
    local q = Color3.fromRGB(233, 234, 238)
    local P = Color3.fromRGB(160, 140, 190)
    local z = "rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json"
    local f = Font.new(z, Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
    local handleIz = Font.new(z, Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
    local V, handleM = 208, 64
    local O = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local X = {
        gui = nil,
        frame = nil,
        fill = nil,
        percentLabel = nil,
        valueLabel = nil,
        carLabel = nil,
        uiScale = nil,
        vidaConn = nil,
        car = nil,
        maxVida = C,
        savedPosition = nil,
    }
    local function handleC(C, Y, g)
        local m = Instance.new(C)
        for C, Y in pairs(Y)do
            m[C] = Y
        end
        m.Parent = g
        return m
    end
    local function waitForChild()
        if gethui then
            local C, Y = pcall(gethui)
            if C and Y then
                return Y
            end
        end
        return H:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end
    local function handleG(C)
        if C <= 20 then
            return E
        end
        if C <= 50 then
            return g
        end
        return Y
    end
    local function isFromOffsetCValid()
        local C = M.CurrentCamera
        return C and C.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
    end
    local function updateInstancePropertiesScale()
        if not X.uiScale then
            return
        end
        local C = isFromOffsetCValid()
        X.uiScale.Scale = math.clamp(C.X / 1280, 0.85, 1.1)
    end
    local function fromOffset()
        local C = isFromOffsetCValid()
        local Y = math.max(40, (C.Y - handleM) - 150)
        return UDim2.fromOffset(14, Y)
    end
    local function updateValueConnection(C)
        if not X.frame then
            return
        end
        if C > X.maxVida then
            X.maxVida = C
        end
        local Y = math.clamp((C / X.maxVida) * 100, 0, 100)
        local g = handleG(Y)
        X.percentLabel.Text = string.format("%d%%", math.floor(Y + 0.5))
        X.percentLabel.TextColor3 = g
        X.valueLabel.Text = string.format("%d / %d", math.floor(C + 0.5), math.floor(X.maxVida + 0.5));
        (m:Create(X.fill, O, {Size = UDim2.new(Y / 100, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = g} )):Play()
    end
    local function updateInstanceProperties()
        if X.gui and X.gui.Parent then
            return
        end
        local C = handleC(
        "ScreenGui",
        {
            Name = "VeteranoDurabilidade",
            ResetOnSpawn = false,
            IgnoreGuiInset = true,
            DisplayOrder = 9999,
            ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        } ,
        waitForChild()
    )
    local g = handleC(
    "Frame",
    {
        Name = "HUD",
        Active = true,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0),
        Position = X.savedPosition or fromOffset(),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(V, handleM),
        BackgroundColor3 = A,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = false,
    } ,
    C
)
X.uiScale = handleC("UIScale", {Scale = 1} , g)
handleC("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)} , g)
handleC(
"UIStroke",
{Color = F, Transparency = 0.2, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border} ,
g
)
handleC(
"UIPadding",
{
    PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 9),
    PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 9),
    PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 11),
    PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 11),
} ,
g
)
X.carLabel = handleC(
"TextLabel",
{
    Name = "Carro",
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
    Size = UDim2.new(1, - 46, 0, 15),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,

m

handleC("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)} , X.fill)
X.valueLabel = handleC(
"TextLabel",

    X.savedPosition = C
