local function vidaDoCarro(C)
    local Y = C:FindFirstChild("Body")
    local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("Pintura")
    local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("LifeController")
    return m and m:FindFirstChild("Vida")
end
o.vidaDoCarro = vidaDoCarro
local function handler()
    local C = isCValid()
    local Y = C and C.SeatPart
    if not Y then
        return nil
    end
    local g = M:FindFirstChild("CarrosSpawnados")
    if not g then
        return nil
    end
    local m = Y
    while m and m.Parent do
        if m.Parent == g then
            return m
        end
        m = m.Parent
    end
    return nil
end
local function secondaryUpdateInstanceProperties()
    if X.vidaConn then
        X.vidaConn:Disconnect()
        X.vidaConn = nil
    end
    X.car = nil
    if X.painel then
        X.painel:SetVisible(false)
    end
end
local function secondaryUpdateValueConnection(Y)
    local g = vidaDoCarro(Y)
    if not g then
        return
    end
    if X.vidaConn then
        X.vidaConn:Disconnect()
    end
    X.car = Y
    X.maxVida = math.max(C, g.Value)
    if X.carLabel then
        X.carLabel.Text = Y.Name
    end
    if X.painel then
        X.painel:SetVisible(true)
    end
    updateValueConnection(g.Value)
    X.vidaConn = (g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value")):Connect(function()
        updateValueConnection(g.Value)
    end)
end
function o.ativarDurabilidade()
    updateInstanceProperties()
    o.durabilidadeRunId = o.durabilidadeRunId + 1
    local C = o.durabilidadeRunId
    task.spawn(function()
        while T.durabilidadeHudEnabled and (not o.destroyed and o.durabilidadeRunId == C)do
            local C = handler()
            if C ~= X.car then
                if C and vidaDoCarro(C)then
                    pcall(secondaryUpdateValueConnection, C)
                else
                    pcall(secondaryUpdateInstanceProperties)
                end
            elseif C and not X.vidaConn then
                pcall(secondaryUpdateValueConnection, C)
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
end
function o.desativarDurabilidade()
    o.durabilidadeRunId = o.durabilidadeRunId + 1
    pcall(secondaryUpdateInstanceProperties)
    if X.painel then
        X.painel:SetVisible(false)
    end
end
function o.destruirDurabilidade()
    pcall(secondaryUpdateInstanceProperties)
    if X.gui then
        pcall(function()
            X.gui:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    X.gui, X.frame, X.fill, X.painel = nil, nil, nil, nil
    X.percentLabel, X.valueLabel, X.carLabel, X.uiScale = nil, nil, nil, nil
end
end
do
    local g = 1e+50
    local m = 10
    local E = 0.1
    local H = {bundles = {} , catalogo = {} , proximaAtualizacao = 0}
    local function isCValid(C, Y)
        local g = C and C:FindFirstChild(Y)
        return g and tonumber(g.Value) or 0
    end
    local function handleC(C)
        local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Config")
        if not Y then
            return nil
        end
        return {nome = C.Name, valor = isCValid(Y, "Valor")}
    end
    local function updateCatalogo()
        local C = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local Y = C:FindFirstChild("OrpheusShared")
        local E = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("Concessionaria")
        local M = E and E:FindFirstChild("Veiculos")
        if not M then
            H.catalogo = {}
            return
        end
        local isCValid = {}
        for C, Y in ipairs(M:GetChildren())do
            if Y:IsA("Model")then
                local C = handleC(Y)
                if C and (C.valor > 0 and C.valor < g)then
                    table.insert(isCValid, C)
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(isCValid, function(C, Y)
            if C.valor == Y.valor then
                return C.nome < Y.nome
            end
            return C.valor > Y.valor
        end)
        H.catalogo = {}
        for C = 1, math.min(m, #isCValid), 1 do
            local Y = isCValid[C]
            H.catalogo[Y.nome] = Y
        end
        for C = 2, #isCValid, 1 do
            assert(isCValid[C - 1].valor >= isCValid[C].valor, "ranking de carros fora de ordem")
        end
    end
    local q = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        return C.PrimaryPart or C:FindFirstChild("DriveSeat", true) or C:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end)
    local P = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        local Y = ((((tostring(math.floor(C + 0.5))):reverse()):gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1.")):reverse()):gsub("^%.", "")
        return Y
    end)
    local z = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C)
        return(((((tostring(C)):gsub("&", "&amp;")):gsub("<", "&lt;")):gsub(">", "&gt;")):gsub('"', "&quot;")):gsub(
        "'",
        "&apos;"
    )
end)
local f = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Y)
    local g = Y:FindFirstChild("DriveSeat", true) or Y:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
    local m = g and (g:IsA("VehicleSeat") and g.Occupant) or nil
    local E = m and C:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m.Parent) or nil
    return E and E.Name or "Desocupado", E ~= nil
end)
local handleIz = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(C, Y, g)
    local m, E, H = pcall(function()
        return C:GetBoundingBox()
    end)
    if not m then
        Y.gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
        return
    end
    local isCValid = M.CurrentCamera
    local handleC = E.Position + Vector3.new(0, H.Y * 0.5, 0)
    if isCValid then
        local C = math.max(1, ((handleC - isCValid.CFrame.Position)):Dot(isCValid.CFrame.LookVector))
        local Y = math.max(1, isCValid.ViewportSize.Y)
        local g = ((2 * C) * math.tan(math.rad(isCValid.FieldOfView * 0.5))) / Y
        handleC = handleC + (isCValid.CFrame.UpVector * g) * 50
    else
        handleC = handleC + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
    end
    Y.gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = handleC - g.Position
end)
local function handler(C)
    local Y = H.bundles[C]
    if not Y then
        return
    end
    if Y.highlight then
        pcall(function()
            Y.highlight:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    if Y.gui then
        pcall(function()
            Y.gui:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    H.bundles[C] = nil
end
local function createKnwareBestCarEsp(C, Y)
    local g = Color3.fromRGB(255, 196, 72)
    local m = Instance.new("Highlight")
    m.Name = "KnwareBestCarESP"
    m.Adornee = C
    m.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    m.FillColor = g
    m.OutlineColor = g
    m.FillTransparency = 0.78
    m.OutlineTransparency = 0
    m.Parent = M.CurrentCamera or M
    local E = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    E.Name = "KnwareBestCarLabel"
    E.Adornee = Y
    E.AlwaysOnTop = true
    E.LightInfluence = 0
    E.MaxDistance = 10000
    E.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 84)
    E.Parent = M.CurrentCamera or M
    local isCValid = Instance.new("Frame")
    isCValid.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18)
    isCValid.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    isCValid.BorderSizePixel = 0
    isCValid.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    isCValid.Parent = E
    local handleC = Instance.new("UICorner")
    handleC.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
    handleC.Parent = isCValid
    local updateCatalogo = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    updateCatalogo.Color = Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 52)
    updateCatalogo.Transparency = 0.2
    updateCatalogo.Thickness = 1
    updateCatalogo.Parent = isCValid
    local q = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    q.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    q.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 7)
    q.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 1, - 14)
    q.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    q.RichText = true
    q.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(233, 234, 238)
    q.TextSize = 13
    q.TextWrapped = true
    q.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    q.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    q.Parent = isCValid
    local P = {highlight = m, gui = E, texto = q, parte = Y}
    H.bundles[C] = P
    return P
end
local updateInstancePropertiesPosition = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    for C, Y in pairs(H.bundles)do
        if Y.highlight then
            Y.highlight.Enabled = false
        end
        if Y.gui then
            Y.gui.Enabled = false
        end
    end
end)
local O = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if not T.bestCarsEspEnabled then
        updateInstancePropertiesPosition()
        return
    end
    if next(H.catalogo) == nil then
        updateCatalogo()
    end
    local C = M:FindFirstChild("CarrosSpawnados")
    local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    if not C or not Y then
        updateInstancePropertiesPosition()
        return
    end
    local g = {}
    for C, m in ipairs(C:GetChildren())do
        if m:IsA("Model")then
            local C = H.catalogo[m.Name]
            local E = q(m)
            if E and C then
                g[m] = true
                local M = H.bundles[m] or createKnwareBestCarEsp(m, E)
                M.highlight.Enabled = true
                M.gui.Enabled = true
                if M.parte ~= E then
                    M.parte = E
                    M.gui.Adornee = E
                end
                handleIz(m, M, E)
                local isCValid, handleC = f(m)
                local updateCatalogo = handleC and "#E85C54" or "#46BE78"
                M.texto.Text = string.format(
                'Ocupante: <font color="%s">%s</font>\nVe\195\173culo: %s\nValor: $<font color="#46BE78">%s</font>\nDist\195\162ncia: %.0fm',
                updateCatalogo,
                z(isCValid),
                z(m.Name),
                P(C.valor),
                ((E.Position - Y.Position)).Magnitude
            )
        else
            handler(m)
        end
    end
end
for C in pairs(H.bundles)do
    if not g[C] or not C.Parent then
        handler(C)
    end
end
end)
function o.ativarBestCarsEsp()
    updateCatalogo()
    H.proximaAtualizacao = 0
    pcall(O)
end
function o.desativarBestCarsEsp()
    updateInstancePropertiesPosition()
end
function o.destruirBestCarsEsp()
    for C in pairs(H.bundles)do
        handler(C)
    end
    H.catalogo = {}
end
handleReady(Y.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    local C = os.clock()
    if C < H.proximaAtualizacao then
        return
    end
    H.proximaAtualizacao = C + E
    pcall(O)
end)))
end
local ff = A.__KNWARE_UI_LIBRARY
local Rf
local Qf, Vf = xpcall(function()
    local F = A.VeteranoTeamUI or A.__VETERANO_UI_LIBRARY
    if F then
        local P = 10
        local z = 2.5
        local function isSendPrefRemoteCValid()
            return H.Team and H.Team.Name or nil
        end
        local function isSendPrefRemoteInstanceValid()
            local C = H:FindFirstChild("NewStats")
            local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Level")
            return Y and tonumber(Y.Value) or 0
        end
        local function format(C)
            if C >= 1000 then
                return string.format("%.0fk", C / 1000)
            end
            return tostring(C)
        end
        local function handleM()
            local C, Y = pcall(function()
                return require((game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")).Mercadinho.PrefModule)
            end)
            if not C or type(Y) ~= "table" or type(Y.Empregos) ~= "table" then
                return {}
            end
            local g = {}
            for C, Y in pairs(Y.Empregos) do
                table.insert(g, {nome = C, level = tonumber(Y.Level) or 0, salario = tonumber(Y.Salario) or 0})
            end
            table.sort(g, function(C, Y)
                if C.level == Y.level then
                    return C.nome < Y.nome
                end
                return C.level < Y.level
            end)
            return g
        end
        local function handleC()
            local C = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local Y = C:FindFirstChild("Mercadinho")
            if not Y then
                return {}
            end
            local g = {}
            local function handleC(C, handleC)
                local E, M = pcall(function()
                    return require(Y[C])
                end)
                if not E or type(M) ~= "table" or type(M[handleC]) ~= "table" then
                    return
                end
                for C, Y in pairs(M[handleC]) do
                    local handleC = tonumber(type(Y) == "table" and Y.Valor or Y)
                    if handleC then
                        g[C] = math.max(g[C] or 0, handleC)
                    end
                end
            end
            handleC("MercadoModule", "ItemsMercadinho")
            handleC("MercadoNegroModule", "ItemsMercadoNegro")
            local E = {}
            for C, Y in pairs(g) do
                table.insert(E, {chave = C, preco = Y})
            end
            local M, H = pcall(function()
                return require(Y.HPModule)
            end)
            if M and (type(H) == "table" and type(H.ItensHP) == "table") then
                for C, Y in pairs(H.ItensHP) do
                    local g = tonumber(type(Y) == "table" and Y.Valor or Y)
                    if g then
                        table.insert(E, {chave = C, preco = g, hpItem = true})
                    end
                end
            end
            table.sort(E, function(C, Y)
                if C.preco == Y.preco then
                    return C.chave < Y.chave
                end
                return C.preco < Y.preco
            end)
            return E
        end
        local function isGValid()
            local C = H:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
            local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Dinheiro")
            return Y and tonumber(Y.Value) or 0
        end
        local function isMValidY()
            local C = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("InventoryUI")
            local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("InvFrame")
            local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("InvPessoal")
            return m and m:FindFirstChild("ConteudoInv")
        end
        local function isGValidM(C)
            local Y = isMValidY()
            if Y then
                local g = Y:FindFirstChild(C, true)
                if g then
                    return g
                end
            end
            local g = H.Character
            local m = H:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            return (g and g:FindFirstChild(C)) or (m and m:FindFirstChild(C)) or nil
        end
        local function isPValid(C)
            local Y = isGValidM(C)
            if not Y then
                return 0
            end
            local g = Y:FindFirstChild("Qnt", true)
            return (g and tonumber(((g.Text or "")):match("%d+"))) or 1
        end
        local updateInstanceProperties = nil
        local b = false
        local y = nil
        local x = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        local c = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        local function updateSendPrefRemote(C)
            o.garbageFarmStatus = tostring(C or "")
            if y and (y.Instance and y.Instance.Parent) then
                y.Instance.Text = o.garbageFarmStatus
            end
        end
        local function isValidZ(C)
            return T.autoFarmLixeiroEnabled and (not o.destroyed and o.garbageFarmRunId == C)
        end
        local function secondaryZ(C, Y)
            local g = os.clock() + math.max(0, C or 0)
            repeat
                if not isValidZ(Y) then
                    return false
                end
                task.wait(math.min(0.1, math.max(0.01, g - os.clock())))
            until os.clock() >= g
            return isValidZ(Y)
        end
        local function handleIsValid()
            local C = H.Character
            return C, C and C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), C and C:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        local function handleG()
            local C = H:FindFirstChild("Inv")
            local Y = 0
            if C then
                for C, g in ipairs(C:GetChildren()) do
                    if g.Name == "Saco de Lixo" then
                        Y = Y + 1
                    end
                end
            end
            return Y
        end
        local function isMValid()
            local C = H:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
            local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Dinheiro")
            return Y and tonumber(Y.Value) or nil
        end
        local function handleSendPrefRemote()
            for C, Y in ipairs(handleM()) do
                if string.lower(Y.nome) == "lixeiro" then
                    return Y
                end
            end
            return {nome = "Lixeiro", level = 1, salario = 0}
        end
        local function sendC(C, Y)
            C:FireServer(Y.nome, Y.level, true)
            local g = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("LixeiroUI")
            local E = m and m:FindFirstChild("LixeiroFrame")
            if E and E.Visible then
                E.Visible = false
            end
        end
        local function sendPrefRemote(C)
            if isSendPrefRemoteCValid() == "Lixeiro" then
                return true
            end
            local Y = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("Mercadinho")
            local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("PrefRemote")
            if not g or not g:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                updateSendPrefRemote("PrefRemote nao encontrado; tentando novamente...")
                return false
            end
            local m = handleSendPrefRemote()
            if isSendPrefRemoteInstanceValid() < m.level then
                updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Lixeiro pede nivel %d; seu nivel e %d", m.level, isSendPrefRemoteInstanceValid()))
                return false
            end
            updateSendPrefRemote("Selecionando o emprego de lixeiro...")
            g:FireServer(m.nome, m.level)
            secondaryZ(0.25, C)
            if not isValidZ(C) then
                return false
            end
            sendC(g, m)
            local E = os.clock() + P
            local M = os.clock() + 1.25
            repeat
                if not isValidZ(C) then
                    return false
                end
                if isSendPrefRemoteCValid() == "Lixeiro" then
                    updateSendPrefRemote("Emprego aceito; aguardando o teleporte...")
                    secondaryZ(1.2, C)
                    return true
                end
                if os.clock() >= M then
                    sendC(g, m)
                    M = os.clock() + 1.25
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
            until os.clock() >= E
            updateSendPrefRemote("O servidor ainda nao confirmou o emprego.")
            return false
        end
        local function isValidM(C, Y)
            return C
                and (
                    C.Parent
                    and (
                        C:IsA("BasePart")
                        and (
                            C.Name == "Lixo"
                            and (
                                C.Transparency < 0.95
                                and (Y and (Y.Parent == C and (Y:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and Y.Enabled)))
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
        end
        local function handler()
            local C, Y, g = handleIsValid()
            local m = M:FindFirstChild("Lixeiro")
            local E = {}
            if not g or not m then
                return E
            end
            for C, Y in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
                local m = Y:FindFirstChild("PegarLixo")
                if isValidM(Y, m) and (not c[Y] and (not x[Y] or x[Y] <= os.clock())) then
                    table.insert(E, {parte = Y, prompt = m, posicao = Y.Position, distancia = ((Y.Position - g.Position)).Magnitude})
                end
            end
            table.sort(E, function(C, Y)
                return C.distancia < Y.distancia
            end)
            return E
        end
        local function isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(C, Y, g)
            if not C or not Y or not Y.Parent then
                return false
            end
            local m = g and math.max(2.5, g.MaxActivationDistance - 0.65) or 4.25
            return ((C.Position - Y.Position)).Magnitude <= m
        end
        local function handleQ(C, Y, g)
            local m = g and math.max(2.5, g.MaxActivationDistance - 0.8) or 4
            local E = Vector3.new(C.Position.X - Y.Position.X, 0, C.Position.Z - Y.Position.Z)
            local M = E.Magnitude > 0.1 and E.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
            local H = math.max(Y.Size.X, Y.Size.Z) * 0.5
            local A = math.clamp(H + 1.25, 1.25, math.max(1.25, m - 0.35))
            return Y.Position + M * A
        end
        local function updateBlockedConnection(C, g, m)
            local H, A, F = handleIsValid()
            if not H or not A or not F or A.Health <= 0 then
                return false
            end
            if isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(F, C, g) then
                return true
            end
            for u = 1, 4, 1 do
                if not isValidZ(m) or not C.Parent then
                    return false
                end
                H, A, F = handleIsValid()
                if not H or not A or not F or A.Health <= 0 then
                    return false
                end
                if isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(F, C, g) then
                    return true
                end
                local q = handleQ(F, C, g)
                local P = E:CreatePath({
                    AgentRadius = 2,
                    AgentHeight = 5,
                    AgentCanJump = true,
                    AgentCanClimb = true,
                    WaypointSpacing = 5,
                })
                local z = pcall(function()
                    P:ComputeAsync(F.Position, q)
                end)
                local o2 = z and (P.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success and P:GetWaypoints()) or {{Position = q, Action = Enum.PathWaypointAction.Walk}}
                local T2 = 0
                local f = false
                local handleIz = P.Blocked:Connect(function(C)
                    if C <= T2 + 1 then
                        f = true
                    end
                end)
                for E2, u2 in ipairs(o2) do
                    T2 = E2
                    if not isValidZ(m) or f then
                        break
                    end
                    H, A, F = handleIsValid()
                    if not H or not A or not F or A.Health <= 0 then
                        break
                    end
                    if isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(F, C, g) then
                        handleIz:Disconnect()
                        return true
                    end
                    if u2.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then
                        A.Jump = true
                        pcall(function()
                            A:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                        end)
                    end
                    A:MoveTo(u2.Position)
                    local q2 = F.Position
                    local P2 = os.clock()
                    local z2 = 0
                    local o3 = os.clock() + 8
                    repeat
                        Y.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        if not isValidZ(m) then
                            handleIz:Disconnect()
                            return false
                        end
                        H, A, F = handleIsValid()
                        if not H or not A or not F then
                            break
                        end
                        if isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(F, C, g) then
                            handleIz:Disconnect()
                            return true
                        end
                        local E3 = os.clock()
                        if ((F.Position - q2)).Magnitude >= 0.8 then
                            q2 = F.Position
                            P2 = E3
                        elseif E3 - P2 >= 1.25 and E3 - z2 >= 1 then
                            z2 = E3
                            A.Jump = true
                            pcall(function()
                                A:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                            end)
                            A:MoveTo(u2.Position)
                        end
                        local T3 = Vector3.new(u2.Position.X - F.Position.X, 0, u2.Position.Z - F.Position.Z)
                        if T3.Magnitude > 1 then
                            local Y2 = RaycastParams.new()
                            Y2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                            Y2.FilterDescendantsInstances = {H, C}
                            local g2 = M:Raycast(F.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), T3.Unit * 3.5, Y2)
                            if g2 and (g2.Instance and (g2.Instance.CanCollide and E3 - z2 >= 0.8)) then
                                z2 = E3
                                A.Jump = true
                                pcall(function()
                                    A:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                        if E3 - P2 >= 3 then
                            f = true
                        end
                    until f or os.clock() >= o3 or ((F.Position - u2.Position)).Magnitude <= 3
                end
                handleIz:Disconnect()
                H, A, F = handleIsValid()
                if F and isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(F, C, g) then
                    return true
                end
                updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Obstaculo detectado; recalculando rota (%d/4)...", u))
                secondaryZ(0.2, m)
            end
            return false
        end
        local function pcall2(C, Y)
            if not C or not C.Parent or not C.Enabled then
                return false
            end
            if not Y and type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                return pcall(fireproximityprompt, C, 0)
            end
            return pcall(function()
                C:InputHoldBegin()
                task.wait(math.max(0.15, C.HoldDuration + 0.1))
                C:InputHoldEnd()
            end)
        end
        local function isValid(C, Y, g)
            local m = handleG()
            for E = 1, 3, 1 do
                if not isValidZ(g) or not isValidM(C, Y) then
                    return not C.Parent or C.Transparency >= 0.95
                end
                local M2, H, A = handleIsValid()
                if not isUpdateBlockedConnectionValid(A, C, Y) then
                    return false
                end
                pcall2(Y, E > 1)
                local F = os.clock() + 2.75
                repeat
                    if not isValidZ(g) then
                        return false
                    end
                    if not C.Parent or C.Transparency >= 0.95 or handleG() > m then
                        return true
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                until os.clock() >= F
            end
            return false
        end
        local function Cz()
            local C = M:FindFirstChild("EntregaLixo")
            local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Lixeira")
            local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("EntregarLixo")
            if Y and (Y:IsA("BasePart") and (g and g:IsA("ProximityPrompt"))) then
                return Y, g
            end
            return nil, nil
        end
        local function Yz(C, Y)
            local g, m = Cz()
            if not g or not m then
                updateSendPrefRemote("Ponto EntregaLixo nao encontrado.")
                return false
            end
            updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("%d/%d coletados; indo entregar...", C, C))
            if not updateBlockedConnection(g, m, Y) then
                if not isValidZ(Y) then
                    return false
                end
                updateSendPrefRemote("Rota da entrega bloqueada; tentando novamente...")
                return false
            end
            local E = handleG()
            local M2 = isMValid()
            for g2 = 1, 3, 1 do
                if not isValidZ(Y) then
                    return false
                end
                updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Entregando lixos (%d/3)...", g2))
                pcall2(m, g2 > 1)
                local H = os.clock() + 4
                repeat
                    if not isValidZ(Y) then
                        return false
                    end
                    local g3 = handleG()
                    local m3 = isMValid()
                    if (E > 0 and g3 < E) or (M2 and (m3 and m3 > M2)) then
                        local Y2 = M2 and (m3 and math.max(0, m3 - M2)) or 0
                        updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Entrega concluida: %d lixos%s", C, Y2 > 0 and string.format(" (+$%d)", Y2) or ""))
                        return true
                    end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                until os.clock() >= H
            end
            updateSendPrefRemote("Entrega ainda nao confirmada; repetindo a rota...")
            return false
        end
        local function gz(C)
            while isValidZ(C) do
                if not sendPrefRemote(C) then
                    secondaryZ(2, C)
                else
                    local Y = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(T.autoFarmLixeiroQuantidade) or 20), 1, 20)
                    local g = 0
                    table.clear(c)
                    while isValidZ(C) and (isSendPrefRemoteCValid() == "Lixeiro" and g < Y) do
                        local m = handler()
                        if #m == 0 then
                            updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Coletados %d/%d; aguardando lixos disponiveis...", g, Y))
                            table.clear(c)
                            secondaryZ(1.25, C)
                        else
                            local E = m[1]
                            if E.distancia > 60 and g == 0 then
                                updateSendPrefRemote("Indo para a area de coleta de lixos...")
                            else
                                updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Indo ao lixo %d/%d...", g + 1, Y))
                            end
                            if updateBlockedConnection(E.parte, E.prompt, C) then
                                updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Coletando lixo %d/%d...", g + 1, Y))
                                if isValid(E.parte, E.prompt, C) then
                                    g = g + 1
                                    c[E.parte] = true
                                    updateSendPrefRemote(string.format("Coletados: %d/%d", g, Y))
                                    secondaryZ(0.45, C)
                                else
                                    if not isValidZ(C) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    x[E.parte] = os.clock() + 3
                                    updateSendPrefRemote("Coleta nao confirmou; procurando outro lixo...")
                                    secondaryZ(0.35, C)
                                end
                            else
                                if not isValidZ(C) then
                                    break
                                end
                                x[E.parte] = os.clock() + 5
                                updateSendPrefRemote("Caminho bloqueado; procurando outra rota...")
                                secondaryZ(0.4, C)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not isValidZ(C) then
                        break
                    end
                    if isSendPrefRemoteCValid() ~= "Lixeiro" then
                        updateSendPrefRemote("Emprego alterado; retomando lixeiro...")
                    elseif g >= Y then
                        while isValidZ(C) and not Yz(g, C) do
                            secondaryZ(1, C)
                        end
                        if isValidZ(C) then
                            secondaryZ(1.25, C)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local function mz(C)
            local vipCheck = isVipUser or (((getgenv and getgenv()) or _G).__VETERANO_IS_VIP)
            local isVip = false
            if type(vipCheck) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(vipCheck)
                isVip = ok and res == true
            end
            if C == true and not isVip then
                T.autoFarmLixeiroEnabled = false
                o.garbageFarmRunId = o.garbageFarmRunId + 1
                updateSendPrefRemote("Auto Farm Lixeiro: VIP necessario.")
                return
            end
            T.autoFarmLixeiroEnabled = C == true
            o.garbageFarmRunId = o.garbageFarmRunId + 1
            local Y = o.garbageFarmRunId
            if T.autoFarmLixeiroEnabled then
                updateSendPrefRemote("Verificando emprego de lixeiro...")
                task.spawn(function()
                    local C2, g2 = pcall(gz, Y)
                    if not C2 and isValidZ(Y) then
                        updateSendPrefRemote("Erro no Auto Farm: " .. tostring(g2))
                        T.autoFarmLixeiroEnabled = false
                        o.garbageFarmRunId = o.garbageFarmRunId + 1
                    end
                end)
            else
                updateSendPrefRemote("Auto Farm parado.")
                local C2, Y2, g2 = handleIsValid()
                if Y2 and g2 then
                    Y2:MoveTo(g2.Position)
                end
            end
        end
local function Ez(C)
    local Y = C.ToggleButton
    if not Y then
        return function()end
    end
    local m = 0.18
    local E = 0.045
    local M = 0.24
    local H = 0.55
    local A = 12
    local F = 48
    local u = false
    local q = nil
    local P = {}
    local z = 0
    local o = 0
    local T = 0
    local f = nil
    local function new(C)
        return Vector2.new(C.Position.X, C.Position.Y)
    end
    local function handleVisible()
        z = 0
        o = 0
        T = 0
        f = nil
    end
    local function handler(C)
        local Y = C.UserInputType
        local g = {posicao = new(C), instante = os.clock()}
        if Y == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            q = g
        elseif Y == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            P[C] = g
        end
    end
    local function updateVisible(C)
        local g = C.UserInputType
        local handler
        if g == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            handler = q
            q = nil
        elseif g == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            handler = P[C]
            P[C] = nil
        else
            return
        end
        if not handler then
            return
        end
        local updateVisible = os.clock()
        local updateInstancePropertiesPosition = updateVisible - handler.instante
        local format = new(C)
        local handleM = ((format - handler.posicao)).Magnitude
        if updateInstancePropertiesPosition > m or handleM > A then
            handleVisible()
            return
        end
        if not u or not Y.Parent then
            handleVisible()
            return
        end
        if o > 0 and updateVisible - o < E then
            return
        end
        local handleC = updateVisible - o
        local isGValid = z == 0 or handleC > M or updateVisible - T > H or not f or ((format - f)).Magnitude > F
        if isGValid then
            z = 1
            T = updateVisible
            f = format
        else
            z = z + 1
        end
        o = updateVisible
        if z >= 3 then
            Y.Visible = not Y.Visible
            handleVisible()
        end
    end
    C:_track(g.InputBegan:Connect(function(C, Y)
        local g = C.UserInputType
        if g == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or g == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if Y then
                if g == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    q = nil
                else
                    P[C] = nil
                end
                handleVisible()
                return
            end
            handler(C)
        end
    end))
    C:_track(g.InputEnded:Connect(function(C)
        updateVisible(C)
    end))
    return function()
        u = true
        handleVisible()
    end
end

    Id = "SintoniaVeterano",
    Title = "VETERANO",
    Accent = "TEAM",
    Subtitle = "VETERANO",

    ToggleButtonSize = 66,
    ToggleButtonImageInset = 3,
} )
Rf = Mz
local Hz = Ez(Mz)
Mz:SetUser( {Role = "Cliente Veterano"} )
local Az = Mz:AddSideTab( {Name = "Seu Jogador", Group = "Geral", Icon = "user"} )
local Fz = Az:AddTopTab( {Name = "Geral", Icon = "user"} )
local uz = Fz:AddColumn()
local qz = 62
local Pz = 6
local function zz(C, Y)
    return F.MergeKeybindRow(C, Y)
end

    Text = "WalkSpeed",
    Default = T.spoofWalkEnabled,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.spoofWalkEnabled = C
        if not C then
            local C = isCValid()
            if C then
                C.WalkSpeed = 16
            end
        end
    end,
} )
)

"spoofWalkSpeed",
oz:AddSlider( {
    Text = "Velocidade",
    Hint = "Com o ClientAC silenciado o slider vale cru, movido por CFrame. Sem prova de silencio o teto cai para 105 studs/s e reduz sozinho para 65 em lag ou colisao. A propriedade WalkSpeed fica sempre em 28 nos dois casos: o servidor le ela replicada e kicka ate com o boneco parado.",
    Min = 16,
    Max = 250,
    Default = T.spoofWalkSpeed,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.spoofWalkSpeed = C
    end,
} )
)
zz(
Tz,
handleIz(
"walkSpeedKeybind",
oz:AddKeybind( {

do
    local C = Az:AddTopTab( {Name = "Cosm\195\169ticos", Icon = "cross"} )
    local Y = (C:AddColumn()):AddSection("Curar")
    local g
    local m
    local E = false
    local function handleU(C)
        for Y, g in ipairs(handleC())do
            if g.chave == C then
                return g
            end
        end
    end
    local function max(C, Y, g)
        return math.max(0, math.ceil((Y - C) / g))
    end
    assert(max(0, 100, 100) == 1 and max(100, 100, 100) == 0, "c\195\161lculo de insumos inv\195\161lido")
    local function sendInGameRemotes(C, Y)
        local function updateText(C)
            if Y and (Y.Instance and Y.Instance.Parent)then
                Y.Instance.Text = C
            end
        end
        if E or b then
            updateText("Outra a\195\167\195\163o est\195\161 em andamento.")
            return
        end
        E = true
        local function handler(C)
            b = false
            E = false
            updateText(C)
        end
        local H, sendInGameRemotes = C.valor()
        if not H then
            handler(C.nome .. " indispon\195\173vel.")
            return
        end
        if H >= C.cheio then
            handler(C.nome .. " j\195\161 est\195\161 cheio.")
            return
        end
        local u = handleU(C.item)
        if not u then
            handler(C.item .. " n\195\163o foi encontrado na Shop.")
            return
        end
        local q = math.max(1, max(H, sendInGameRemotes, C.ganho))
        local P = isPValid(C.item)
        local z = math.max(0, q - P)
        local T = z * u.preco
        if isGValid() < T then
            handler(string.format("Faltam $%d para %s x%d.", T - isGValid(), C.item, z))
            return
        end
        if z > 0 then
            b = true
            updateText(string.format("Comprando %s x%d...", C.item, z))
            local Y = updateInstanceProperties(u, z)
            b = false
            if not Y then
                handler("A compra autom\195\161tica de " .. (C.item .. " foi recusada."))
                return
            end
            local E = os.clock() + 10
            repeat
                task.wait(0.15)
            until isPValid(C.item) >= q or o.destroyed or os.clock() >= E
        end
        local f = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("InGameRemotes")
        f = f and f:FindFirstChild("FomeRem")
        if not f or not f:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
            handler("Remote FomeRem n\195\163o encontrado.")
            return
        end
        for Y = 1, q, 1 do
            if isPValid(C.item) == 0 then
                handler(C.item .. " n\195\163o chegou ao invent\195\161rio.")
                return
            end
            H = C.valor()
            updateText(string.format("Usando %s %d/%d...", C.item, Y, q))
            f:FireServer(C.item)
            task.wait(6)
            local E = C.valor()
            if E <= H then
                handler("O servidor n\195\163o confirmou o uso de " .. (C.item .. "."))
                return
            end
            if E >= C.cheio then
                break
            end
        end
        H = C.valor()
        handler(string.format("%s preenchido: %d/%d.", C.nome, math.floor(H + 0.5), sendInGameRemotes))
    end
    local u = {
        cura = {
            nome = "Vida",
            item = "Tratamento",
            ganho = 100,
            cheio = 100,
            valor = function()
                local C = isCValid()
                return C and C.Health, C and C.MaxHealth or 100
            end,
        } ,
        fome = {
            nome = "Fome",
            item = "Lanche",
            ganho = 100,
            cheio = 98,
            valor = function()
                local C = H:FindFirstChild("Status")
                local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Fome")
                return Y and tonumber(Y.Value), 100
            end,
        } ,
        sede = {
            nome = "Sede",
            item = "\195\129gua",
            ganho = 100,
            cheio = 98,
            valor = function()
                local C = H:FindFirstChild("Status")
                local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("Sede")
                return Y and tonumber(Y.Value), 100
            end,
        } ,
    }
    Y:AddButton( {
        Text = "Curar",
        Callback = function()
            sendInGameRemotes(u.cura, g)
        end,
    } )
    Y:AddDivider("Insumos")
    Y:AddButton( {
        Text = "Preencher fome",
        Callback = function()
            sendInGameRemotes(u.fome, m)
        end,
    } )
    Y:AddButton( {
        Text = "Preencher Sede",
        Callback = function()
            sendInGameRemotes(u.sede, m)
        end,
    } )
end
-- VIP: free key system nao libera auto farm de lixo/pecas.
-- Builds VIP devem setar __VETERANO_LICENSE.vip = true (ou tier/plan = "vip").
local function isVipUser()
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local lic = env.__VETERANO_LICENSE
    if type(lic) == "table" then
        if lic.vip == true then
            return true
        end
        local tier = tostring(lic.tier or lic.plan or lic.role or ""):lower()
        if tier == "vip" or tier == "premium" or tier == "membro" then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end
-- expoe no env para guards internos (mz) independentemente do escopo local
do
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    env.__VETERANO_IS_VIP = isVipUser
end
local function requireVipFeature(featureName)
    if isVipUser() then
        return true
    end
    Mz:Notify( {
        Title = "Recurso VIP",
        Text = tostring(featureName)
            .. " e exclusivo para VIP. Adquira o VIP no Discord para liberar o Auto Farm.",
        Duration = 5,
    } )
    return false
end
local function Xz(C)
    Mz:Notify( {Title = "Sintonia", Text = tostring(C)} )
end

    Xz("Voc\195\170 est\195\161 num place diferente do alvo.")
end

local cz = {
    {nome = "Banco", raiz = "Banco", pasta = "Malotes", peca = "Grana", unidade = "Malas"} ,
    {nome = "Joalheria", raiz = "Joalheria", pasta = "JoiasPraRoubar", peca = "Roubador", unidade = "Joias"} ,
    {nome = "Cassino", raiz = "Cassino", pasta = "Malotes", peca = "Grana", unidade = "Malas"} ,
}
local function lz(C)
    local Y = M:FindFirstChild(C.raiz)
    local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild(C.pasta)
    if not g then
        return 0
    end
    local m = 0
    for Y, g in ipairs(g:GetChildren())do
        if g.Name == C.peca then
            local C = g:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
            if C and C.Enabled then
                m = m + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return m
end
do
    local C = ((ez:AddTopTab( {Name = "Armas", Icon = "sword"} )):AddColumn()):AddSection("Arma equipada")
    local g = C:AddLabel("Nenhuma arma equipada.")
    local m = setmetatable( {} , {__mode = "k"} )
    local E = {
        "CameraShakingEnabled",
        "Recoil",
        "AngleX",
        "AngleY",
        "FireRate",
        "ReloadTime",
        "WalkSpeedRedutionEnabled",
        "WalkSpeedRedution",
        "VisualizerEnabled",
        "BulletMaterial",
        "BulletTransparency",
        "FadeTime",
        "BulletSize",
    }
    local M = {
        {"Ciano", Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)} ,
        {"Verde", Color3.fromRGB(60, 255, 90)} ,
        {"Rosa", Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 190)} ,
        {"Roxo", Color3.fromRGB(160, 70, 255)} ,
        {"Vermelho", Color3.fromRGB(255, 45, 45)} ,
        {"Azul", Color3.fromRGB(70, 130, 255)} ,
        {"Laranja", Color3.fromRGB(255, 145, 30)} ,
        {"Branco", Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)} ,
        {"Arco-iris", nil} ,
    }
    local A = nil
    local function handleC()
        local C = H:FindFirstChild("OrpheusGunCFG")
        local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("CorBala")
        if Y and A == nil then
            A = Y.Color
        end
        return Y
    end
    local u = nil
    local function isUpdateInstancePropertiesYValid()
        local C = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
        local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("ArmaClient")
        local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("TrailBulletBlock")
        return g and g:FindFirstChild("BulletTrail")
    end
    local function updateColor(C)
        local Y = isUpdateInstancePropertiesYValid()
        if not Y or not Y:IsA("Beam")then
            return
        end
        if u == nil then
            u = {
                LightEmission = Y.LightEmission,
                LightInfluence = Y.LightInfluence,
                Brightness = Y.Brightness,
                Width0 = Y.Width0,
                Width1 = Y.Width1,
                Transparency = Y.Transparency,
            }
        end
        if C then
            if Y.LightEmission == 1 and Y.Brightness == 6 then
                return
            end
            Y.LightEmission = 1
            Y.LightInfluence = 0
            Y.Brightness = 6
            Y.Width0, Y.Width1 = 0.9, 0.9
            Y.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
        elseif Y.LightEmission ~= u.LightEmission then
            for C, g in pairs(u)do
                Y[C] = g
            end
        end
    end
    local function updateInstancePropertiesColor()
        local C = handleC()
        updateColor(T.armaTracer)
        if not T.armaTracer then
            if C and A then
                C.Color = A
            end
            return
        end
        if not C then
            return
        end
        if T.armaTracerCor == "Arco-iris" then
            C.Color = Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.25) % 1, 1, 1)
            return
        end
        for Y, g in ipairs(M)do
            if g[1] == T.armaTracerCor and g[2]then
                C.Color = g[2]
                return
            end
        end
    end
    local function updateInstanceProperties(C)
        updateInstancePropertiesColor()
        if not T.armaTracer then
            return
        end
        C.VisualizerEnabled = true
        C.BulletMaterial = Enum.Material.Neon
        C.BulletTransparency = 0
        C.FadeTime = 0.45
    end
    local function handler()
        local C = H.Character
        local Y = C and C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("Cfg")
        if not g or not g:IsA("ModuleScript")then
            return nil, Y
        end
        local m, E = pcall(require, g)
        if not m or type(E) ~= "table" or E.BaseDamage == nil then
            return nil, Y
        end
        return E, Y
    end
    local function handleY(C)
        local Y = m[C]
        if Y then
            return Y
        end
        Y = {}
        for g, m in ipairs(E)do
            Y[m] = C[m]
        end
        m[C] = Y
        return Y
    end
    local function armaAlgumModAtivo()
        return T.armaNoRecoil
            or T.armaMunicaoInfinita
            or T.armaReloadRapido
            or T.armaSemLentidao
            or T.armaCadenciaCustom
            or T.armaTracer
    end
    local function secondaryUpdateInstanceProperties(C)
        -- Nao reescreve Cfg se nenhum mod de arma estiver ligado (evita bug de tiro / cursor mao)
        if not armaAlgumModAtivo() then
            return
        end
        local Y = handleY(C)
        if T.armaNoRecoil then
            C.CameraShakingEnabled = false
            C.Recoil, C.AngleX, C.AngleY = 0, 0, 0
        else
            if Y.CameraShakingEnabled ~= nil then
                C.CameraShakingEnabled = Y.CameraShakingEnabled
            end
            if Y.Recoil ~= nil then
                C.Recoil, C.AngleX, C.AngleY = Y.Recoil, Y.AngleX, Y.AngleY
            end
        end
        if T.armaReloadRapido then
            C.ReloadTime = 0
        elseif Y.ReloadTime ~= nil then
            C.ReloadTime = Y.ReloadTime
        end
        if T.armaCadenciaCustom then
            C.FireRate = 1 / math.max(1, T.armaTirosPorSegundo)
        elseif Y.FireRate ~= nil then
            C.FireRate = Y.FireRate
        end
        if T.armaSemLentidao then
            C.WalkSpeedRedutionEnabled = false
            C.WalkSpeedRedution = 0
        else
            if Y.WalkSpeedRedutionEnabled ~= nil then
                C.WalkSpeedRedutionEnabled = Y.WalkSpeedRedutionEnabled
            end
            if Y.WalkSpeedRedution ~= nil then
                C.WalkSpeedRedution = Y.WalkSpeedRedution
            end
        end
        if T.armaTracer then
            updateInstanceProperties(C)
        else
            if Y.VisualizerEnabled ~= nil then
                C.VisualizerEnabled = Y.VisualizerEnabled
            end
            if Y.BulletMaterial ~= nil then
                C.BulletMaterial = Y.BulletMaterial
            end
            if Y.BulletTransparency ~= nil then
                C.BulletTransparency = Y.BulletTransparency
            end
            if Y.FadeTime ~= nil then
                C.FadeTime = Y.FadeTime
            end
            if Y.BulletSize ~= nil then
                C.BulletSize = Y.BulletSize
            end
            updateInstancePropertiesColor()
        end
    end
    local function restaurarArmas()
        pcall(updateColor, false)
        local C = H:FindFirstChild("OrpheusGunCFG")
        C = C and C:FindFirstChild("CorBala")
        if C and A then
            pcall(function()
                C.Color = A
            end)
        end
        for C, Y in pairs(m)do
            for Y, g in pairs(Y)do
                pcall(function()
                    C[Y] = g
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    o.restaurarArmas = restaurarArmas
    local handleM = getupvalues or (debug and debug.getupvalues)
    local handleC = setupvalue or (debug and debug.setupvalue)
    local isGValid = {tool = nil, alvos = nil, proxima = 0}
    local function updateAlvos(C, Y)
        if isGValid.tool == C and isGValid.alvos then
            return isGValid.alvos
        end
        if isGValid.tool == C and os.clock() < isGValid.proxima then
            return nil
        end
        isGValid = {tool = C, proxima = os.clock() + 3}
        if not(getgc and (handleM and handleC))then
            return nil
        end
        local g = C:FindFirstChild("OrpheusGunServer")
        local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("Mag")
        local E = m and m.Value
        if not E or E <= 0 then
            return nil
        end
        local M = {}
        for g, m in ipairs(getgc(false))do
            if type(m) == "function" then
                local g = debug.info(m, "n")
                if g == "Reload" or g == "HandleFire" or g == "UpdateGUI" then
                    local g = handleM(m)
                    local H = false
                    for g, m in pairs(g)do
                        if m == Y or m == C then
                            H = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if H then
                        for C, Y in pairs(g)do
                            if Y == E then
                                M[#M + 1] = {fn = m, idx = C}
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if#M == 0 then
            return nil
        end
        isGValid.alvos = M
        return M
    end
    local function secondaryHandler(C, Y)
        local g = updateAlvos(C, Y)
        if not g then
            return false
        end
        for C, g in ipairs(g)do
            local m = handleM(g.fn)
            if m[g.idx] ~= Y.AmmoPerMag then
                handleC(g.fn, g.idx, Y.AmmoPerMag)
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    handleIz(
    "armaNoRecoil",
    C:AddToggle( {
        Text = "Sem recuo",
        Hint = "Zera Recoil/AngleX/AngleY e o tremor de camera da arma equipada.",
        Default = T.armaNoRecoil,
        Callback = function(C)
            T.armaNoRecoil = C
        end,
    } )
)
handleIz(
"armaMunicaoInfinita",
C:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Municao infinita",
    Hint = "Mantem o pente cheio pelo upvalue da arma. Precisa de getgc/setupvalue no executor.",
    Default = T.armaMunicaoInfinita,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.armaMunicaoInfinita = C
    end,
} )
)
handleIz(
"armaReloadRapido",
C:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Recarga instantanea",
    Hint = "ReloadTime = 0. A animacao ainda toca, mas a arma ja volta a atirar.",
    Default = T.armaReloadRapido,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.armaReloadRapido = C
    end,
} )
)
handleIz(
"armaSemLentidao",
C:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Sem lentidao com arma",
    Hint = "Desliga a reducao de WalkSpeed que algumas armas aplicam.",
    Default = T.armaSemLentidao,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.armaSemLentidao = C
    end,
} )
)
handleIz(
"armaCadenciaCustom",
C:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Cadencia custom",
    Hint = "Sobrescreve o FireRate da arma. Cada tiro dispara remotes de som e efeito -- cadencia muito alta e barulho na rede.",
    Default = T.armaCadenciaCustom,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.armaCadenciaCustom = C
    end,
} )
)
handleIz(
"armaTirosPorSegundo",
C:AddSlider( {
    Text = "Tiros por segundo",
    Min = 1,
    Max = 30,
    Step = 1,
    Default = T.armaTirosPorSegundo,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.armaTirosPorSegundo = math.clamp(math.floor(C), 1, 30)
    end,
} )
)
handleIz(
"armaTracer",
C:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Tracer neon",
    Hint = "Bala em material Neon, sem transparencia e com rastro mais longo. O jogo replica o tracer pelo BulletRemote, entao os outros jogadores tambem veem a cor.",
    Default = T.armaTracer,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.armaTracer = C
    end,
} )
)
do
    local Y = {}
    for C, g in ipairs(M)do
        Y[#Y + 1] = g[1]
    end
    handleIz(
    "armaTracerCor",
    C:AddDropdown( {
        Text = "Cor do tracer",
        Options = Y,
        Default = T.armaTracerCor,
        Callback = function(C)
            T.armaTracerCor = C
        end,
    } )
)
end
local isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid = 0
handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if o.destroyed then
        return
    end
    if os.clock() - isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid < 0.15 then
        return
    end
    isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid = os.clock()
    -- Sem mods ativos: so atualiza o texto, sem require/escrita no Cfg (evita bug de tiro)
    if not armaAlgumModAtivo() then
        local char = H.Character
        local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if tool then
            g:SetText(tool.Name .. "  (mods de arma desligados)")
        else
            pcall(updateInstancePropertiesColor)
            g:SetText("Nenhuma arma equipada.")
        end
        return
    end
    local C, Y = handler()
    if not C then
        pcall(updateInstancePropertiesColor)
        g:SetText(Y and ("Sem Cfg: " .. Y.Name) or "Nenhuma arma equipada.")
        return
    end
    pcall(secondaryUpdateInstanceProperties, C)
    local m = ""
    if T.armaMunicaoInfinita and not secondaryHandler(Y, C)then
        m = " | municao: procurando..."
    end
    g:SetText(
    string.format(
    "%s  -  dano %s | recuo %s | %.2fs/tiro%s",
    Y.Name,
    tostring(C.BaseDamage),
    tostring(C.Recoil),
    C.FireRate,
    m
)
)
end))
end
local Dz = Mz:AddSideTab( {Name = "Diversos", Group = "Geral", Icon = "globe"} )
local jz = Dz:AddTopTab( {Name = "Diversos", Icon = "wrench"} )
local Wz = jz:AddColumn()
local vz = Wz:AddSection("Diversos")
local Jz = (jz:AddColumn()):AddSection("Coletar Itens")
handleIz(
"perfectLockpick",
vz:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Perfect Lock Pick",
    Default = T.perfectLockpick,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.perfectLockpick = C
    end,
} )
)
handleIz(
"bestWeaponEnabled",
vz:AddToggle( {
    Text = "Equipar melhor arma.",
    Default = T.bestWeaponEnabled,
    Callback = function(C)
        Tf(C)
    end,
} )
)
do
    local C = {}
    local g = nil
    local m
    local E = Instance.new("Frame")
    E.Name = "ColetaItensHUD"
    E.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 328, 0, 96)
    E.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(310, 300)
    E.BackgroundColor3 = Mz.Theme.Panel
    E.BorderSizePixel = 0
    E.Active = true
    E.ZIndex = 50
    E.Visible = T.coletaHudEnabled
    E.Parent = Mz.ToggleButton.Parent
    local M = Instance.new("UICorner")
    M.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    M.Parent = E
    local H = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    H.Color = Mz.Theme.Border
    H.Thickness = 1
    H.Parent = E
    local A = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    A.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    A.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 8)
    A.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 24, 0, 24)
    A.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    A.Text = "Itens no chao"
    A.TextColor3 = Mz.Theme.Text
    A.TextSize = 14
    A.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    A.Active = true
    A.ZIndex = 51
    A.Parent = E
    local F = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    F.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    F.BorderSizePixel = 0
    F.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 38)
    F.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 20, 1, - 48)
    F.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    F.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Mz.Theme.TextMuted
    F.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    F.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
    F.ZIndex = 51
    F.Parent = E
    local u = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    u.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    u.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    u.Parent = F
    local q = createDivisor(E, {Titulo = A, Divisor = 32} )
    updateInstancePropertiesPosition(E, A)
    handleIz(
    "coletaHudEnabled",
    Jz:AddToggle( {
        Text = "Mostrar HUD de itens",
        Hint = "Lista flutuante dos itens dropados com teleporte rapido.",
        Default = T.coletaHudEnabled,
        Callback = function(C)
            T.coletaHudEnabled = C
            q:SetVisible(C)
        end,
    } )
)
m = Jz:AddDropdown( {
    Text = "Item mais proximo",
    Options = {"(procurando itens...)"} ,
    Callback = function(Y)
        local m = C[Y]
        g = m and m.item or nil
    end,
} )
local function createItem()
    local Y = iF()
    table.clear(C)
    local E, M = {} , nil
    for Y, m in ipairs(Y)do
        if Y > 20 then
            break
        end
        local H = string.format("%d. %s  -  %dm", Y, m.nome, math.floor(m.distancia + 0.5))
        E[#E + 1] = H
        C[H] = m
        if m.item == g then
            M = H
        end
    end
    if#E == 0 then
        E[1] = "(nenhum item por perto)"
    end
    m:SetOptions(E, true)
    if M then
        m:Set(M)
    else
        g = nil
    end
    for C, Y in ipairs(F:GetChildren())do
        if Y:IsA("GuiObject")then
            Y:Destroy()
        end
    end
    if#Y == 0 then
        local C = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        C.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        C.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        C.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        C.Text = "Nenhum item dropado"
        C.TextColor3 = Mz.Theme.TextMuted
        C.TextSize = 13
        C.ZIndex = 52
        C.Parent = F
    else
        for C, Y in ipairs(Y)do
            if C > 20 then
                break
            end
            local g = Instance.new("Frame")
            g.Name = "Item_" .. C
            g.BackgroundColor3 = Mz.Theme.Card
            g.BorderSizePixel = 0
            g.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 4, 0, 34)
            g.LayoutOrder = C
            g.ZIndex = 52
            g.Parent = F
            local m = Instance.new("UICorner")
            m.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
            m.Parent = g
            local E = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            E.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            E.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 0)
            E.Size = UDim2.new(1, - 62, 1, 0)
            E.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            E.Text = string.format("%s  -  %dm", Y.nome, math.floor(Y.distancia + 0.5))
            E.TextColor3 = Mz.Theme.Text
            E.TextSize = 12
            E.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            E.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            E.ZIndex = 53
            E.Parent = g
            local M = Instance.new("TextButton")
            M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            M.Position = UDim2.new(1, - 5, 0.5, 0)
            M.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 24)
            M.BackgroundColor3 = Mz.Theme.Accent
            M.BorderSizePixel = 0
            M.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            M.Text = "TP"
            M.TextColor3 = Mz.Theme.Background
            M.TextSize = 12
            M.ZIndex = 53
            M.Parent = g
            local H = Instance.new("UICorner")
            H.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
            H.Parent = M
            M.Activated:Connect(function()
                if not Y.item.Parent then
                    return
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                    GF(Y)
                    pcall(createItem)
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
end
Jz:AddButton( {
    Text = "Tp",
    Callback = function()
        local Y = C[m:Get()]
        if not Y or not Y.item.Parent then
            createItem()
            Mz:Notify( {Title = "Coletar Itens", Text = "Escolha um item da lista primeiro."} )
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local C = GF(Y)
            createItem()
            Mz:Notify( {Title = "Coletar Itens", Text = C and ("Coletado: " .. Y.nome)
                or ("Nao alcancou: " .. Y.nome)} )
        end)
    end,
} )
handleIz(
"itemTeleportSpeed",
Jz:AddSlider( {
    Text = "Velocidade do TP (studs/s)",
    Hint = "Ate " .. (vF .. " studs/s; reduz automaticamente para 36 quando a condicao fica instavel."),
    Min = 20,
    Max = JF,
    Step = 5,
    Default = T.itemTeleportSpeed,
    Callback = function(C)
        T.itemTeleportSpeed = C
    end,
} )
)
local z = 0
pcall(createItem)
handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if o.destroyed then
        return
    end
    if os.clock() - z < 2 then
        return
    end
    z = os.clock()
    pcall(createItem)
end))
end
do
    local g = Wz:AddSection("Roubos")
    local m = "#50DC64"
    local E = "#DC5050"
    local M = "#60A5FA"
    local H = Color3.fromHex(m)
    local A = Color3.fromHex(E)
    local F = Color3.fromHex(M)
    local function format(C, Y)
        local g = Y > 0
        return string.format(
        '%s: <font color="%s">%s</font> - <font color="%s">%d %s</font>',
        C.nome,
        g and m or E,
        g and "Aberto" or "Fechado",
        M,
        Y,
        C.unidade
    )
end

local handleC = Instance.new("TextLabel")

handleC.BackgroundTransparency = 1
handleC.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
handleC.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
handleC.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X

handleC.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
handleC.TextSize = 13
handleC.RichText = true
handleC.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
handleC.TextColor3 = Mz.Theme.TextMuted

local function createTextLabel(C, Y, g, m, E)
    local M = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    M.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    M.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(Y, 0)
    M.Position = UDim2.new(g, 0, 0, 0)
    M.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    M.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    M.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    M.TextSize = 13
    M.TextXAlignment = m
    M.TextColor3 = E
    M.Text = ""
    M.ZIndex = 51
    M.Parent = C
    return M
end
local isCValid = {}
for C, Y in ipairs(cz)do
    local m = Instance.new("Frame")

    E.Instance.RichText = true
    E.Instance.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    isCValid[C] = {
        box = E,
        nome = createTextLabel(m, 0, 0, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
        estado = createTextLabel(m, 0.5, 0.5, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, A),
        quantidade = createTextLabel(m, 1, 1, Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, F),
    }
    isCValid[C].nome.Text = Y.nome
end
local function isCValid(C)
    return C:find("Pol") ~= nil or C:find("GCM") ~= nil or C:find("GATE") ~= nil
end
local function handleC()
    local Y = 0
    for C, g in ipairs(C:GetPlayers())do
        local m = g.Team
        if m and isCValid(m.Name)then
            Y = Y + 1
        end
    end
    return Y
end

local function updateInstanceProperties()
    for C, Y in ipairs(cz)do
        local g = lz(Y)
        local m = g > 0
        local E = isCValid[C]
        E.estado.Text = m and "Aberto" or "Fechado"
        E.estado.TextColor3 = m and H or A
        E.quantidade.Text = string.format("%d %s", g, Y.unidade)
        E.box.Instance.Text = format(Y, g)
    end

    handleC.Text = C
    if isGValidM then
        isGValidM.Instance.Text = C
    end
end

        Xz("Personagem ainda nao carregou.")

    Y.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    Y.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end

local function getInstancePropertiesMagnitude(C)
    local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
    local g = C.Character
    local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not Y or not m then
        return nil
    end
    return((m.Position - Y.Position)).Magnitude
end
local function updateInstanceProperties()
    local Y = {}
    for C, g in ipairs(C:GetPlayers())do
        if g ~= H then
            Y[g] = true
            local C = A[g]
            if not C then
                C = createPlayer(g)
                A[g] = C
            end
            local m = getInstancePropertiesMagnitude(g)
            C.Sub.Text = m and string.format("@%s  -  %dm", g.Name, math.floor(m + 0.5))
            or ("@" .. (g.Name .. '  -  <font color="#e05252">sem personagem</font>'))
            C.Root.LayoutOrder = m and math.clamp(math.floor(m), 0, 99998) or 99999
        end
    end
    for C, g in pairs(A)do
        if not Y[C]then
            g.Root:Destroy()
            A[C] = nil
        end
    end
    M.Native.Root.Visible = next(A) == nil
end
updateInstanceProperties()
local T = 0
handleReady(Y.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if o.destroyed then
        return
    end
    if os.clock() - T < 0.5 then
        return
    end
    T = os.clock()
    pcall(updateInstanceProperties)
end))
end
local az, iz
do
    local C = Dz:AddTopTab( {Name = "Trabalho e compras", Icon = "hammer"} )
    az = (C:AddColumn()):AddSection("Empregos")
    iz = C:AddColumn()
    local Y = iz:AddSection("GCM")
    local function sendM(C, Y)
        local sendM = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("Mercadinho")
        local m = sendM and sendM:FindFirstChild(C)
        if m then
            pcall(function()
                m:FireServer(Y)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function handleC(C)
        local Y = H:FindFirstChild("Inv")
        if Y and Y:FindFirstChild(C)then
            return true
        end
        for Y, sendM in ipairs( {H:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), H.Character} )do
            if sendM and sendM:FindFirstChild(C)then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    Y:AddButton( {
        Text = "Equipar farda de GCM",
        Callback = function()
            sendM("FardasRemote", "GCM")
        end,
    } )
    local E = {}
    for C, M in ipairs( {"Algemas", "GlockPolicial", "Radin", "G3Policial", "FuzilPolicial", "Tablet"} )do
        local H = Y:AddLabel("<b>" .. (M .. "</b>: Voc\195\170 j\195\161 possui esse item"))
        H.Instance.RichText = true
        local A
        A = Y:AddButton( {
            Text = "Equipar " .. M,
            Callback = function()
                sendM("EquiparArmamento", M)
                A.Instance.Visible = false
                H.Instance.Visible = true
            end,
        } )
        local function updateVisible()
            local C = handleC(M)
            H.Instance.Visible = C
            A.Instance.Visible = not C
        end
        updateVisible()
        table.insert(E, updateVisible)
    end
    local function onChildAdded()
        for C, Y in ipairs(E)do
            pcall(Y)
        end
    end
    local function isVisibleValid()
        return H.Team ~= nil and H.Team.Name == "GCM"
    end
    Y.Container.Visible = isVisibleValid()
    handleReady((H:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team")):Connect(function()
        Y.Container.Visible = isVisibleValid()
        onChildAdded()
    end))
    handleReady((Y.Container:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible")):Connect(function()
        if Y.Container.Visible ~= isVisibleValid()then
            Y.Container.Visible = isVisibleValid()
        end
    end))
    local F = H:FindFirstChild("Inv")
    if F then
        handleReady(F.ChildAdded:Connect(onChildAdded))
        handleReady(F.ChildRemoved:Connect(onChildAdded))
    end
end
local dz = az:AddLabel("Emprego atual: " .. (isSendPrefRemoteCValid() or "nenhum"))
local Gz = {}
local function tz(C, Y, g)
    return string.format(
    "%s   \194\183   Nv %d   \194\183   $%s%s",
    C.nome,
    C.level,
    format(C.salario),
    Y and "   (Atual)" or (g and "   (Nv insuf.)" or "")
)
end
local function Kz()
    local C, Y = isSendPrefRemoteCValid(), isSendPrefRemoteInstanceValid()
    for g, m in ipairs(Gz)do
        local E = C == m.emprego.nome
        m.btn.Label.Text = tz(m.emprego, E, Y < m.emprego.level)
        m.btn.Label.TextColor3 = E and Mz.Theme.Text or Mz.Theme.TextMuted
    end
end
local CF = false
local function YF(C)
    if CF then
        return
    end
    if isSendPrefRemoteCValid() == C.nome then
        dz.Instance.Text = "Voc\195\170 j\195\161 trabalha como " .. C.nome
        return
    end
    local Y = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("Mercadinho")
    local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("PrefRemote")
    if not g or not g:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
        dz.Instance.Text = "PrefRemote n\195\163o encontrado"
        return
    end
    CF = true
    dz.Instance.Text = "Trocando para " .. (C.nome .. "...")
    task.spawn(function()
        g:FireServer(C.nome, C.level, true)
        local Y = os.clock() + P
        repeat
            task.wait(0.2)
        until o.destroyed or isSendPrefRemoteCValid() == C.nome or os.clock() >= Y
        if o.destroyed then
            return
        end
        if isSendPrefRemoteCValid() == C.nome then
            dz.Instance.Text = "Agora voc\195\170 \195\169 " .. C.nome
        else
            dz.Instance.Text = string.format(
            "N\195\163o rolou \226\128\148 %s pede n\195\173vel %d, voc\195\170 tem %d",
            C.nome,
            C.level,
            isSendPrefRemoteInstanceValid()
        )
    end
    CF = false
    Kz()
end)
end
for C, Y in ipairs(handleM())do
    local g = az:AddButton( {
        Text = tz(Y, false, false),
        Callback = function()
            YF(Y)
        end,
    } )
    table.insert(Gz, {emprego = Y, btn = g} )
end
