local function HF()
    local C = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    for C, Y in ipairs(C and C:GetChildren() or {} )do
        local g = Y.Name == "TextBoxUI" and Y:FindFirstChild("Box", true)
        if g and g.PlaceholderText == "Quantidade" then
            return Y, g
        end
    end
end
updateInstanceProperties = function(C, Y)
    local g, m = isGValid(), isPValid(C.chave)
    Y = Y and math.max(1, math.floor(Y)) or nil
    if g < C.preco * (Y or 1)then
        return false, "saldo"
    end
    local E = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("Mercadinho")
    local M = C.hpItem and "HPRemote" or "GlobalComprarItem"
    local F = E and E:FindFirstChild(M)
    if not F or not F:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
        return false, M
    end
    if Y and not C.hpItem then
        local C = HF()
        if C then
            local Y = C:FindFirstChild("BRecusar", true)
            if Y and A.firesignal then
                A.firesignal(Y.MouseButton1Click)
            else
                local Y = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("InGameRemotes")
                Y = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("MandaTextbox")
                if Y then
                    Y:FireServer("Response", false)
                end
                C:Destroy()
            end
            task.wait(0.35)
        end
    end
    for Y = 1, (C.hpItem and (Y or 1) or 1), 1 do
        F:FireServer(C.chave)
        if C.hpItem then
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end
    if Y and not C.hpItem then
        local C, g
        local m = os.clock() + 3
        repeat
            task.wait(0.05)
            C, g = HF()
        until C or o.destroyed or os.clock() >= m
        if not C then
            return false, "quantidade"
        end
        local E = C:FindFirstChild("Aceitar", true)
        g.Text = tostring(Y)
        if E and A.getconnections then
            local C = os.clock() + 2
            repeat
                task.wait(0.05)
            until#A.getconnections(E.MouseButton1Click) > 0 or os.clock() >= C
        else
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        if E and A.firesignal then
            pcall(A.firesignal, E.MouseButton1Click)
            task.wait(0.4)
        end
        if C.Parent then
            local Y = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("InGameRemotes")
            Y = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("MandaTextbox")
            if not Y then
                return false, "quantidade"
            end
            Y:FireServer("Response", g.Text)
            C:Destroy()
        end
    end
    local function handler()
        local C = H:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        local Y = C and C:FindFirstChild("MsgsUI")
        local g = Y and Y:FindFirstChild("BGFrame", true)
        local m = g and g:FindFirstChild("Aceitar", true)
        local E = g and g:FindFirstChild("Not", true)
        if m and (A.firesignal and (E and ((tostring(E.Text)):lower()):find("comprar")))then
            pcall(A.firesignal, m.MouseButton1Click)
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    local q = false
    local P = os.clock() + 30
    repeat
        if not q and handler()then
            q = true
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
    until isGValid() < g or isPValid(C.chave) > m or o.destroyed or os.clock() >= P
    if o.destroyed then
        return false, "encerrado"
    end
    if isGValid() < g or isPValid(C.chave) > m then
        return true, g - isGValid()
    end
    return false, "recusado"
end
local function AF(C)
    if b then
        return
    end
    if not C.hpItem and isGValidM(C.chave)then
        mF.Instance.Text = C.chave .. " j\195\161 est\195\161 no invent\195\161rio"
        return
    end
    local Y = isGValid()
    if Y < C.preco then
        mF.Instance.Text = string.format("Faltam $%d para %s", C.preco - Y, C.chave)
        return
    end
    local g = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("Mercadinho")
    local m = C.hpItem and "HPRemote" or "GlobalComprarItem"
    local E = g and g:FindFirstChild(m)
    if not E or not E:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
        mF.Instance.Text = m .. " n\195\163o encontrado"
        return
    end
    b = true
    mF.Instance.Text = "Comprando " .. (C.chave .. "...")
    task.spawn(function()
        local g, m = updateInstanceProperties(C)
        if o.destroyed then
            return
        end
        b = false
        if g then
            mF.Instance.Text = m > 0 and string.format("%s comprado por $%d", C.chave, m)
            or C.chave .. " chegou no invent\195\161rio"
        else
            mF.Instance.Text = m == "saldo" and string.format("Faltam $%d para %s", C.preco - Y, C.chave)
            or "Servidor recusou: " .. C.chave
        end
        MF(true)
    end)
end

        Callback = function()

        end,
    } )
    table.insert(EF, {produto = Y, btn = g} )
end

do
    local C = false
    local Y
    local function updateText(C)
        if Y then
            Y.Instance.Text = C
        end
    end
    local function sendSayMessageRequest(C)
        local Y = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if Y.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
            local g = Y:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
            local updateText = g and (g:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral") or g:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextChannel"))
            if not updateText then
                return false
            end
            return pcall(function()
                updateText:SendAsync(C)
            end)
        end
        local g = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")):FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
        local updateText = g and g:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
        if not updateText or not updateText:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
            return false
        end
        return pcall(function()
            updateText:FireServer(C, "All")
        end)
    end
    local function handler()
        for C, Y in ipairs(handleC())do
            if Y.chave == "MiliTec" and not Y.hpItem then
                return Y
            end
        end
    end
    local function callback()
        if C or b then
            updateText("A Shop est\195\161 ocupada.")
            return
        end
        C = true
        local function secondaryHandler(secondaryHandler)
            b = false
            C = false
            updateText(secondaryHandler)
            MF(true)
        end
        local g = rz()
        if not g then
            secondaryHandler("Entre e sente no seu carro primeiro.")
            return
        end
        if isPValid("MiliTec") == 0 then
            local C = handler()
            if not C then
                secondaryHandler("MiliTec n\195\163o encontrado na Shop.")
                return
            end
            if isGValid() < C.preco then
                secondaryHandler(string.format("Faltam $%d para comprar a MiliTec.", C.preco - isGValid()))
                return
            end
            b = true
            updateText("Comprando MiliTec...")
            local g = updateInstanceProperties(C, 1)
            b = false
            if not g then
                secondaryHandler("A compra autom\195\161tica da MiliTec foi recusada.")
                return
            end
        end
        updateText("Enviando /consertar...")
        if not sendSayMessageRequest("/consertar")then
            secondaryHandler("N\195\163o consegui enviar o comando no chat.")
            return
        end
        secondaryHandler("Comando /consertar enviado. O jogo vai reparar o carro.")
    end
    uF:AddButton( {Text = "Reparar carro", Callback = callback} )
    do
        local C = Instance.new("TextButton")
        C.Name = "FloatingRepair"
        C.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        C.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, 84)
        C.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(132, 40)
        C.BackgroundColor3 = Mz.Theme.Panel
        C.BorderSizePixel = 0
        C.AutoButtonColor = false
        C.Text = "Reparar carro"
        C.TextColor3 = Mz.Theme.Text
        C.TextSize = 14
        C.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        C.ZIndex = 50
        C.Visible = T.floatingRepairEnabled
        C.Parent = Mz.ToggleButton.Parent
        local Y = Instance.new("UICorner")
        Y.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        Y.Parent = C
        local updateText = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        updateText.Color = Mz.Theme.Border
        updateText.Thickness = 1
        updateText.Parent = C
        local sendSayMessageRequest =
        {moved = false, kind = nil, start = Vector2.new(), origin = Vector2.new()}
        handleReady(C.InputBegan:Connect(function(Y)
            local g = Y.UserInputType
            if g ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and g ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                return
            end
            sendSayMessageRequest.kind, sendSayMessageRequest.moved = g, false
            sendSayMessageRequest.start = Vector2.new(Y.Position.X, Y.Position.Y)
            sendSayMessageRequest.origin = C.AbsolutePosition
        end))
        handleReady(g.InputChanged:Connect(function(Y)
            if not sendSayMessageRequest.kind then
                return
            end
            local g = Y.UserInputType
            if
            not(
            (
            sendSayMessageRequest.kind == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and g == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        )
        or (
        sendSayMessageRequest.kind == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        and g == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    )
)
then
    return
end
local updateText = Vector2.new(Y.Position.X, Y.Position.Y) - sendSayMessageRequest.start
if updateText.Magnitude >= 4 then
    sendSayMessageRequest.moved = true
end
local handler = M.CurrentCamera
local callback = handler and handler.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
local F = math.clamp(
sendSayMessageRequest.origin.X + updateText.X,
8,
math.max(8, (callback.X - C.AbsoluteSize.X) - 8)
)
local u = math.clamp(
sendSayMessageRequest.origin.Y + updateText.Y,
8,
math.max(8, (callback.Y - C.AbsoluteSize.Y) - 8)
)
C.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(F, u + C.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2)
end))
handleReady(g.InputEnded:Connect(function(C)
    if sendSayMessageRequest.kind and C.UserInputType == sendSayMessageRequest.kind then
        sendSayMessageRequest.kind = nil
    end
end))
handleReady(C.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not sendSayMessageRequest.moved then
        callback()
    end
end))

end))

        handler.kind = nil

local function handler()
    if isPValid("Micha") > 0 or b then
        return
    end
    local C
    for Y, g in ipairs(handleC())do
        if g.chave == "Micha" and not g.hpItem then
            C = g
            break
        end
    end
    if not C or isGValid() < C.preco then
        return
    end
    b = true
    pcall(updateInstanceProperties, C, 1)
    b = false
end

F.Config.Folder = "VeteranoTeam/sintonia"
local fF = oF:AddSection("Configs")
if not F.Config.isSupported()then
    fF:AddLabel(
    "Seu executor nao expoe filesystem (writefile/readfile), entao salvar configs nao esta disponivel."
)
else
    local function isYmValid(isYmValid)
        if typeof(isYmValid) == "EnumItem" then
            return(string.gsub(tostring(isYmValid), "^Enum%.%a+%.", ""))
        end
        return isYmValid and tostring(isYmValid) or nil
    end
    local function handler(isYmValid)
        if typeof(isYmValid) == "EnumItem" then
            return isYmValid
        end
        if type(isYmValid) ~= "string" then
            return nil
        end
        local handler, g = pcall(function()
            return Enum.KeyCode[isYmValid]
        end)
        if handler and g then
            return g
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function handleY()
        local handler = {}
        for handleY, m in pairs(f)do
            local E, M = pcall(function()
                return m:Get()
            end)
            if E then
                if typeof(M) == "EnumItem" then
                    M = isYmValid(M)
                end
                handler[handleY] = M
            end
        end
        return handler
    end
    local function secondaryHandler(isYmValid)
        if type(isYmValid) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        for handleY, secondaryHandler in pairs(f)do
            local E = isYmValid[handleY]
            if E ~= nil then
                if type(E) == "string" and typeof(secondaryHandler:Get()) == "EnumItem" then
                    E = handler(E)
                end
                if E ~= nil then
                    pcall(function()
                        secondaryHandler:Set(E)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local E = fF:AddTextbox( {Text = "Nome", Placeholder = "nome da config..."} )
    local M = fF:AddDropdown( {Text = "Configs salvas", Options = F.Config.list()} )
    local function alternateHandler(isYmValid)
        M:SetOptions(F.Config.list(), true)
        if isYmValid then
            M:Set(isYmValid)
        end
    end
    fF:AddButton( {
        Text = "Criar nova config",
        Callback = function()
            local isYmValid = F.Config._sanitize(E:Get())
            local handler, secondaryHandler = F.Config.save(isYmValid, handleY())
            if handler then
                alternateHandler(isYmValid)
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Config salva", Text = "'" .. (isYmValid .. "' criada."), Duration = 3} )
            else
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Erro ao salvar", Text = tostring(secondaryHandler or "falha"), Duration = 4} )
            end
        end,
    } )
    fF:AddButton( {
        Text = "Carregar config",
        Callback = function()
            local isYmValid = M:Get()
            if not isYmValid then
                Mz:Notify( {
                    Title = "Selecione uma config",
                    Text = "Escolha no dropdown antes de carregar.",
                    Duration = 3,
                } )
                return
            end
            local handler, handleY = F.Config.load(isYmValid)
            if handler then
                secondaryHandler(handler)
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Config carregada", Text = "'" .. (isYmValid .. "' aplicada."), Duration = 3} )
            else
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Erro ao carregar", Text = tostring(handleY or "falha"), Duration = 4} )
            end
        end,
    } )
    fF:AddButton( {
        Text = "Editar config selecionada",
        Callback = function()
            local isYmValid = M:Get()
            if not isYmValid then
                Mz:Notify( {
                    Title = "Selecione uma config",
                    Text = "Escolha a config a editar no dropdown.",
                    Duration = 3,
                } )
                return
            end
            local handler, secondaryHandler = F.Config.save(isYmValid, handleY())
            if handler then
                Mz:Notify( {
                    Title = "Config atualizada",
                    Text = "'" .. (isYmValid .. "' sobrescrita com os valores atuais."),
                    Duration = 3,
                } )
            else
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Erro ao editar", Text = tostring(secondaryHandler or "falha"), Duration = 4} )
            end
        end,
    } )
    fF:AddButton( {
        Text = "Excluir config selecionada",
        Callback = function()
            local isYmValid = M:Get()
            if not isYmValid then
                Mz:Notify( {
                    Title = "Selecione uma config",
                    Text = "Escolha a config a excluir no dropdown.",
                    Duration = 3,
                } )
                return
            end
            local handler, handleY = F.Config.delete(isYmValid)
            if handler then
                alternateHandler()
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Config excluida", Text = "'" .. (isYmValid .. "' apagada."), Duration = 3} )
            else
                Mz:Notify( {Title = "Erro ao excluir", Text = tostring(handleY or "falha"), Duration = 4} )
            end
        end,
    } )
end
Mz:SetVisible(false)
if Mz.ToggleButton then
    Mz.ToggleButton.Visible = false
end

    pcall(jz, false)
    T.silentAimEnabled = false
    T.aimbotEnabled = false
    T.silentAimFovVisible = false
    T.armaNoRecoil = false
    T.armaMunicaoInfinita = false
    T.armaReloadRapido = false
    T.armaSemLentidao = false
    T.armaCadenciaCustom = false
    T.armaTracer = false
    if o.restaurarArmas then
        pcall(o.restaurarArmas)
    end
    T.bestWeaponEnabled = false
    o.bestWeaponRunId = o.bestWeaponRunId + 1
    T.autoFarmLixeiroEnabled = false
    o.garbageFarmRunId = o.garbageFarmRunId + 1
    T.durabilidadeHudEnabled = false
    if o.destruirDurabilidade then
        pcall(o.destruirDurabilidade)
    end
    T.bestCarsEspEnabled = false
    if o.destruirBestCarsEsp then
        pcall(o.destruirBestCarsEsp)
    end
    T.vFlyEnabled = false
    T.noclipCarEnabled = false
    if o.restaurarNoclipCar then
        pcall(o.restaurarNoclipCar)
    end
    T.boostFactor = 1
    table.clear(o.relationships)
    table.clear(o.relationshipConnections)
    T.rolarEnabled = false
    pcall(Zz)
    T.noclipEnabled = false
    pcall(Nz)
    T.flyEnabled = false
    pcall(xz)
    pcall(kF)
    pcall(function()
        if nF then
            nF:Remove()
        end
    end)
    pcall(pz)
    pcall(function()
        local C = isCValid()
        if C then
            Tz(C, false)
            C:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
        end
        local Y = oz()
        if Y then
            Y.Disabled = false
        end
    end)
    for C, Y in ipairs(o.connections)do
        pcall(function()
            Y:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    o.connections = {}
    for C, Y in pairs(T.bundles)do
        qF(Y)
        T.bundles[C] = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        local C = isCValid()
        if C then
            local Y = isUpdatePlayerGetMagnitudeCValid()
            if Y then
                C:MoveTo(Y.Position)
            end
            C.WalkSpeed = 16
            C.JumpPower = 50
        end
    end)
end
return true
